2026-10-18THE ASSESSOR WHO KEPT THE DAM STANDINGOCENJEVALEC, KI JE OHRANIL JEZ

For eleven years one honest-looking man has signed a safety certificate he knows is false, and the only people who can fix the dam are the ones his lie was meant to protect.Enajst let je na videz pošten mož podpisoval varnostno potrdilo, za katero je vedel, da je lažno, jez pa lahko popravijo le ljudje, ki jih je njegova laž skušala zaščititi.

PublishedObjavljeno 2026-10-18T19:20:08.283Z · Permanent linkTrajna povezava

01 · A Number That Holds Still

Harrow Vale, water-board office · Monday, 16:10 · fifty-six days before the storm season

The regional authority had sent her to audit the valley's flood model, and it was a good model. Illuminara had spent two days inside it the way a locksmith spends an afternoon inside a lock. Rainfall had a spread. Soil had a spread. Only one input sat perfectly still, and it sat above six thousand roofs.

IlluminaraLeft buttress, block thirty-one. Eleven years of seam readings, one a month. Mr Prycé, water does not sit that quietly in a hillside. Every other gauge shivers when it rains on the ridge. This one is deaf.

Dovan Prycé set a cup of tea at her elbow before he answered. He was sixty-one, neat and unhurried, a man who had spent his life being the quietest person in rooms where decisions were made.

Dovan PrycéThe gauge is old and the seam is dry. I'd call that a compliment to the grouting. Harrow is a dull dam, Ms Illuminara. Most of my work is writing satisfactory in a careful hand.

She had been a court interpreter once, and she still listened for sentences that had been rehearsed. This one had been sanded. Dull, he said, gently, like a man closing a door he had oiled himself.

IlluminaraI'm not accusing anyone. I'm asking what the dam would have to look like for that series to be the honest reading. I would like to go and see. Tonight, if you'll lend me the gallery key.

The pause was shorter than half a second. It was not fear. It was the sound of a man deciding which of two truths to offer first.

Dovan PrycéThe light is best at dusk. Take the blue key. I'll follow when I've locked up.

He did not look at the shelf behind his desk, where a bicycle wheel with one spoke missing hung from a nail. She noticed that he did not look.

02 · The Pencil on the Wall

Harrow Dam, left-buttress gallery, block 31 · the same evening, 19:40

Concrete is a record of weight. Illuminara laid her palm flat on the gallery wall and read it as a set of roads along which force travelled, and a few places where the roads disagreed. At block thirty-one one road forked that should not have forked. Load was slipping sideways through a seam no thicker than a pencil line, and under her hand the seam was not still.

Interpretation, she told herself. Not evidence. Test it.

The official crack gauge, a brass tell-tale glued across the seam, had been painted over, scale and all, in fresh municipal green. Beside it, at knee height, a ruler was pinned to the wall with a bicycle spoke bent into a pointer across the crack. Down the plaster ran a column of pencil ticks, each with a date in a small, careful hand. She read them from the bottom. For nine years they crept four-tenths of a millimetre a year. Then the last two winters, a millimetre and a half each. Then, at the top, three ticks in nine days.

Dovan PrycéYou found my spoke.

IlluminaraI found your pencil. It's the most honest record in this valley.

He sat down on a cable drum as though his legs had agreed to something before he had.

Dovan PrycéFour-tenths I could live beside. Every morning now I come down before the office opens to see whether the pointer has moved, and every morning I decide not to write what it says.

IlluminaraHow long, in your judgement?

Dovan PrycéA seam like this doesn't snap. It stops giving, and on the day it stops it has nothing left to give. Another wet autumn with the melt on top? I wouldn't bet the valley on the second. I wouldn't bet it on the first.

IlluminaraThen why the paint?

He laughed once, at himself.

Dovan PrycéEleven years ago I was the junior assessor. Old Ketteridge wrote a margin note on this block: requires attention. Aldermere read it as unsafe, because their covenant has a trigger and that was the word that pulled it. They called the loan in thirty days. The Works shut. Two thousand two hundred people left in one winter and I helped load the vans. The next certificate said satisfactory, with monitoring in my hand, and I have been monitoring ever since. Your honesty is paid for out of other people's houses. Show me a repair nobody has to lose a job for, or admit you will do what I did, later, with more witnesses.

It was a good objection, and Illuminara did not hurry past it. Behind him, in the dark of the gallery, water ticked in a drain, loud as a clock.

IlluminaraI don't have the repair. I have two facts, and neither is a verdict yet: the seam is moving, and for eleven years you were the only person who knew. So here is what I can promise, and where it stops. Tonight I report nothing. Nothing with your name on it leaves this gallery, and nothing about the dam goes to the lender or the town, until there is a plan with room for you in it. But I won't leave the seam to your pencil, or to mine. Sunniva Achterberg lays a fibre-optic cable across block thirty-one at first light, so that we argue with a measurement and not with me. I read force, Mr Prycé, and I can be wrong. And teach me the valley's money first.

Dovan PrycéThen give the promise an end, and I'll give you the number. Water on the downstream face of that block. Or fifteen hundredths of a millimetre across the crack in six hours. That is what I have been afraid of since the second winter. A seam that does that is not creeping any more.

IlluminaraThen that is the line, and you drew it, not I. Until the cable is live, the spoke is read on the hour, by you and by me in turn, each figure written under the last, and the same line holds for the spoke as for the cable. If the cable crosses it, or I see water on that face, the week is over that hour. I ring the regional emergency line, the evacuation plan opens, and you hear it from me first. What you tell the board about eleven years is yours to choose. Whether people are warned if the seam goes is not. Inside that line I am asking you for a week.

He took the bent spoke between two fingers and for a while looked only at his own handwriting.

Dovan PrycéA week. Come to the house. I've a great deal of paper.

03 · The Word and the Date

Prycé's kitchen, Harrow Vale · the same night, 01:20

The loan agreement lay open on the kitchen table, a stack as thick as a brick, eleven years of his pencil in the margins. He taught her the valley's money the way a man teaches a language he has had to speak alone. At clause fourteen point three his voice went flat.

Dovan PrycéDam Condition Event. If the licensing assessor certifies the dam Class D, unsafe, the lender may call the facility within thirty days. That is the whole trap. It doesn't matter what the repair costs. It matters that I sign the word.

A trap, Illuminara thought, is built to catch one particular animal. She followed the sentence to its end, where a subordinate clause lay in its own shade.

IlluminaraUnless, at the date of such certificate, a Remedial Programme approved by the Lender's Engineer is then in progress. Have you read that?

Dovan PrycéMany times. To have a programme approved, someone must disclose the defect to the lender. It's a locked room, and the key is the same word.

IlluminaraWho discloses? Not you. You are the assessor; you certify. The Borrower discloses. Read me fourteen point five.

He turned to the riders at the back and stopped at one he had not opened in years.

Dovan PrycéThe Borrower may give written notice of a suspected condition. The Credit Officer may grant a standstill of up to ninety days for the Lender's Engineer to review a remedial programme. No such notice is a certificate or an admission as to classification, and no default is waived.

He looked at the date on the rider. It was the winter after the Works shut.

Dovan PrycéAldermere wrote that door themselves, after the vans. It is addressed to the Borrower, and I have never been the Borrower, so I filed it under administrative.

IlluminaraThen the word was never the key, and it was never the lock. It's the date. Fourteen point three asks what is happening on the day you certify, not what was said before it. So the order matters more than the courage. Voss gives notice under fourteen point five. Halloran grants the standstill. Aldermere's engineer reads a cable and approves a programme. Only then do you certify, and not an hour before. Certify first, and the proviso never attaches.

Dovan PrycéAnd until then the seam keeps moving.

He turned pages. The Works Director, for inspection, maintenance or safety, could lower the reservoir by up to twelve metres without notice to anyone.

IlluminaraThrust on a seam goes with the square of the depth. Harrow holds thirty-eight metres at full pool. Take off eleven and the push on block thirty-one is about half. A drawdown is not a programme, and the lender would be right to refuse to call it one. It is what keeps the seam alive for the days the paper takes.

Dovan PrycéEleven metres is thirty-three million cubic metres. The Works would be pouring its own stock down the river for nothing.

IlluminaraNot for nothing, if somebody downstream wants it. Who is short of water in the autumn?

He opened his mouth to say no one, and closed it. The terraces: four thousand hectares of orchard and winter wheat, and the off-stream reservoirs that Corrin Aske's irrigation union filled every March at pumping prices.

IlluminaraThen let him bite a price off it. The draw must be slow, no more than a quarter of a metre a day, or the wet shoulders of the valley slump into the pool. That is forty-four days. You have fifty-six. Start on Thursday, or the arithmetic runs out before the water does.

Prycé sat quietly for some time. Outside, somebody's dog barked at nothing.

Dovan PrycéYou've left out the strongest thing against you, and I noticed. Even if the lender permits the programme, I have certified satisfactory eleven times. That is misrepresentation, a default in its own right whatever the dam is doing. The proviso protects a man who is late. It doesn't protect a liar.

IlluminaraNo, it doesn't. That one is yours to answer, and I can't answer it for you. But you are the one who has to stand.

Dovan PrycéAnd the people below?

IlluminaraThey hear what you know on the day the lender does, and they hear the rest from you before anyone discusses your name.

Every hour until the grey came up, one of them went down the lane to the gallery, read the bent spoke against its ruler and wrote the figure under the last one. It did not move.

04 · What the Water Is Worth

Tarn Works, director's office · Wednesday, 07:30

Marguerite Voss listened to all of it standing, with her coat still on, which in Harrow Vale was a form of courtesy. When Prycé had finished she did not look at Illuminara. She looked at him.

Marguerite VossEleven years I have signed for a dam I could not see inside, on the word of a man I trusted with my children's school.

Dovan PrycéYes.

Marguerite VossI'd have done the same in your chair, which is why I can throw this chair at you. You will say it to the board yourself, Dovan, in your own words, and you won't hide behind the crack. Those are my terms. I'll sign the maintenance order for the low gates myself: dawn, Thursday. And I'll sign the notice to Aldermere under fourteen point five, as Borrower: a suspected condition, independently measured, and the assessor to give his own account in person on Monday. Your name isn't on it. It's true, and the rest is yours to say.

Dovan PrycéIt's a true sentence. I'll say the rest.

Sunniva Achterberg had driven through the night with a drum of fibre-optic cable in the back of a hatchback and spent the day lying on a gallery floor. She put a printout on the desk. It sat inside the line Prycé had drawn in the gallery, and not by much.

Sunniva AchterbergPoint three one of a millimetre across block thirty-one in eighteen hours. That is faster than your pencil, Mr Prycé. Yesterday I'd have said dull. Today I'll say it's the tail of something. Take eleven metres off and my model gives the margin back. I'll sign safer, measured. I won't sign safe. I've never seen that word survive a spring.

Marguerite VossThe water pays a third of stage one: twenty-two anchors in blocks twenty-nine to thirty-three. Halloran can release a first tranche on his own signature, up to a line, and I've priced stage one to sit under it. The notice reaches Aldermere at nine on Thursday, and at the same hour a bulletin goes on every door in the valley: the seam, the pool coming down, a public meeting on Monday. The lender won't hear one word the valley hasn't.

Corrin Aske arrived at noon in his boots and listened with a silence that grew more dangerous as it went.

Corrin AskeYou want me to buy your fear.

Marguerite VossI want you to buy thirty-three million cubic metres for the terraces at nine cents against the nineteen it costs you to pump.

Corrin AskeAt nine. In my reservoirs by Christmas, not March, with a written guarantee you can't take back. And a seat on the dam safety panel. My people sleep below that wall too. If you want me to sleep, I read the cable myself. I don't hear about it.

Marguerite VossDone. The seat, and the guarantee.

Corrin AskeAnd him?

Dovan PrycéI'll answer for the certificates. I'm not asking you to like it.

Corrin AskeGood. I don't.

05 · Residents First

Harrow town hall, Aldermere's credit committee on the screen · the following Monday, 15:00

Teodor Halloran appeared on the screen with a printed agenda in his hand, a careful man with a careful tie. He had granted the standstill on Thursday. Imre Dalca, Aldermere's engineer, had spent Friday reading Achterberg's cable himself and had approved stage one of the programme at nine that morning. The hall was full, and Illuminara said nothing.

Teodor HalloranThis is a credit review. Item one is covenant compliance.

Anneke RoweItem one is me. I nurse at the clinic nine hundred metres below the toe of that dam, and my sons' school is on the flat beside it. Before anyone talks about a loan, tell me whether my children can sleep.

Teodor HalloranMs Rowe, with respect, this is a lender's—

Anneke RoweWith respect, I'll hear it first or I'll hear nothing.

Dovan Prycé stood. His notes shook once and then did not. He had practised, Illuminara thought, for eleven years, in a mirror she had never seen.

Dovan PrycéFor eleven years I certified Harrow Dam satisfactory, with monitoring. It was not. Block thirty-one has a seam moving faster each winter, and I hid the rate. Since Thursday the pool has been lowered one point one metres, and an independent cable shows the movement has slowed by a fifth. Mr Dalca has approved stage one of a remedial programme, and so, at three o'clock, as licensing assessor, I classify Harrow Dam Class D at full pool. Ms Rowe, I will not tell you the school is safe. Ilse Bram's flood unit reran the breach map on Friday. At full pool, a breach of block thirty-one puts four metres of water on your flat in eleven minutes. At the pool we will hold by storm season, about half a metre in forty. That is a different flood. It is not no flood. If the cable shows more than fifteen hundredths of a millimetre in six hours, or the pool passes thirty-one metres, the sirens sound and the flat is cleared, whatever the sky is doing. I am the reason you had to ask.

Anneke Rowe said nothing for a while.

Anneke RoweForty minutes is a wish if it's one road and six hundred children. The flat clears in twenty-five or I want a second road. And the clinic goes on the radio net the same hour as the gallery.

Dovan PrycéThe radio I can put in the plan myself. The stopwatch at Wednesday's siren test is yours.

She sat down, which in that room was a verdict of its own.

Anneke RoweWednesday, and I'll stand under the siren myself. Go on. Say the rest to him.

Teodor HalloranMr Prycé, you have just told me you falsified eleven certificates, and my committee's first instinct will be to call the loan. My standstill covers the condition. It waives nothing. A committee will ask whether a programme was in progress on the date of your certificate, and whether approved means funded. Mr Dalca approved stage one at nine, I released the first tranche at ten on my own signature, and you certified at three. I have minuted that order. Had you certified on Thursday, I would be telling you the proviso never attached.

Dovan PrycéI know. I was made to wait.

Halloran looked, for the first time, at the woman sitting beside the assessor.

Teodor HalloranAnd you are?

IlluminaraSomeone who was sent to check a flood model. One number, if I may: if Aldermere called the facility today, on a dam with a moving seam, a valley to evacuate and a Works shut down, what does the collateral fetch?

Teodor HalloranA very small number.

He looked at the figure on his own screen for longer than a man looks who has already decided.

Teodor HalloranI can't waive a misrepresentation; that belongs to committee. I can refer it with the standstill running and a recommendation that includes that number. I'll record the facility as under active remediation, not unsafe. Mr Prycé's certificates stay reserved, the committee will rule within ninety days, and I would find a lawyer.

Dovan PrycéI have one. I should have taken her advice some years ago.

06 · Holding

Harrow Dam, crest · forty-nine days later, 02:10

The first storm of the season came off the ridge like a door thrown open. Four hundred millimetres in two days. The pool rose two point four metres in the night, and it had eleven metres of empty valley to rise into. Twenty-two anchors, drilled into blocks twenty-nine to thirty-three, held the seam in a grip it had not known since the dam was young.

Before the rain, Achterberg had written three lines on a card and taped it above the gallery radio. At a rise of two point four metres the seam would open by no more than six hundredths of a millimetre. Past the line Prycé had drawn in the gallery, the sirens would sound. At the top of every hour Aske's night man read the cable aloud over the radio. By midnight, one hundredth. By one, two. At two, three.

Dovan PrycéForty-four days of drawdown. The Works carrying new debt for ten years. Eleven families gone since September, who'd have gone anyway, they tell me, though I have never found that comfort. I'm out on the thirty-first. The committee will fine me and take my licence. My lawyer says I'll keep my house.

IlluminaraAnd Ms Rowe?

Dovan PrycéThe boys went to school on the flat on Monday. She timed the siren drill on Wednesday. Twenty-three minutes. She says she still wakes at three. I told her I do as well, and she said good.

Rain hammered the crest. Below them the valley lay dark and unevacuated, six thousand people asleep behind a wall that had, for the first time in eleven years, been described truthfully.

At three the pulse crested, and Achterberg's voice came over the radio from the gallery.

Sunniva Achterberg (radio)Crest. Four hundredths. Inside the band. The anchor cells rose with the water, so the anchors are carrying it. If the lowered pool had done this alone, they'd have read nothing. Safer, measured. I'll write safe on the card when I have seen it survive a spring.

Dovan PrycéIt moved.

IlluminaraIt was allowed to. That's the difference.

Prycé took the pencil from behind his ear. In the gallery the bent spoke stood where he had left it, and tonight he did not need to go down and read it. In the lamp of the radio shed he opened a pocket notebook, copied out the crest figure and the date beside it, and then, in the space where for eleven years he had written the careful word, wrote another.

Holding.

Illuminara stayed until the water began to fall, and by morning she was gone. The valley woke to a river running full and quiet, and the school bus came down past the terraces and stopped at the clinic, as it did every day.

What the critic still says doesn't work

A dam assessor who hid a moving seam to save his valley chooses truth when Illuminara turns his strongest objection into an ordered repair, a resident-owned safety rule and a measurable storm test that keeps both the people and their livelihood standing.

Tonight's shape

Six scenes across eight weeks: a number that holds still, a seam in the dark, a loan read for its date and a door nobody had used, a price for water, a credit committee that hears the valley first, and a storm measured against a band written beforehand.

Slovenski prevod: GPT. Književna ocena velja za angleški izvirnik.

01 · Številka, ki miruje

Harrow Vale, pisarna uprave za vode · ponedeljek, 16:10 · šestinpetdeset dni pred sezono neurij

Regionalna oblast jo je poslala preverit poplavni model doline in model je bil dober. Illuminara je v njem preživela dva dni, kakor ključavničar preživi popoldne v ključavnici. Padavine so imele razpon. Prst je imela razpon. Le en vhodni podatek je popolnoma miroval, in miroval je nad šest tisoč strehami.

IlluminaraLevi opornik, blok enaintrideset. Enajst let meritev stika, ena na mesec. Gospod Prycé, voda v pobočju ne miruje tako spokojno. Vsak drug merilnik zatrepeta, ko dežuje po grebenu. Ta je gluh.

Dovan Prycé ji je postavil skodelico čaja ob komolec, preden je odgovoril. Imel je enainšestdeset let, bil je urejen in miren, človek, ki je vse življenje preživel kot najtišja oseba v prostorih, kjer so sprejemali odločitve.

Dovan PrycéMerilnik je star, stik pa suh. Temu bi rekel pohvala injektiranju. Harrow je dolgočasen jez, gospa Illuminara. Večina mojega dela je skrbno zapisovanje besede zadovoljivo.

Nekoč je bila sodna tolmačka in še vedno je prisluškovala stavkom, ki so bili vnaprej izurjeni. Ta je bil gladko obrušen. Dolgočasen, je rekel nežno, kakor človek, ki zapira vrata, ki jih je sam naoljil.

IlluminaraNikogar ne obtožujem. Sprašujem, kakšen bi moral biti jez, da bi bila ta serija poštena meritev. Rada bi ga videla. Nocoj, če mi posodite ključ galerije.

Premor je trajal manj kot pol sekunde. To ni bil strah. Bil je zvok človeka, ki se odloča, katero od dveh resnic bo ponudil najprej.

Dovan PrycéSvetloba je najboljša ob mraku. Vzemite modri ključ. Prišel bom za vami, ko zaklenem.

Ni pogledal proti polici za svojo pisalno mizo, kjer je na žeblju viselo kolesarsko kolo z eno manjkajočo napero. Opazila je, da ni pogledal.

02 · Svinčnik na zidu

Jez Harrow, galerija levega opornika, blok 31 · isti večer, 19:40

Beton je zapis teže. Illuminara je položila dlan na steno galerije in jo brala kot sistem poti, po katerih potuje sila, ter nekaj mest, kjer se poti niso strinjale. Pri bloku enaintrideset se je razcepila pot, ki se ne bi smela. Obremenitev je drsela vstran skozi stik, ki ni bil debelejši od črte svinčnika, in stik pod njeno roko ni miroval.

Razlaga, si je rekla. Ne dokaz. Preizkusi jo.

Uradni merilnik razpoke, medeninasti kazalnik, prilepljen čez stik, je bil skupaj z lestvico prebarvan s svežo občinsko zeleno barvo. Ob njem, v višini kolena, je bilo na zid pripeto ravnilo, čez razpoko pa je kot kazalec segala upognjena kolesarska napera. Po ometu navzdol je tekla kolona črtic s svinčnikom, vsaka z datumom v drobni, skrbni pisavi. Brala jih je od spodaj. Devet let so napredovale za štiri desetinke milimetra na leto. Nato zadnji dve zimi vsakič za milimeter in pol. In potem na vrhu tri črtice v devetih dneh.

Dovan PrycéNašli ste mojo napero.

IlluminaraNašla sem vaš svinčnik. To je najpoštenejši zapis v tej dolini.

Sedel je na boben za kable, kakor da so njegove noge nekaj sprejele pred njim.

Dovan PrycéOb štirih desetinkah sem lahko živel ob njem. Zdaj vsako jutro pridem sem, preden se odpre pisarna, da pogledam, ali se je kazalec premaknil, in vsako jutro se odločim, da ne bom zapisal, kar kaže.

IlluminaraKoliko časa po vaši presoji?

Dovan PrycéTakšen stik ne poči. Neha popuščati in na dan, ko neha, nima več česa popustiti. Še ena mokra jesen, povrh pa taljenje snega? Doline ne bi zastavil na drugo. Tudi na prvo ne.

IlluminaraZakaj potem barva?

Enkrat se je zasmejal samemu sebi.

Dovan PrycéPred enajstimi leti sem bil mlajši ocenjevalec. Stari Ketteridge je ob tem bloku na rob zapisal: zahteva ukrepanje. Aldermere je to prebral kot nevarno, ker ima njihova kreditna zaveza sprožilec in ta beseda ga je potegnila. Posojilo so odpoklicali v tridesetih dneh. Obrat so zaprli. Dva tisoč dvesto ljudi je odšlo v eni zimi in pomagal sem nalagati kombije. Na naslednjem potrdilu je bilo z mojo roko zapisano zadovoljivo, ob spremljanju, in odtlej spremljam. Vašo poštenost plačujejo hiše drugih ljudi. Pokažite mi popravilo, zaradi katerega nihče ne izgubi službe, ali pa priznajte, da boste nekoč storili, kar sem jaz, le z več pričami.

To je bil dober ugovor in Illuminara ni hitela mimo njega. Za njim je v temi galerije v odtoku kapljala voda, glasna kakor ura.

IlluminaraPopravila nimam. Imam dve dejstvi, nobeno pa še ni razsodba: stik se premika in enajst let ste bili edini, ki je to vedel. Zato vam lahko obljubim tole, in tu se obljuba konča. Nocoj ne prijavim ničesar. Nič z vašim imenom ne zapusti te galerije in nič o jezu ne gre posojilodajalcu ali mestu, dokler ne obstaja načrt, v katerem je prostor tudi za vas. Toda stika ne bom prepustila vašemu svinčniku, niti svojemu. Sunniva Achterberg bo ob prvem svitu čez blok enaintrideset položila optični kabel, da se bova prepirala z meritvijo, ne z menoj. Berem silo, gospod Prycé, in lahko se motim. Najprej pa me poučite o denarju doline.

Dovan PrycéDajte obljubi konec, pa vam dam številko. Voda na dolvodni strani tega bloka. Ali petnajst stotink milimetra čez razpoko v šestih urah. Tega se bojim od druge zime. Stik, ki naredi to, ne leze več.

IlluminaraPotem je to meja in narisali ste jo vi, ne jaz. Dokler kabel ni dejaven, bova napero odčitavala vsako uro, izmenoma vi in jaz, vsako vrednost pa zapisala pod prejšnjo; ista meja velja za napero in za kabel. Če jo kabel prestopi ali če vidim vodo na tisti strani, je tedna konec v tisti uri. Pokličem regionalno številko za nujne primere, odpre se načrt evakuacije, vi pa to prvi slišite od mene. Kaj boste upravi povedali o enajstih letih, izberete sami. Ali bodo ljudje opozorjeni, če stik popusti, pa ne. Znotraj te meje vas prosim za en teden.

Upognjeno napero je prijel med dva prsta in nekaj časa gledal le svojo pisavo.

Dovan PrycéEn teden. Pridite k meni. Veliko papirja imam.

03 · Beseda in datum

Prycéjeva kuhinja, Harrow Vale · ista noč, 01:20

Posojilna pogodba je odprta ležala na kuhinjski mizi, kup debel kakor opeka, v robovih pa enajst let njegovih zapiskov s svinčnikom. Denarja doline jo je učil tako, kakor človek uči jezik, ki ga je moral govoriti sam. Pri členu štirinajst pika tri mu je glas otrdel.

Dovan PrycéDogodek stanja jezu. Če pooblaščeni ocenjevalec razvrsti jez v razred D, nevaren, lahko posojilodajalec v tridesetih dneh odpokliče posojilo. To je vsa past. Ni pomembno, koliko stane popravilo. Pomembno je, da podpišem to besedo.

Past, je pomislila Illuminara, je narejena za eno določeno žival. Stavku je sledila do konca, kjer je odvisni člen ležal v lastni senci.

IlluminaraRazen če na datum takega potrdila že poteka program sanacije, ki ga je odobril inženir posojilodajalca. Ste to prebrali?

Dovan PrycéVelikokrat. Da bi program odobrili, mora nekdo posojilodajalcu razkriti napako. To je zaklenjena soba, ključ pa je ista beseda.

IlluminaraKdo razkrije? Ne vi. Vi ste ocenjevalec; vi potrjujete. Razkrije posojilojemalec. Preberite mi štirinajst pika pet.

Dovan PrycéPosojilojemalec lahko pisno obvesti o sumu stanja. Kreditni uradnik lahko odobri mirovanje do devetdeset dni, da inženir posojilodajalca pregleda program sanacije. Tako obvestilo ni potrdilo ali priznanje razvrstitve in nobena kršitev ni odpuščena.

Dovan PrycéAldermere je ta vrata napisal sam, po kombijih. Naslovljena so na posojilojemalca, jaz pa nikoli nisem bil posojilojemalec, zato sem jih vložil med administrativno.

IlluminaraPotem beseda nikoli ni bila ključ in nikoli ni bila ključavnica. Datum je. Člen štirinajst pika tri sprašuje, kaj se dogaja na dan, ko potrdite, ne kaj je bilo rečeno prej. Zato je zaporedje pomembnejše od poguma. Vossova da obvestilo po členu štirinajst pika pet. Halloran odobri mirovanje. Aldermerejev inženir odčita kabel in odobri program. Dovan PrycéDo takrat pa se stik še naprej premika.

Obračal je strani. Direktor Obrata je lahko zaradi pregleda, vzdrževanja ali varnosti gladino zbiralnika znižal za največ dvanajst metrov, ne da bi kogar koli obvestil.

IlluminaraPotisk na stik narašča s kvadratom globine. Harrow pri polnem bazenu drži osemintrideset metrov. Odvzemite enajst in potisk na blok enaintrideset bo približno polovičen. Znižanje ni program in posojilodajalec bi ga upravičeno zavrnil kot takega. Je pa tisto, kar ohrani stik živ v dneh, ki jih potrebuje papir.

Dovan PrycéEnajst metrov je triintrideset milijonov kubičnih metrov. Obrat bi svojo zalogo izlival po reki za nič.

IlluminaraNe za nič, če jo kdo dolvodno potrebuje. Komu jeseni manjka vode?

Odprl je usta, da bi rekel nikomur, pa jih spet zaprl. Terase: štiri tisoč hektarjev sadovnjakov in ozimne pšenice ter zunajtočne zbiralnike, ki jih je namakalna zveza Corrina Askeja vsako leto marca polnila po črpalni ceni.

IlluminaraNaj torej iz nje odgrizne ceno. Praznjenje mora biti počasno, največ četrt metra na dan, sicer bodo mokra ramena doline zdrsnila v bazen. To je štiriinštirideset dni. Imate jih šestinpetdeset. Začnite v četrtek, sicer zmanjka računice, še preden zmanjka vode.

Prycé je nekaj časa tiho sedel. Zunaj je pes nekoga lajal v nič.

Dovan PrycéIzpustili ste najmočnejšo stvar proti sebi in opazil sem. Tudi če posojilodajalec dovoli program, sem enajstkrat potrdil zadovoljivo. To je napačno prikazovanje, samostojna kršitev ne glede na jez. Izjema varuje človeka, ki zamuja. Ne varuje lažnivca.

04 · Koliko je vredna voda

Obrat Tarn, direktorjeva pisarna · sreda, 07:30

Marguerite Voss je vsemu prisluhnila stoje in s plaščem še vedno na sebi, kar je bilo v Harrow Valu znamenje vljudnosti. Ko je Prycé končal, ni pogledala Illuminare. Pogledala je njega.

Marguerite VossNa vašem stolu bi storila enako, zato lahko ta stol vržem v vas. Sami boste to povedali upravi, Dovan, s svojimi besedami, in ne boste se skrili za razpoko. To so moji pogoji. Teodor HalloranTo je kreditni pregled. Prva točka je skladnost s kreditnimi zavezami.

Posojilo bom evidentiral kot dejavno sanirano, ne kot nevarno. Potrdila gospoda Prycéja ostajajo pridržana, odbor bo odločil v devetdesetih dneh, jaz pa bi si poiskal odvetnika.

06 · Drži

Jez Harrow, krona · devetinštirideset dni pozneje, 02:10

Prvo neurje sezone je čez greben prišlo kakor na stežaj odprta vrata. Štiristo milimetrov v dveh dneh. Bazen se je ponoči dvignil za dve celi štiri desetinke metra, nad njim pa je bilo enajst metrov prazne doline. Dvaindvajset sider, zavrtanih v bloke od devetindvajset do triintrideset, je držalo stik v prijemu, kakršnega ni poznal, odkar je bil jez mlad.

Pred dežjem je Achterbergova na kartico zapisala tri vrstice in jo prilepila nad radio v galeriji. Ob dvigu za dve celi štiri desetinke metra naj bi se stik odprl za največ šest stotink milimetra. Za mejo, ki jo je Prycé narisal v galeriji, bi se oglasile sirene. Ob vsaki polni uri je Askejev nočni delavec po radiu glasno prebral meritev kabla. Do polnoči ena stotinka. Do ene dve. Do dveh tri.

Dovan PrycéŠtiriinštirideset dni zniževanja. Obrat bo deset let nosil nov dolg. Enajst družin je odšlo od septembra; pravijo, da bi odšle v vsakem primeru, čeprav v tem nikoli nisem našel utehe. Enaintridesetega končam. Odbor me bo kaznoval in vzel mi bo licenco. Moja odvetnica pravi, da bom obdržal hišo.

IlluminaraIn gospa Rowe?

Dovan PrycéFanta sta v ponedeljek šla v šolo na ravnici. V sredo je merila čas vaje s sirenami. Triindvajset minut. Pravi, da se ob treh še vedno zbuja. Rekel sem ji, da se tudi jaz, in rekla je: dobro.

Dež je udarjal po kroni. Pod njima je dolina ležala temna in neevakuirana, šest tisoč ljudi je spalo za zidom, ki je bil prvič v enajstih letih opisan resnično.

Ob treh je val dosegel vrh in iz galerije se je po radiu oglasil Achterbergin glas.

Sunniva Achterberg (radio)Vrh. Štiri stotinke. Znotraj območja. Celice sider so se dvignile z vodo, torej obremenitev nosijo sidra. Če bi to povzročil samo znižani bazen, ne bi pokazale ničesar. Varneje, izmerjeno. Besedo varno bom zapisala na kartico, ko bom videla, da preživi pomlad.

Dovan PrycéPremaknilo se je.

IlluminaraSmelo se je. V tem je razlika.

Prycé je izza ušesa vzel svinčnik. V galeriji je upognjena napera stala tam, kjer jo je pustil, in nocoj mu ni bilo treba iti dol, da bi jo odčital. V svetlobi radijske lope je odprl žepno beležnico, prepisal vrednost ob vrhu in datum ob njej, nato pa v prostor, kamor je enajst let zapisoval previdno besedo, napisal drugo.

Drži.

Illuminara je ostala, dokler voda ni začela upadati, do jutra pa je odšla. Dolina se je zbudila ob polni in tihi reki, šolski avtobus pa je pripeljal mimo teras in ustavil pri kliniki, kakor vsak dan.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Ocenjevalec jezu, ki je prikrival premikajoči se stik, da bi rešil svojo dolino, izbere resnico, ko Illuminara njegov najmočnejši ugovor spremeni v urejeno sanacijo, varnostno pravilo v lasti prebivalcev in merljiv preizkus v neurju, ki ohrani tako ljudi kot njihov način preživljanja.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Šest prizorov v osmih tednih: številka, ki miruje, stik v temi, posojilo, prebrano zaradi datuma in vrat, ki jih nihče ni uporabil, cena vode, kreditni odbor, ki najprej prisluhne dolini, ter neurje, izmerjeno glede na vnaprej zapisano območje.

2026-10-17THE VILLAGE THAT COULD NOT SLEEPVAS, KI NI MOGLA SPATI

Fourteen wounded villagers need surgery before the pass closes, every anaesthetic the clinic owns brings the same fever, and the one thing the patients share is not in their blood.Štirinajst ranjenih vaščanov potrebuje operacijo, preden se prelaz zapre, vsak anestetik v kliniki povzroči enako vročico, edina stvar, ki je bolnikom skupna, pa ni v njihovi krvi.

PublishedObjavljeno 2026-10-17T19:18:30Z · Permanent linkTrajna povezava

01 · Fever

Nemea clinic, operating room · 14:40

The third patient began to burn forty seconds after the anaesthetic took. Dr. Corin Hale stopped the drip before the monitor had finished saying so, and the room filled with the sound of everyone breathing in. Bartek Lind, who drove timber trucks and had been dug out of the cab of one that morning, lay rigid under the lights with a temperature climbing a degree every four minutes.

Osvin Pike, seventy, and Tomas Ketter, who tended the upper kilns, had burned the same way an hour before, one after the other. Both had been packed in ice and both had lived, and neither could be put under again. Illuminara had come up the pass with the first relief convoy and had been given a stool in the corner and a promise that she would be told if she was needed. She was needed now. She watched Hale pack the man in ice, and she did not speak until his temperature turned.

Corin HaleThat is the third. Same fever, three different people, and every agent I own does it. It is malignant hyperthermia or something that wears its face. It runs in families, and I have the dantrolene for two bodies, not fourteen.

Pelagia RuskThese are not families. I have the parish books. Eleven of the fourteen are Nemea people and they came here from three different valleys across a hundred years. Nobody in this clinic is anybody's cousin.

Corin HaleThen it is bad luck three times, and I do not operate on bad luck.

Corin HaleRegional blocks avoid those agents, but the spleen and the girl's leg cannot be reached that way. For the others it is one nerve at a time, twice the work, and each patient or family must consent. I might finish eight before dawn if my hands hold.

Dr. Rusk had run the village clinic for thirty years and was wearing the same cardigan she had been wearing when the mountain came down. She laid the list on the counter: fourteen names, four who could wait a night, ten who could not. A ruptured spleen. A pelvis. A girl of nine with a femur through the skin.

Nell Brandt, on the radioNemea, Brandt. Pass closes to freezing rain at twenty hundred. I can lift at seventeen and at nineteen, three stretchers a lift. That is six of fourteen. Tell me which six.

Pelagia RuskEight stay behind with an anaesthetic that fevers them.

Nell Brandt, on the radioThen six, and I will pray for the eight.

Illuminara took the list from the counter and read it as she read a load path, looking for the place where the weight did not go where it should. It took her a long minute. Then she asked Dr. Hale for the three charts, and the times on them.

02 · The Order of the Fevers

Nemea clinic, ward corridor · 15:05

She laid the charts side by side on the floor of the corridor, where the light was best. Beside the three reactions she set two more charts from among Rusk's fourteen: Sana Moreau, one of the three non-Nemea patients, a hiker whose wrist had been splinted with a sedative at noon without fever; and Ilo Vell, fourteen, sedated at one o'clock with a fever that had come and gone on its own.

IlluminaraA family trait would not care where a person slept. Read these five as a gradient. Osvin sat beside an upper-village hearth for seventy winters; Tomas lived at the kilns; Bartek slept two nights at the inn, which burns dark cake from the upper kilns; Ilo came home yesterday; Sana has not been indoors in Nemea once. Their reactions fall in that order.

Corin HaleA gradient by hours indoors. That is a coincidence of five.

IlluminaraFive out of five, in one direction. A gene cannot count nights. Something in the houses can. What is here that is not in the valley?

Nobody answered. Down the corridor a woman in a burned shawl, who had been sitting with her injured husband for six hours, lifted her head. This was Marta Vell, Ilo's mother and keeper of the village's resin kilns, and she had not been asked for anything yet.

Marta VellResin. Every house burns it. It has always been so; my grandmother's grandmother burned it. But it is not the old resin, not since the dry autumn.

Pelagia RuskWhat do you mean, not the old resin?

Marta VellThe upper kilns ran short. We stretched the cakes with bark off the scorched slope, the one that came down today. Dark cake. The houses below the church burn the pale stock still, because they were slow to buy. The kiln houses and the upper houses burn dark.

Illuminara said aloud that she had not known it and could not have known it.

IlluminaraThen here is the working model, written before we test it: something in the dark smoke may slow the same clearance pathway the anaesthetic needs. That is a prediction, not a diagnosis.

Corin HaleAnd the blood-gas analyser does not measure enzymes.

IlluminaraNot directly. Mix each plasma drop with the clinic's clear substrate and use the oxygen sensor to read consumption. If the dark cake is involved, the rate should follow hours in its smoke. Twelve minutes, no drug given. Marta, name the fourteen houses and what each burns.

Corin HaleAnd if you are wrong, ten people wait on a hearth superstition.

IlluminaraIf I am wrong, the machine says so in twelve minutes, and we have lost less time than your next attempt would cost.

Hale looked at the three charts for a long moment. Then he went to find the plasma tubes.

03 · Twelve Minutes

Nemea clinic, laboratory corner · 15:20 to 16:15

She wrote the ranking in the clinic log in ink, with the time, and Rusk signed beneath it as witness. At the bottom: the fourteen names in the order the machine ought to give them, slowest to fastest, built from Marta's list of what each house burned and how long each patient had sat in its smoke. Sana Moreau went last, as the fastest of all.

The machine took four samples at a time. Hale loaded it with a technician's care and a sceptic's face while Illuminara stood behind the curtain and did not touch the controls. At minute twelve the first run settled: Osvin Pike, slowest by a margin that made the technician swear softly, then Bartek Lind. Run after run, the slopes came out in the order she had written. When Hale loaded the fourth run, only two tubes remained. The room went quiet with a texture it had not had at the third fever.

In the fourth run the order broke. Osvin's grand-niece Ines, her left forearm splinted from the slide, sat on a stretcher beside his bed. She lived in a pale-stock house below the church. By Marta's list and Illuminara's ranking she belonged near the fast end. She stood fourth from the slow end.

Corin HaleThere. One in fourteen. Your hearth explains thirteen and fumbles the other.

IlluminaraIt is wrong. Good. A hearth that explained all fourteen would be a story. Marta—where did Ines sit today?

Marta VellShe sat on that stretcher beside Osvin. They carried him here from the slide at nine, and she has not left his bed.

Illuminara did not move for a moment. She had been looking at the houses, and the smoke she had been looking for had been in the room she was standing in.

IlluminaraDr. Rusk. What does this clinic burn?

Pelagia RuskThe big stove in the waiting room. The upper kiln sent down a sledge of cake at dawn, to keep the wounded warm. Dark cake. It is the only stove that heats all the beds.

There was a silence in which the hum of the machine was the loudest thing in the building.

IlluminaraThen the clinic is dosing every patient while they wait. The longer we argue, the slower the clearance. We are not waiting out the danger. We are feeding it.

On the screen the last pair settled. Sana Moreau, fastest of all, as written. The only fever in the room now was in Hale's face, and he was too honest a physician to hide that it was shame.

04 · The Stove

Nemea clinic, waiting room · 16:20 to 17:46

The stove stood at the end of the room like a low black animal, and a dozen faces turned from it to her as she came in. It was nine degrees outside the blanket line. Eight patients lay in shock with their feet toward the heat.

Pelagia RuskNo. If that goes cold, I lose the spleen and the girl to the chill before any anaesthetic touches them. You have found a poison and want me to put out the only fire.

IlluminaraNot put it out. Change one grate at a time, keep the other dark grate burning until the pale side catches, and measure every bed through the change. Marta, how much pale stock is left below the church?

Marta VellForty cakes. It was to last the village until the thaw. I can put the bakery's hot soapstone under the two shock beds while the first grate catches, but the stones will not hold all night.

IlluminaraThen I am asking you for the winter, and your stones buy the swap time. I will not pretend either is small.

Pelagia RuskTemperatures every four minutes. If any bed loses half a degree before the pale side catches, the dark grate stays lit. That is my stop condition.

Marta did not answer for the time it takes to count forty cakes against a hundred houses. Behind her a man lying under a coat said something about his own hearth and what it had meant for four hundred years, and she said, without turning, that the hearth had meant it for the living.

Marta VellOpen the storehouse. But I want it written that the village gave it, and that the dark cake does not go to the lowlands to be sold. It goes in the river.

IlluminaraWritten, and I will sign it where the village can read it.

The staged swap began at 16:24. Two kiln workers banked the left grate and laid pale cake on the right while Marta slid hot soapstone beneath the two shock beds. Hale opened the high vents. At 16:36 the girl's bedside reading had fallen four tenths of a degree. Rusk ordered the dark grate held for four more minutes; Marta shifted both hottest stones beneath the child and wrapped the exposed leg above the splint. By 16:44 the reading had recovered a tenth and the pale side held. Rusk alone gave the word to empty the dark grate, and the full pale burn caught four minutes later. While it caught, Rusk clipped a sheet to each flight stretcher with the dark-smoke hours and fever histories; Hale signed a request that the receiving anaesthetist hold the general until a clearance reading, or use a cooler regional option.

Nell Brandt, on the radioThe spleen and the girl's leg are the two your blocks cannot reach. I lift at seventeen with those two. The third stretcher space stays empty; nobody else is beyond the blocks, and I will not delay them to fill it. Note that I am not waiting for your assay.

IlluminaraDo not wait. Carry them. The next readings are for the twelve who remain.

At 17:04, forty minutes after the swap began and two minutes after Brandt lifted, Hale drew the second plasma set. Twelve minutes later the slopes had begun to rise. They rose unevenly, and Osvin Pike, who had breathed the most, had risen almost not at all.

Corin HaleTold you. He is the one it is in the blood for.

IlluminaraHe is also the one who has sat longest in the smoke. Draw him again at 17:34, seventy minutes after the swap began. If I am wrong about him, I will be glad to learn it now.

At 17:34 Hale drew the sample. At 17:46 the slope came up, slow and unmistakable. Osvin's was a washout, not a flaw. Hale took the sheet in both hands.

Corin HaleThen here is what I will do, and not one thing more. The two unreachable injuries are already in the air. I will block the eight urgent patients who remain, nerve by nerve, with consent and the numbers in front of them. The four who could safely wait will stay stabilised until their curves clear my line, and only then go under the general.

Pelagia RuskEight urgent blocks, four delayed generals, and the machine decides the order, not the clock.

Marta would not sign for her son's block until Hale put the plasma curve in her hands and showed her where Ilo sat and where the line ran. She read it with a finger. Then she signed, and said that she did not understand a word of it, and that she understood the line.

05 · What the Pass Kept

Nemea clinic and landing field · 17:00 to 04:10

The helicopter lifted at 17:02 with the girl of nine and the man with the ruptured spleen, and went up into the beginning of the rain with its lights burning. Brandt radioed from the valley at 17:58 that they were down and handed over: the receiving anaesthetist had read both sheets, run clearance readings and held each general until its line rose. The second lift at nineteen hundred stood on the pad unneeded, and she logged it that way, and told Illuminara afterwards that it was the best use of a flight she had ever made.

Dr. Hale blocked the eight urgent patients who remained. He worked from the least recovered to the most, with Rusk's hands beside his, and each time the plasma line had said who should be blocked and each time the family or the patient had said yes to the number. The four who had been stable enough to wait cleared his line in the early hours, went under the general and woke without fevers. The blocks took twice as long and held. Ines, her forearm dressed after her turn, fell asleep against the wall before Osvin Pike was wheeled in last at three-forty.

At 04:10 the last suture was in. In the waiting room the stove burned pale cake with its vents open, and nobody was cold, and nobody was feverish.

Illuminara stayed through the rest of the night because there was a log to finish. She wrote the protocol herself, in a plain hand, as Hale would use it and not as she would. A bench test: plasma and a clear substrate, with the oxygen sensor reading the consumption rate, twelve minutes, no drug given. A line below which a patient was blocked, not put under. A pair of readings half an hour apart for anyone who breathed smoke of unknown stock, because the slope moved and the machine could tell. Under it she wrote the name of the village that had given the winter, and the date, and Marta Vell signed it where the village could read it.

Pelagia RuskWhat does it cost us? Say the whole of it.

IlluminaraForty cakes of pale resin, which is the village's heat until the thaw. Dark cake does not burn indoors in Nemea again, and the kilns stand idle until someone finds what in the scorched bark does this. Two of the fourteen face long recoveries far from home. And the line on the sheet is provisional. It comes from fourteen people and one machine, and I would like it checked in a laboratory before anyone trusts it twice.

Pelagia RuskChecked. But it was right tonight.

IlluminaraIt was wrong once tonight, about Osvin's niece, and that is why it was right about the stove.

Rusk looked down the ward, where the patients slept in the pale light of the new fire. Then she asked Marta Vell to bring in forty cakes from the cold storehouse, and Marta, who had been awake for twenty-two hours, said she would bring them herself.

What the critic still says doesn't work

A village's own winter fuel turns out to be the anaesthetic's hidden accomplice, and a ranking written in ink before the test, wrong once and honest about it, lets four people with four vetoes change the fire without losing the wounded.

Tonight's shape

Intimate medical emergency in five scenes: a pattern read from the order of the reactions, a prediction written down before the test, one miss that exposes the real source in the clinic itself, and a costly village-wide agreement; the physician, the pilot, the headwoman and the anaesthetist each keep a veto.

Slovenski prevod: GPT. Književna ocena velja za angleški izvirnik.

01 · Vročica

Klinika Nemea, operacijska soba · 14:40

Tretji bolnik je začel goreti štirideset sekund po tem, ko je anestetik prijel. Dr. Corin Hale je ustavil infuzijo, še preden je monitor dokončal opozorilo, in sobo je napolnil zvok, kako so vsi hkrati zajeli sapo. Bartek Lind, voznik tovornjakov z lesom, ki so ga tisto jutro izkopali iz kabine enega od njih, je otrdel ležal pod lučmi, temperatura pa mu je naraščala za stopinjo vsake štiri minute.

Sedemdesetletni Osvin Pike in Tomas Ketter, ki je skrbel za zgornje peči, sta uro prej gorela enako, eden za drugim. Oba so obdali z ledom in oba sta preživela, vendar nobenega niso smeli znova uspavati. Illuminara je čez prelaz prišla s prvim konvojem pomoči; dali so ji stol v kotu in obljubili, da ji bodo povedali, če jo bodo potrebovali. Zdaj so jo. Gledala je, kako je Hale moža obdal z ledom, in spregovorila šele, ko se je njegova temperatura obrnila navzdol.

Corin HaleTo je tretji. Ista vročica, trije različni ljudje in povzroči jo vsak anestetik, ki ga imam. To je maligna hipertermija ali pa nekaj, kar nosi njen obraz. Pojavlja se v družinah, jaz pa imam dantrolena za dve telesi, ne za štirinajst.

Pelagia RuskTo niso družine. Imam župnijske knjige. Enajst od štirinajstih je ljudi iz Nemee, sem pa so v zadnjih sto letih prišli iz treh različnih dolin. Nihče v tej kliniki ni sorodnik nikogar drugega.

Corin HalePotem gre trikrat za nesrečno naključje, jaz pa ne operiram na podlagi nesrečnih naključij.

Corin HaleRegionalne blokade se izognejo tem anestetikom, toda tako ne morem doseči vranice in dekličine noge. Pri drugih pomeni en živec naenkrat, dvakrat toliko dela, vsak bolnik ali družina pa mora privoliti. Morda jih do zore dokončam osem, če bodo moje roke zdržale.

Dr. Rusk je vaško kliniko vodila trideset let in nosila isto jopico, ki jo je imela na sebi, ko se je utrgala gora. Na pult je položila seznam: štirinajst imen, štirje, ki so lahko počakali eno noč, deset, ki niso mogli. Počena vranica. Medenica. Devetletna deklica s stegnenico skozi kožo.

Nell Brandt, po radijski zveziNemea, Brandt. Ledeni dež bo ob dvajseti uri zaprl prelaz. Poletim lahko ob sedemnajstih in ob devetnajstih, tri nosila na polet. To je šest od štirinajstih. Povejte mi, katerih šest.

Pelagia RuskOsem jih ostane z anestetikom, ki jim povzroča vročico.

Nell Brandt, po radijski zveziPotem šest, za osem pa bom molila.

Illuminara je vzela seznam s pulta in ga brala, kakor je brala pot obremenitve, iskaje mesto, kjer se teža ni razporedila, kakor bi se morala. Potrebovala je dolgo minuto. Nato je dr. Hala prosila za tri kartoteke in za čase na njih.

02 · Zaporedje vročic

Klinika Nemea, hodnik oddelka · 15:05

Kartoteke je razgrnila drugo ob drugo na tleh hodnika, kjer je bila svetloba najboljša. Ob tri reakcije je položila še dve kartoteki izmed Ruskovih štirinajstih: Sana Moreau, ena od treh bolnic, ki niso bile iz Nemee, pohodnica, ki so ji opoldne ob pomirjevalu naravnali zapestje brez vročice; ter štirinajstletni Ilo Vell, ki so mu ob enih dali pomirjevalo, vročica pa je prišla in sama izzvenela.

IlluminaraDružinski dejavnik se ne bi menil za to, kje kdo spi. Teh pet berite kot gradient. Osvin je sedemdeset zim sedel ob ognjišču v zgornji vasi; Tomas je živel pri pečeh; Bartek je dve noči spal v gostišču, ki kuri temne pogače iz zgornjih peči; Ilo se je včeraj vrnil domov; Sana v Nemei še niti enkrat ni bila v zaprtem prostoru. Njihove reakcije si sledijo v tem zaporedju.

Corin HaleGradient po urah v zaprtih prostorih. To je naključje petih.

IlluminaraPet od petih, v eni smeri. Gen ne zna šteti noči. Nekaj v hišah jih lahko. Kaj je tukaj, česar v dolini ni?

Nihče ni odgovoril. Na drugem koncu hodnika je glavo dvignila ženska v ožganem šalu, ki je šest ur sedela ob svojem poškodovanem možu. To je bila Marta Vell, Ilova mati in skrbnica vaških smolnih peči, in doslej je še nihče ni ničesar vprašal.

Marta VellSmola. Kuri jo vsaka hiša. Tako je bilo od nekdaj; kurila jo je že mati moje prababice. Toda to ni stara smola, ne po suhi jeseni.

Pelagia RuskKako to misliš, da ni stara smola?

Marta VellZgornjim pečem je je zmanjkalo. Pogače smo podaljšali z lubjem z ožganega pobočja, tistega, ki se je danes utrgalo. Temne pogače. Hiše pod cerkvijo še kurijo svetlo zalogo, ker so jo kupovale počasi. Hiše pri pečeh in zgornje hiše kurijo temno.

Illuminara je na glas povedala, da tega ni vedela in da tega ni mogla vedeti.

IlluminaraPotem je delovni model, zapisan, preden ga preizkusimo, takšen: nekaj v temnem dimu morda upočasnjuje isto presnovno pot, ki jo potrebuje anestetik. To je napoved, ne diagnoza.

Corin HaleAnalizator krvnih plinov pa ne meri encimov.

IlluminaraNe neposredno. Vsako kapljico plazme zmešajte z bistrim substratom klinike in s senzorjem kisika odčitajte porabo. Če je vpletena temna pogača, bi morala hitrost slediti uram v njenem dimu. Dvanajst minut, brez danega zdravila. Marta, naštej štirinajst hiš in povej, kaj kuri vsaka.

Corin HaleČe se motiš, deset ljudi čaka zaradi vraževerja o ognjišču.

IlluminaraČe se motim, bo naprava to povedala v dvanajstih minutah, izgubili pa bomo manj časa, kot bi nas stal vaš naslednji poskus.

Hale je dolgo gledal tri kartoteke. Nato je šel po epruvete s plazmo.

03 · Dvanajst minut

Klinika Nemea, laboratorijski kotiček · 15:20 do 16:15

Razvrstitev je s časom vred s črnilom zapisala v dnevnik klinike, Rusk pa se je kot priča podpisala pod njo. Na dnu je bilo štirinajst imen v zaporedju, v katerem naj bi jih razvrstila naprava, od najpočasnejšega do najhitrejšega, sestavljenem po Martinem seznamu goriva vsake hiše in časa, ki ga je vsak bolnik presedel v njenem dimu. Sana Moreau je bila zadnja, najhitrejša med vsemi.

Naprava je sprejela po štiri vzorce. Hale jo je polnil s tehnikovo natančnostjo in skeptikovim obrazom, Illuminara pa je stala za zaveso in se ni dotaknila krmil. Po dvanajstih minutah se je prva serija ustalila: Osvin Pike, najpočasnejši z razliko, ob kateri je tehnik potiho zaklel, nato Bartek Lind. Serija za serijo so se nakloni razvrstili tako, kakor jih je zapisala. Ko je Hale naložil četrto serijo, sta ostali le še dve epruveti. Tišina v sobi je dobila teksturo, ki je ob tretji vročici ni imela.

V četrti seriji se je zaporedje podrlo. Osvinova pranečakinja Ines, z levo podlaktjo v opornici zaradi plazu, je sedela na nosilih ob njegovi postelji. Živela je v hiši s svetlo zalogo pod cerkvijo. Po Martinem seznamu in Illuminariini razvrstitvi bi morala biti blizu hitrega konca. Bila je četrta od počasnega konca.

Corin HaleTako. Ena od štirinajstih. Tvoje ognjišče pojasni trinajst ljudi, pri zadnji pa se spotakne.

IlluminaraNapačno je. Dobro. Ognjišče, ki bi pojasnilo vseh štirinajst, bi bilo zgodba. Marta — kje je danes sedela Ines?

Marta VellSedela je na tistih nosilih ob Osvinu. Po plazu so ga ob devetih prinesli sem, ona pa ni zapustila njegove postelje.

Illuminara se trenutek ni premaknila. Gledala je hiše, dim, ki ga je iskala, pa je bil v prostoru, v katerem je stala.

IlluminaraDr. Rusk. Kaj kuri ta klinika?

Pelagia RuskVeliko peč v čakalnici. Zgornja peč je ob zori poslala sani pogač, da bi ranjencem bilo toplo. Temne pogače. To je edina peč, ki ogreva vse postelje.

Nastala je tišina, v kateri je bilo brnenje naprave najglasnejši zvok v stavbi.

IlluminaraPotem klinika med čakanjem odmerja dim vsakemu bolniku. Dlje ko se prepiramo, počasnejše je izločanje. Ne čakamo, da nevarnost mine. Hranimo jo.

Na zaslonu se je ustalil zadnji par. Sana Moreau, najhitrejša med vsemi, kakor je bilo zapisano. Edina vročica v sobi je bila zdaj na Halovem obrazu, bil pa je preveč pošten zdravnik, da bi skrival, da gre za sram.

04 · Peč

Klinika Nemea, čakalnica · 16:20 do 17:46

Peč je stala na koncu sobe kakor nizka črna žival in ducat obrazov se je ob Illuminariinem prihodu obrnil od nje k njej. Onkraj vrste odej je bilo devet stopinj. Osem bolnikov je v šoku ležalo z nogami proti toploti.

Pelagia RuskNe. Če se to ohladi, bom zaradi mraza izgubila vranico in deklico, še preden se ju dotakne kakršen koli anestetik. Našla si strup, zdaj pa želiš pogasiti edini ogenj.

IlluminaraNe pogasiti. Menjajmo po eno rešetko, drugo temno rešetko pa pustimo goreti, dokler se svetla stran ne razgori, ter med menjavo merimo vsako posteljo. Marta, koliko svetle zaloge je še pod cerkvijo?

Marta VellŠtirideset pogač. Zadoščale naj bi vasi do odjuge. Medtem ko se prva rešetka razgoreva, lahko pod dve postelji bolnikov v šoku položim vroč milovec iz pekarne, toda kamni ne bodo držali vso noč.

IlluminaraPotem te prosim za zimo, tvoji kamni pa nam kupijo čas za menjavo. Ne bom se pretvarjala, da je eno ali drugo majhna stvar.

Pelagia RuskTemperature vsake štiri minute. Če katera koli postelja izgubi pol stopinje, preden se svetla stran razgori, ostane temna rešetka prižgana. To je moj pogoj za ustavitev.

Marta ni odgovorila toliko časa, kolikor ga je potrebno, da prešteješ štirideset pogač proti stotim hišam. Za njo je moški, ki je ležal pod plaščem, nekaj rekel o lastnem ognjišču in o tem, kaj je pomenilo štiristo let, ona pa je brez obračanja odgovorila, da je ognjišče pomenilo to za žive.

Marta VellOdprite skladišče. Toda hočem zapisano, da ga je dala vas in da temnih pogač ne bodo prodali v nižini. Končajo v reki.

IlluminaraZapisano, podpisala pa bom tam, kjer bo vas lahko prebrala.

Postopna menjava se je začela ob 16:24. Delavca pri pečeh sta zadušila levo rešetko in na desno položila svetle pogače, Marta pa je vroč milovec potisnila pod postelji dveh bolnikov v šoku. Hale je odprl zgornje zračnike. Ob 16:36 je odčitek ob dekličini postelji padel za štiri desetinke stopinje. Rusk je ukazala, naj temno rešetko pustijo še štiri minute; Marta je oba najbolj vroča kamna premaknila pod otroka in ovila izpostavljeni del noge nad opornico. Do 16:44 se je odčitek popravil za desetinko in svetla stran je vzdržala. Samo Rusk je dala ukaz, naj temno rešetko izpraznijo, polni svetli ogenj pa se je razgorel štiri minute pozneje. Medtem ko se je razgoreval, je Rusk na vsaka letalska nosila pripela list z urami v temnem dimu in zgodovino vročic; Hale je podpisal zahtevo, naj sprejemni anestezist splošno anestezijo zadrži do odčitka izločanja ali uporabi hladnejšo regionalno možnost.

Nell Brandt, po radijski zveziVranica in dekličina noga sta poškodbi, ki ju z blokadami ne morete doseči. Ob sedemnajstih poletim s tema dvema. Tretje mesto za nosila ostane prazno; nihče drug ni zunaj dosega blokad, jaz pa ju ne bom zadrževala zato, da ga zapolnim. Zapišite, da ne čakam na vašo analizo.

IlluminaraNe čakaj. Odpelji ju. Naslednji odčitki so za dvanajst, ki ostajajo.

Ob 17:04, štirideset minut po začetku menjave in dve minuti po Brandtinem vzletu, je Hale odvzel drugi niz plazme. Dvanajst minut pozneje so se nakloni začeli dvigati. Dvigali so se neenakomerno, Osvin Pike, ki je vdihnil največ dima, pa se skoraj ni premaknil.

Corin HaleSaj sem ti rekel. Pri njem je to v krvi.

IlluminaraOn je tudi tisti, ki je najdlje sedel v dimu. Ponovno mu vzemi vzorec ob 17:34, sedemdeset minut po začetku menjave. Če se glede njega motim, bom vesela, da to izvem zdaj.

Ob 17:34 je Hale odvzel vzorec. Ob 17:46 se je naklon dvignil, počasi in nezmotljivo. Pri Osvinu je šlo za izpiranje, ne za okvaro. Hale je prijel list z obema rokama.

Corin HalePotem bom storil tole in niti ene stvari več. Poškodovanca, katerih poškodb z blokadami ni mogoče doseči, sta že v zraku. Osmim nujnim bolnikom, ki ostajajo, bom en živec za drugim naredil blokade, s privolitvijo in številkami pred njimi. Štirje, ki lahko varno počakajo, bodo ostali stabilizirani, dokler njihove krivulje ne presežejo moje črte, šele nato bodo dobili splošno anestezijo.

Pelagia RuskOsem nujnih blokad, štiri odložene splošne anestezije, vrstni red pa določi naprava, ne ura.

Marta ni hotela podpisati privolitve za sinovo blokado, dokler ji Hale ni dal v roke krivulje plazme ter pokazal, kje je Ilo in kje teče črta. S prstom jo je prebrala. Nato je podpisala in rekla, da ne razume niti besede, razume pa črto.

05 · Kar je prelaz obdržal

Klinika Nemea in pristajališče · 17:00 do 04:10

Helikopter je ob 17:02 vzletel z devetletno deklico in moškim s počeno vranico ter se z gorečimi lučmi dvignil v začetek dežja. Brandt je iz doline ob 17:58 po radijski zvezi sporočila, da so pristali in bolnika predali: sprejemni anestezist je prebral oba lista, opravil meritvi izločanja in pri obeh zadržal splošno anestezijo, dokler se njuni črti nista dvignili. Drugi polet ob devetnajstih je ostal nepotreben na ploščadi, ona pa ga je tako tudi zabeležila in pozneje Illuminari povedala, da je bil to najbolje uporabljen polet v njenem življenju.

Dr. Hale je osmim nujnim bolnikom, ki so ostali, naredil blokade. Delal je od najmanj okrevanega do najbolj, ob njem so bile Ruskine roke, vsakič pa je črta plazme povedala, komu naj naredi blokado, in vsakič je družina ali bolnik številki rekel da. Štirje, ki so bili dovolj stabilni, da so čakali, so v zgodnjih urah prečkali njegovo črto, dobili splošno anestezijo in se zbudili brez vročice. Blokade so trajale dvakrat dlje in držale. Ines je z oskrbljeno podlaktjo po svojem posegu zaspala ob steni, preden so Osvina Pika zadnjega ob 3:40 odpeljali v operacijsko sobo.

Ob 04:10 je bil narejen zadnji šiv. V čakalnici je peč z odprtimi zračniki kurila svetle pogače, nikogar ni zeblo in nihče ni imel vročice.

Illuminara je ostala do konca noči, ker je bilo treba dokončati dnevnik. Protokol je napisala sama, z navadno pisavo, tako, kakor ga bo uporabljal Hale, ne tako, kakor bi ga ona. Preizkus na klopi: plazma in bistri substrat, senzor kisika pa odčita hitrost porabe, dvanajst minut, brez danega zdravila. Črta, pod katero bolnik dobi blokado, ne splošne anestezije. Dva odčitka s polurnim razmikom za vsakogar, ki je vdihoval dim neznane zaloge, kajti naklon se je spreminjal in naprava je to lahko pokazala. Pod njim je zapisala ime vasi, ki je dala svojo zimo, in datum, Marta Vell pa se je podpisala tam, kjer je vas lahko prebrala.

Pelagia RuskKaj nas to stane? Povej vse.

IlluminaraŠtirideset pogač svetle smole, ki so toplota vasi do odjuge. Temnih pogač v Nemei ne bodo nikoli več kurili v zaprtih prostorih, peči pa bodo stale, dokler nekdo ne odkrije, kaj v ožganem lubju povzroča to. Dva od štirinajstih čaka dolgo okrevanje daleč od doma. In črta na listu je začasna. Izvira iz štirinajstih ljudi in ene naprave, rada pa bi, da jo preverijo v laboratoriju, preden ji kdo zaupa še drugič.

Pelagia RuskPreverili jo bodo. Toda nocoj je bila pravilna.

IlluminaraNocoj se je enkrat zmotila, pri Osvinovi nečakinji, in prav zato je pravilno pokazala na peč.

Rusk je pogledala po oddelku, kjer so bolniki spali v svetli luči novega ognja. Nato je Marto Vell prosila, naj iz hladnega skladišča prinese štirideset pogač, Marta, ki je bila budna dvaindvajset ur, pa je rekla, da jih bo prinesla sama.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Vaško zimsko gorivo se izkaže za skritega sostorilca anestetika, razvrstitev, zapisana s črnilom pred preizkusom, enkrat napačna in o tem iskrena, pa štirim ljudem s štirimi pravicami veta omogoči zamenjati ogenj, ne da bi izgubili ranjence.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Intimna medicinska nevarnost v petih prizorih: vzorec, razbran iz zaporedja reakcij, napoved, zapisana pred preizkusom, ena izjema, ki razkrije pravi vir v sami kliniki, in drag dogovor cele vasi; zdravnik, pilotka, vaška predstojnica in anestezist vsak obdržijo pravico veta.

Shared strengthsSkupno prepoznane odlike

  • Both judges praised the falsifiable medical reasoning: a written ranking, one honest miss and a second exposure source turn diagnosis into observable causal work.
  • Both judges found agency genuinely distributed across the physician, fuel keeper, anaesthetist, patients and flight crew, with consent and stop conditions shaping the outcome.

Shared reservationsSkupni zadržki

  • Both judges found the biochemical mechanism and its rapid bedside discrimination somewhat compressed relative to the certainty required for treatment.
  • Both judges noted that several exchanges state protocol and causality with exceptional efficiency, leaving Illuminara and some supporting characters with limited private texture.
