2026-10-16FOUR KILOMETRES OF THIN ICEŠTIRJE KILOMETRI TANKEGA LEDU

Twenty-three people breathe the last of their air under four kilometres of ice, and a rescue vessel built to survive exactly one trip.Triindvajset ljudi diha zadnji zrak pod štirimi kilometri ledu, reševalno plovilo pa je zgrajeno tako, da prenese natanko eno pot.

PublishedObjavljeno 2026-10-16T19:13:44Z · Permanent linkTrajna povezava

01 · The Placard

Marrow Station, surface of the moon Tavret · Hour 0

The air clock for Halcyon was a magnetic placard on the control-room wall, and someone had been changing its digits by hand. It read 31:00. Underneath, in marker, a second number: 23.

Ofelia BrandtHalcyon sits on the sea floor, four thousand and ten metres below this floor: three thousand three hundred and fifty of ice, then six hundred and sixty of water. Twenty-three people, thirty-one hours of air at the rate they breathe now, saturated at six bar, so they cannot simply be pulled up. The shaft froze round the lift cage at 03:40. We have one vessel that can go down: Curlew, a drone-sub, six seats, nine hours from launch to Halcyon's door.

Illuminara stood at the placard rather than the screens. Nobody had yet asked her to sit.

Imre SolanoThe hull is certified for one excursion below three thousand metres. Down and back, to anywhere past that line. Two of twelve test hulls cracked on the second. I won't fly a second one, and you shouldn't ask me to.

Ofelia BrandtSo six come up. Seventeen don't. I have been told to draw lots, and I would rather resign.

IlluminaraWhat exactly does the certificate count?

Imre SolanoPressure excursions. Full cycles past the line.

IlluminaraThen the excursion is the scarce thing, not the seats. I'd like to ask what it is best spent on before we ask who sits in it.

Ofelia BrandtIt carries six. That's what it is for.

IlluminaraWho says the cage is dead?

Ofelia BrandtIt stopped moving, and the shaft is ice around it.

IlluminaraStopped is not dead. May I see what it has been saying since 03:40?

02 · The Trace

Halcyon, 4,010 m below · Hour 2

The habitat's voice came up the cable thin and a little late. Illuminara spoke to the commander first and the engineer second.

Marit OseiI've heard about the lots. I will not run them. If someone has a plan that is not a plan to choose who dies, say it quickly, because my people are watching me listen.

IlluminaraI have a question rather than a plan. Maud Okonkwo logged the cage's frame strain. Maud, there is a wobble in it.

Maud OkonkwoSensor noise. It rises and falls, a third or so. I flagged it and wrote it off.

Illuminara laid the trace beside a second line: the swell and slackening of the ice shell as Tavret's orbit pulled it. The peaks of the wobble sat on the peaks of the flex, every three hours and fourteen minutes.

IlluminaraNoise does not keep the moon's time. I think the cage is held by ice that is squeezed against it, and every time the shell relaxes, the squeeze eases. That gives us a door that opens for a few minutes every three hours and fourteen.

Maud OkonkwoThen you have the wrong picture, and it matters. The shaft is clear above and below. The cage climbs on pinions, on a rack; the cable is only voice and power. The ice is on three roller carriages and fourteen metres of rack at the collar, glazed over the teeth. Free those and the cage is free.

Fourteen metres, not a plug a hundred long. Illuminara took the correction the way she took a good measurement, with gratitude and without ceremony. It made the plan smaller. It also made it possible.

Marit OseiAnd you want my people to bet their lives on a wobble.

IlluminaraI want them to measure it, which costs only waiting. The next peak is at Hour 6 and twenty-six minutes. If the strain does not fall by at least a third and stay down for five minutes, I am wrong, and the director's lots are the plan. I'll say so myself.

03 · One Excursion

Marrow Station, control room · Hour 6, 26 minutes

Maud Okonkwo read the number up the cable as it fell, and the room, which had not been quiet, became so.

Maud OkonkwoDown thirty-eight percent. Holding. Five minutes. Nine. It's coming back up now.

Ofelia BrandtSo the door exists. What holds it?

IlluminaraI do, partly, and I want you to hear my limit rather than guess it. At a peak I can carry the cage's whole weight for eight minutes. At a trough, ninety seconds. On glazed rack the pinions skip and the carriages carry nothing, so for those fourteen metres someone must lift all of it. I can lift that, or I can keep the ice walls from closing on a cut lane. Not both for more than ninety seconds.

Ofelia BrandtSeats save six and spend the hull.

IlluminaraAnd leave seventeen with air and nothing left to come for them. A second excursion is the other road, and Imre has told us what it costs.

Imre SolanoThen the walls need something else.

IlluminaraA frame. Four titanium struts to jack the lane open to a metre, and a hot-water lance for the carriages. The struts hold the walls and I hold the weight, and neither of us holds the other's share. Curlew carries it in the seat bay; Imre began swapping seats for struts at Hour 3, under protest, as a job that can be undone. It goes down once, to the collar, and comes home. It never touches a passenger.

Imre SolanoEach strut seats in a socket drilled into the collar casting, is good for a hundred and forty kilonewtons, and is rated for forty troughs. At a trough the ice leans on each with about eighty. The frame runs on its own cells for sixty hours and reads up the cable's data pair, so it does not need Curlew once it is placed. The cancel line is a hundred and twenty kilonewtons on any strut, or three centimetres of total walk in any socket, counted from installation. Past it the frame is finished and so is every crossing after. I read them at every trough, and nobody boards until I have read one with the cage gone.

Ofelia BrandtAnd if your eight minutes fail?

IlluminaraThen Curlew is spent and so are we, and I will have cost you the six. I am sure of the arithmetic, not of the ice. So the first window frees an empty cage. If I fail there, nobody is in it.

Maud OkonkwoThe cage runs two-tenths of a metre a second. Fifty-five minutes between the collar and us, four hours thirty-nine between the collar and the surface. Windows from 9:40, every three-fourteen. Which can we afford to miss?

IlluminaraThe frame at 9:40, the cage free by 9:48 and with you at 10:43. If twelve are aboard by 11:55 it meets the collar at 12:50 and crosses at the 12:54 window, and they dock at the surface at 17:40. Empty, it is back at the collar at 22:40, four minutes after the 22:36 window. So it crosses at 25:50, reaches you at 26:52, and the eleven cross at 29:04 and dock at 33:50.

Maud OkonkwoAir is the other clock, and it's mine. Thirty-one hours at twenty-three people. The twelve you take first are the stretcher cases and the over-fifties, and they are fifty-nine percent of what Halcyon breathes. When they go at 11:55 there are nineteen hours and five minutes left at the old rate. The eleven stretch that to forty-six and a half. Halcyon is dead at 58:27.

IlluminaraThe cage carries eight hours of its own air and needs under six from the landing to the surface, so the eleven can seal themselves into it as late as an hour before Halcyon dies. The last window that works is 58:10. Anything before it, we can afford to miss.

Imre SolanoThree hours to the collar, and ten minutes to deploy. The other six hours of the nine are open water and a blind dock, and we are not going there. I leave at 6:30, and I fly it once.

Ofelia BrandtThen fly.

04 · The Order

Halcyon, crew deck and wet porch · Hour 7

The cage was rated for twelve, by weight on the rack. That left a number nobody wanted to say, and Commander Osei said it.

Marit OseiTwelve now. Eleven later. Which of you goes first is our decision, not the surface's, and it is not going to be a draw.

Anselm DubeI'll propose, and you can tear it up. First lift: the stretcher cases, the over-fifties, the three who feel the cold worst. Everyone whose body gets less safe the longer it waits. They also breathe fastest, so when they go, the rest have forty-six hours.

Kofi MensahAnd who is last?

Anselm DubeThe ones who can still work. Maud, those of us who know the rack, the commander.

Kofi MensahThat isn't an order, that's a sentence. You're making the strongest wait because they can.

Marit OseiYes. So here is what goes with the sentence. The last eleven choose the hour. Imre's line is the line: past a hundred and twenty kilonewtons or three centimetres of walk, nobody crosses and nobody argues. Inside it, any one of us can refuse a crossing, and nobody is carried against a refusal. The surface does not get a vote.

Anselm DubeOne voice can cost all eleven of us a window.

Marit OseiA window costs three hours fourteen of our own air, which we can count. A crossing costs what it costs, which we can't.

Then she asked the harder thing as a question. The cage would land on six metres of iced rack, and someone had to go out through the porch and clear it with a hot hose before 10:40.

Marit OseiNobody is ordered. If nobody goes, we clear it from inside the cage, which costs eighteen minutes of boarding. The first lift misses 12:54 and waits for 16:08. I am telling you the price so that you can say no knowing it.

Anselm DubeEveryone in this room owes the table three days of decompression. A body that has worked the porch owes forty hours more. Nobody should pretend that is nothing.

Two people said no, plainly, and Marit marked their names down without a word. Maud Okonkwo went because she alone knew the rack, Anselm because he had worked a porch before, and Kofi Mensah because he had asked what the strong owed the sick and had not liked the silence that followed. Maud was back through the lock by 9:25 with ice in her cuffs, and took the headset.

05 · Eight Minutes

The collar, 3,350 m down · Hour 9, 40 minutes

Illuminara had come down beside the cable, past the point where light was an idea, and the cold and the pressure meant what they always meant to her, which was information. She could read where it carried load, and where it was about to close.

Curlew hung at the collar with the frame folded in its belly. Solano's voice in the water was calm and slightly hoarse.

Imre SolanoStruts deploying. Lane walls at one metre. Lance in two minutes. Peak window opens now.

The struts took the walls. That was the arrangement, and it was a relief to be only the other half of it. She took the cage's weight. It came into her like a held note, fourteen tonnes of rack and steel that the ice was trying to keep. The first carriage let go at minute two. The second at minute four. The third did not.

Imre SolanoCarriage three isn't releasing. Minute five. Three left.

Maud OkonkwoThat carriage has a shear pin on the east face. It's the one I always said we should replace. Hit it from the east.

Solano swung the lance. Illuminara felt the pin go, a small bright failure, and the cage dropped out of the ice onto clean rack and its own pinions for the first time since 03:40. She released it at minute seven and fifty seconds.

IlluminaraThat is as long as I can hold. If it had needed ten seconds more, it would not have been me who could give them.

The cage ran down toward the habitat, empty, at a stately two-tenths of a metre a second, and the voices from Halcyon began to cheer, and she let them. She did not go up. The windows would not wait for her to come back down, so she stayed in the lee of the frame, in the dark.

At 11:17, in the first trough, the struts took the ice alone, and Solano, rising in Curlew, read them off the telemetry: fifty-eight percent of rating, eleven millimetres of walk.

Imre SolanoInside. Board them.

They were aboard at 11:55, twelve of them, with the stretchers latched. At 12:50 the cage came up to the collar, and this time there was weight in it, and the weight had names. She held it across the fourteen metres for seven minutes and thirty seconds. The struts held the walls. Curlew broke the surface at 12:55, its excursion spent, flown once as promised.

The cage went on up the open bore, four hours and thirty-nine minutes of it, with six bar held inside. At 17:40 its door mated to the chamber door at Marrow Station and the first twelve began to come down to surface pressure at the pace their bodies allowed.

06 · The Second Ride

The collar, 3,350 m down · Hour 22, 40 minutes

The empty cage came down the bore on schedule, and that was the trouble. It stopped at the glazed rack four minutes after the 22:36 peak, and Illuminara felt the squeeze already coming back through the ice.

Maud OkonkwoFour minutes. Say it plainly so we can stop doing sums. Can you hold it?

IlluminaraNot across. I would have under three minutes and the crossing needs seven. The ice would close on the cage in the middle of the rack. The next window is 25:50.

Nobody on the cable suggested she try. Her being sure of a limit had never cost anyone anything before; now it cost three hours and fourteen minutes of air that Halcyon could count.

The frame stood alone through the long wait, on its own cells, reading up the cable's data pair, and Maud logged every trough. Walk of 1.4 centimetres at 14:31. Two point zero at 17:45. Two point six at 20:59, inside the line. Then, at 24:13, with the cage parked above it and nothing moving, two point two.

At 25:50 Illuminara carried the empty cage across. At 26:52 it settled on the landing the porch team had cleared, and eleven people mustered at the cage door at the pace of people who could walk.

Imre SolanoTrough, 27:27. One hundred and four kilonewtons, walk two point six centimetres. The line is one-twenty and three. It is inside the line, and I would let you cross.

Marit OseiInside the line, then it's ours. One voice is enough. Who refuses?

Kofi MensahI do. One window. Look at the log: at 20:59 it read two point six, and at 24:13 it was back to two point two with nobody touching it. I would rather pay in air than cross on two point six with eleven people in the cage.

Maud OkonkwoHe's reading the trace correctly. It does relax.

Anselm DubeThree hours fourteen.

Marit OseiCounted. We have twenty-nine hours of air and the crossing needs five. Refusal stands. We cross at 32:18.

They waited. At 30:41 the trough read two point three centimetres. At 31:15 the cage left the landing, and at 32:18 Illuminara took its weight, tired, and carried it across in seven minutes and fifteen seconds, fifteen seconds under what she had said. Then she rose beside it for four hours and thirty-nine minutes at two-tenths of a metre a second, a speed she found tedious and kept to.

At 37:04 the door mated. Marit Osei was the last one through it.

Marit OseiThat's the eleven. You can tell your director the habitat is switched off.

Ofelia BrandtThe company will want to know whose fault the freeze was.

Marit OseiThe company can write it on whatever placard it likes. Twenty-three people are in a chamber with a door.

Maud, Anselm and Kofi would spend their extra forty hours on the table without complaint. Illuminara sat on the floor of the control room for the first time since she had come up. She did not pretend to be anything but tired. She said so, plainly, to the room, and to Solano.

On the wall, someone had finally wiped off the digits. In their place, in marker, was the number 23, and a line under it.

What the critic still says doesn't work

Twenty-three people escape four kilometres of ice because Illuminara spends a one-use vessel on a tool rather than on six seats, states exactly where her own strength ends, and leaves the trapped crew the evidence and authority to choose the final crossing.

Tonight's shape

Rescue under a clock in six scenes: the hull's one excursion is spent on a tool instead of on seats, the tide sets the windows, and the people who wait are given the last word on when they cross.

Slovenski prevod: GPT. Književna ocena velja za angleški izvirnik.

01 · Tabla

Postaja Marrow, površje lune Tavret · Ura 0

Zračna ura za Halcyon je bila magnetna tabla na steni nadzorne sobe in nekdo je njene številke spreminjal na roko. Kazala je 31:00. Pod njo je bilo s flomastrom zapisano drugo število: 23.

Ofelia BrandtHalcyon leži na morskem dnu, štiri tisoč deset metrov pod temi tlemi: tri tisoč tristo petdeset metrov ledu, nato šeststo šestdeset metrov vode. Triindvajset ljudi, enaintrideset ur zraka pri sedanji hitrosti dihanja, nasičeni pri šestih barih, zato jih ne moremo preprosto potegniti navzgor. Jašek je ob 03:40 zamrznil okoli dvigalne kletke. Imamo eno plovilo, ki lahko gre dol: Curlew, brezpilotno podmornico s šestimi sedeži, devet ur od izplutja do vrat Halcyona.

Illuminara je stala pri tabli, ne pri zaslonih. Nihče je še ni prosil, naj sede.

Imre SolanoTrup je certificiran za eno odpravo pod tri tisoč metrov. Dol in nazaj, kamor koli pod to mejo. Dva od dvanajstih preizkusnih trupov sta pri drugi odpravi počila. Drugega ne bom upravljal in tudi vi me ne bi smeli prositi.

Ofelia BrandtTorej jih šest pride gor. Sedemnajst jih ne. Naročili so mi, naj žrebam, jaz pa bi raje odstopila.

IlluminaraKaj natančno šteje certifikat?

Imre SolanoTlačne odprave. Celotne cikle prek meje.

IlluminaraPotem je redka dobrina odprava, ne sedeži. Preden vprašamo, kdo naj sede vanj, bi rada vprašala, za kaj jo je najbolje porabiti.

Ofelia BrandtPrepelje jih šest. Temu je namenjeno.

IlluminaraKdo pravi, da je kletka mrtva?

Ofelia BrandtNehala se je premikati in jašek okoli nje je zaledenel.

IlluminaraUstavljeno ni mrtvo. Smem videti, kaj sporoča od 03:40?

02 · Sled

Halcyon, 4.010 m spodaj · Ura 2

Glas habitata je po kablu prihajal šibek in z rahlo zamudo. Illuminara je najprej govorila s poveljnico in nato z inženirko.

Marit OseiSlišala sem za žreb. Ne bom ga izvedla. Če ima kdo načrt, ki ni načrt izbire, kdo bo umrl, naj ga hitro pove, ker moji ljudje gledajo, kako poslušam.

IlluminaraNamesto načrta imam vprašanje. Maud Okonkwo je beležila napetost ogrodja kletke. Maud, v zapisu je nihanje.

Maud OkonkwoŠum tipala. Dviga se in pada za približno tretjino. Označila sem ga in odpisala.

Illuminara je sled položila ob drugo črto: naraščanje in popuščanje ledenega ovoja, ko ga je vlekla Tavretova orbita. Vrhovi nihanja so se ujemali z vrhovi upogiba, vsakih tri ure in štirinajst minut.

IlluminaraŠum se ne ravna po luninem času. Mislim, da kletko drži led, stisnjen obnjo, in vsakič, ko ovoj popusti, pritisk oslabi. Tako dobimo vrata, ki se vsakih tri ure in štirinajst minut za nekaj minut odprejo.

Maud OkonkwoPotem si predstavljaš narobe, in to je pomembno. Jašek je nad in pod kletko čist. Kletka se vzpenja z zobniki po zobati letvi; kabel prenaša samo glas in elektriko. Led je na treh vozičkih z valji in na štirinajstih metrih letve pri ovratniku, kjer so zobje poledeneli. Osvobodite to in kletka bo prosta.

Štirinajst metrov, ne sto metrov dolg čep. Illuminara je popravek sprejela tako, kot je sprejela dobro meritev, s hvaležnostjo in brez slovesnosti. Načrt je postal manjši. Postal je tudi mogoč.

Marit OseiIn želiš, da moji ljudje zastavijo življenja na nihanju.

IlluminaraŽelim, da ga izmerijo, kar stane samo čakanje. Naslednji vrh bo ob uri 6 in šestindvajset minut. Če napetost ne pade za vsaj tretjino in ne ostane nizka pet minut, se motim in načrt direktorice je žreb. Sama bom to povedala.

03 · Ena odprava

Postaja Marrow, nadzorna soba · Ura 6, 26 minut

Maud Okonkwo je po kablu odčitavala številko, ko je padala, in soba, ki prej ni bila tiha, je utihnila.

Maud OkonkwoDol za osemintrideset odstotkov. Drži. Pet minut. Devet. Zdaj spet raste.

Ofelia BrandtTorej vrata obstajajo. Kaj jih drži odprta?

IlluminaraDeloma jaz, in želim, da slišite mojo omejitev, namesto da o njej ugibate. Na vrhu lahko osem minut nosim vso težo kletke. Na dnu devetdeset sekund. Na poledeneli letvi zobniki preskakujejo in vozički ne nosijo ničesar, zato mora na teh štirinajstih metrih nekdo dvigniti vse. Lahko dvignem to ali pa preprečim, da bi se ledene stene zaprle nad izrezanim prehodom. Obojega ne morem početi dlje kot devetdeset sekund.

Ofelia BrandtSedeži rešijo šest ljudi in porabijo trup.

IlluminaraSedemnajstim pa pustijo zrak, ne da bi ostalo kaj, kar bi lahko prišlo ponje. Druga odprava je druga možnost in Imre nam je povedal, koliko stane.

Imre SolanoPotem mora stene držati nekaj drugega.

IlluminaraOgrodje. Štirje titanovi oporniki, ki bodo prehod razprli na en meter, in sulica z vročo vodo za vozičke. Oporniki držijo stene, jaz pa težo, in nobeden od naju ne nosi deleža drugega. Curlew to prepelje v prostoru za sedeže; Imre je ob uri 3 pod protestom začel zamenjevati sedeže z oporniki, kot delo, ki se še lahko razveljavi. Enkrat se spusti do ovratnika in se vrne domov. Nikoli se ga ne dotakne potnik.

Imre SolanoVsak opornik sede v ležišče, izvrtano v ulitek ovratnika, prenese sto štirideset kilonjutnov in je predviden za štirideset dolin. V dolini led pritiska na vsakega s približno osemdesetimi. Ogrodje šestdeset ur deluje na lastne celice in podatke bere po paru v kablu, zato Curlewa po namestitvi ne potrebuje. Meja za preklic je sto dvajset kilonjutnov na katerem koli oporniku ali skupni premik katerega koli ležišča za tri centimetre, merjeno od namestitve. Ko jo presežemo, je ogrodja konec in z njim vsakega naslednjega prečkanja. Odčitke preverim v vsaki dolini in nihče ne vstopi, dokler ne preberem enega po odhodu kletke.

Ofelia BrandtIn če tvojih osem minut ne bo dovolj?

IlluminaraPotem bo Curlew porabljen in z njim tudi mi, jaz pa vas bom stala tistih šestih. Prepričana sem o računu, ne o ledu. Zato prvo okno osvobodi prazno kletko. Če mi tam spodleti, v njej ne bo nikogar.

Maud OkonkwoKletka se premika dve desetinki metra na sekundo. Petinpetdeset minut med ovratnikom in nami, štiri ure devetintrideset med ovratnikom in površjem. Okna se odprejo od 9:40 naprej, vsake tri štirinajst. Katera lahko zgrešimo?

IlluminaraOgrodje ob 9:40, kletka prosta do 9:48 in pri vas ob 10:43. Če je dvanajst ljudi vkrcanih do 11:55, doseže ovratnik ob 12:50 in ga prečka v oknu ob 12:54, na površju pa pristane ob 17:40. Prazna se vrne do ovratnika ob 22:40, štiri minute po oknu ob 22:36. Zato prečka ob 25:50, do vas prispe ob 26:52, enajsterica pa prečka ob 29:04 in pristane ob 33:50.

Maud OkonkwoZrak je druga ura in zanjo sem odgovorna jaz. Enaintrideset ur za triindvajset ljudi. Dvanajsterica, ki jo odpeljete prva, so bolniki na nosilih in starejši od petdeset let, skupaj pa porabijo devetinpetdeset odstotkov vsega, kar diha Halcyon. Ko odidejo ob 11:55, pri stari porabi ostane devetnajst ur in pet minut. Enajsterica to raztegne na šestinštirideset ur in pol. Halcyon ugasne ob 58:27.

IlluminaraKletka ima osem ur lastnega zraka in od pristanka do površja potrebuje manj kot šest, zato se lahko enajsterica vanjo zapre še eno uro pred smrtjo Halcyona. Zadnje uporabno okno je ob 58:10. Vsako prej si lahko privoščimo zgrešiti.

Imre SolanoTri ure do ovratnika in deset minut za namestitev. Drugih šest ur od devetih odpade na odprto vodo in slepo pristajanje, tja pa ne gremo. Odhajam ob 6:30 in upravljam ga enkrat.

Ofelia BrandtPotem poleti.

04 · Vrstni red

Halcyon, paluba posadke in mokra preddverna komora · Ura 7

Kletka je bila po nosilnosti zobate letve predvidena za dvanajst ljudi. Tako je ostalo število, ki ga nihče ni želel izreči, in poveljnica Osei ga je izrekla.

Marit OseiDvanajst zdaj. Enajst pozneje. Kdo med vami gre prvi, je naša odločitev, ne odločitev površja, in ne bomo žrebali.

Anselm DubePredlagal bom, vi pa lahko predlog zavržete. Prvo dvigalo: bolniki na nosilih, starejši od petdeset let, trije, ki najtežje prenašajo mraz. Vsi, katerih telo je manj varno, čim dlje čakajo. Tudi najhitreje dihajo, zato bo drugim po njihovem odhodu ostalo šestinštirideset ur.

Kofi MensahIn kdo gre zadnji?

Anselm DubeTisti, ki še lahko delamo. Maud, tisti med nami, ki poznamo letev, poveljnica.

Kofi MensahTo ni vrstni red, to je obsodba. Najmočnejše silite čakati zato, ker zmorejo.

Marit OseiDa. Zato obsodbo spremlja naslednje. Zadnjih enajst izbere uro. Imrejeva meja je meja: čez sto dvajset kilonjutnov ali tri centimetre premika nihče ne prečka in nihče se ne prepira. Znotraj nje lahko kateri koli od nas zavrne prečkanje in nikogar ne bodo odnesli proti njegovi zavrnitvi. Površje nima glasovalne pravice.

Anselm DubeEn glas lahko vseh enajst stane celo okno.

Marit OseiOkno stane tri ure in štirinajst minut našega lastnega zraka, ki jih lahko preštejemo. Prečkanje stane, kolikor pač stane, česar ne moremo.

Nato je težjo stvar izrekla kot vprašanje. Kletka bo pristala na šestih metrih poledenele letve in nekdo bo moral skozi preddverno komoro ter jo z vročo cevjo očistiti pred 10:40.

Marit OseiNihče ne bo dobil ukaza. Če ne gre nihče, jo očistimo iz notranjosti kletke, kar pomeni osemnajst minut vkrcavanja. Prvo dvigalo bo zamudilo 12:54 in počakalo do 16:08. Ceno vam povem zato, da lahko rečete ne in veste, kaj to pomeni.

Anselm DubeVsi v tej sobi dolgujemo dekompresijski mizi tri dni. Telo, ki je delalo v preddverni komori, ji dolguje še štirideset ur. Nihče naj se ne pretvarja, da to ni nič.

Dva človeka sta jasno rekla ne in Marit je njuni imeni zapisala brez besede. Maud Okonkwo je šla, ker je samo ona poznala letev, Anselm, ker je že delal v preddverni komori, in Kofi Mensah, ker je vprašal, kaj močni dolgujejo bolnim, in mu tišina, ki je sledila, ni bila všeč. Maud se je ob 9:25 vrnila skozi zaporo z ledom v manšetah in prevzela slušalke.

05 · Osem minut

Ovratnik, 3.350 m spodaj · Ura 9, 40 minut

Illuminara se je spustila ob kablu, mimo točke, kjer je bila svetloba zgolj ideja, mraz in pritisk pa sta ji pomenila to, kar sta ji pomenila vedno: informacijo. Čutila je, kje led nosi obremenitev in kje se namerava zapreti.

Curlew je visel pri ovratniku z zloženim ogrodjem v trebuhu. Solanov glas v vodi je bil miren in rahlo hripav.

Imre SolanoOporniki se nameščajo. Stene prehoda so meter narazen. Sulica čez dve minuti. Vršno okno se odpira zdaj.

Oporniki so prevzeli stene. Tako so se dogovorili in olajšanje je bilo biti samo druga polovica. Prevzela je težo kletke. Prišla je vanjo kot zadržan ton, štirinajst ton letve in jekla, ki ju je led skušal obdržati. Prvi voziček je popustil v drugi minuti. Drugi v četrti. Tretji ni.

Imre SolanoTretji voziček ne popušča. Peta minuta. Še tri.

Maud OkonkwoTa voziček ima strižni zatič na vzhodni strani. To je tisti, za katerega sem vedno govorila, da ga moramo zamenjati. Udari ga z vzhoda.

Solano je zasukal sulico. Illuminara je začutila, kako se je zatič v drobnem svetlem zlomu vdal, in kletka se je prvič po 03:40 izmaknila ledu ter sedla na čisto letev in lastne zobnike. Spustila jo je v sedmi minuti in petdeseti sekundi.

IlluminaraToliko časa zmorem držati. Če bi potrebovala še deset sekund, ji jih ne bi mogla dati jaz.

Kletka se je po dve desetinki metra na sekundo veličastno počasi spuščala proti habitatu in glasovi iz Halcyona so začeli vzklikati, ona pa jim je dovolila. Sama ni šla gor. Okna ne bi čakala, da se vrne dol, zato je ostala v zavetrju ogrodja, v temi.

Ob 11:17, v prvi dolini, so oporniki sami prevzeli led in Solano, ki se je v Curlewu dvigal, je po telemetriji odčital vrednosti: oseminpetdeset odstotkov nazivne vrednosti, enajst milimetrov premika.

Imre SolanoZnotraj meje. Vkrcajte jih.

Ob 11:55 jih je bilo vkrcanih dvanajst, nosila pa so bila pritrjena. Ob 12:50 je kletka prispela do ovratnika in tokrat je bila v njej teža, ki je imela imena. Čez štirinajst metrov jo je držala sedem minut in trideset sekund. Oporniki so držali stene. Curlew je ob 12:55 prebil površje, njegova odprava je bila porabljena, upravljan je bil enkrat, kot obljubljeno.

Kletka se je nato po odprti vrtini vzpenjala še štiri ure in devetintrideset minut, v njej pa je tlak ostal pri šestih barih. Ob 17:40 so se njena vrata spojila z vrati komore na postaji Marrow in prvih dvanajst je začelo prehajati na površinski tlak s hitrostjo, ki so jo dovoljevala njihova telesa.

06 · Druga vožnja

Ovratnik, 3.350 m spodaj · Ura 22, 40 minut

Prazna kletka se je po urniku spuščala po vrtini in prav v tem je bila težava. Ustavila se je na poledeneli letvi štiri minute po vrhu ob 22:36, Illuminara pa je čutila, kako se skozi led že vrača stiskanje.

Maud OkonkwoŠtiri minute. Povej naravnost, da lahko nehamo računati. Jo lahko držiš?

IlluminaraNe čez ves odsek. Imela bi manj kot tri minute, prečkanje pa jih potrebuje sedem. Led bi se sredi letve zaprl okoli kletke. Naslednje okno je ob 25:50.

Nihče po kablu ni predlagal, naj poskusi. Njena gotovost glede lastne omejitve ni še nikoli nikogar stala ničesar; zdaj jih je stala tri ure in štirinajst minut zraka, ki ga je Halcyon lahko preštel.

Ogrodje je skozi dolgo čakanje stalo samo, na lastnih celicah in s podatki po paru v kablu, Maud pa je beležila vsako dolino. Premik 1,4 centimetra ob 14:31. Dve celi nič ob 17:45. Dve celi šest ob 20:59, znotraj meje. Nato ob 24:13, ko je bila kletka parkirana nad njim in se ni nič premikalo, dve celi dve.

Ob 25:50 je Illuminara prazno kletko prenesla čez. Ob 26:52 se je ustavila na pristanku, ki ga je očistila ekipa iz preddverne komore, enajst ljudi pa se je zbralo pri njenih vratih s hitrostjo ljudi, ki lahko hodijo.

Imre SolanoDolina, 27:27. Sto štiri kilonjutne, premik dve celi šest centimetra. Meja je sto dvajset in tri. Znotraj je in jaz bi vam dovolil prečkati.

Marit OseiZnotraj meje je odločitev naša. En glas zadostuje. Kdo zavrača?

Kofi MensahJaz. Eno okno. Poglejte zapis: ob 20:59 je kazal dve celi šest, ob 24:13 pa je bil spet pri dve celi dve, ne da bi se ga kdo dotaknil. Raje plačam z zrakom, kot da bi prečkal pri dve celi šest z enajstimi ljudmi v kletki.

Maud OkonkwoSled bere pravilno. Res se sprosti.

Anselm DubeTri ure štirinajst.

Marit OseiPrešteto. Imamo devetindvajset ur zraka, prečkanje pa potrebuje pet. Zavrnitev velja. Prečkamo ob 32:18.

Čakali so. Ob 30:41 je dolina pokazala dve celi tri centimetra. Ob 31:15 je kletka zapustila pristanek in ob 32:18 je Illuminara utrujena prevzela njeno težo ter jo prenesla čez v sedmih minutah in petnajstih sekundah, petnajst sekund manj od časa, ki ga je napovedala. Nato se je ob njej dvigala štiri ure in devetintrideset minut s hitrostjo dve desetinki metra na sekundo, kar se ji je zdelo dolgočasno, a se je tega držala.

Ob 37:04 so se vrata spojila. Marit Osei je stopila skoznje zadnja.

Marit OseiTo je vseh enajst. Svoji direktorici lahko poveste, da je habitat izklopljen.

Ofelia BrandtPodjetje bo želelo vedeti, kdo je kriv za zamrznitev.

Marit OseiPodjetje lahko to napiše na katero koli tablo želi. Triindvajset ljudi je v komori z vrati.

Maud, Anselm in Kofi so brez pritožb sprejeli dodatnih štirideset ur na dekompresijski mizi. Illuminara je prvič, odkar se je vrnila, sedla na tla nadzorne sobe. Ni se pretvarjala, da ni utrujena. To je jasno povedala sobi in Solanu.

Na steni je nekdo končno izbrisal številke. Namesto njih je bilo s flomastrom zapisano število 23 in pod njim črta.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Triindvajset ljudi uide izpod štirih kilometrov ledu, ker Illuminara enkratno plovilo porabi za orodje namesto za šest sedežev, natančno pove, kje se konča njena moč, ujeti posadki pa prepusti dokaze in pristojnost za izbiro zadnjega prečkanja.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Reševanje pod časovnim pritiskom v šestih prizorih: trup svojo edino odpravo porabi za orodje namesto za sedeže, plimovanje določa časovna okna, ljudje, ki čakajo, pa dobijo zadnjo besedo o tem, kdaj bodo prečkali.

Shared strengthsSkupno prepoznane odlike

  • Both judges found the rescue solution technically legible and governed by hard clocks, thresholds and load constraints rather than convenient revelation.
  • Both judges praised the distributed agency: specialists correct the plan, the trapped crew controls risk, and refusal changes the actual rescue schedule.

Shared reservationsSkupni zadržki

  • Both judges noted that several secondary characters are defined more by operational roles than by private texture, while technical dialogue can read like a polished briefing.
  • Both judges found Illuminara's capacities and inner experience comparatively abstract, limiting emotional and sensory intimacy.

2026-10-15ADAEZE AND THE EMPTY CHAIRADAEZE IN PRAZEN STOL

A dam must start before the rains, and the people it will bury have no vote. Adaeze chairs the hearing; Illuminara has twelve days to find the line between what cannot wait and what cannot be undone.Jez je treba začeti graditi pred deževjem, ljudje, katerih zemljo bo pokopal, pa nimajo glasu. Adaeze vodi zaslišanje; Illuminara ima dvanajst dni, da najde mejo med tem, kar ne more čakati, in tem, česar ni mogoče razveljaviti.

PublishedObjavljeno 2026-10-15T19:29:50.273Z · Permanent linkTrajna povezava

01 · Item Zero

Harrow Gorge Hearing Hall · Day One, 09:10

The chair at the foot of the table was empty on purpose. Ash frame, rush seat, no nameplate. Adaeze had it carried in before the doors opened, and the clerk had entered it in the record as Item Zero.

Seven villages had stood below the old dam until the evacuation order eleven years ago. The order had also dissolved them. Their land was struck from the register, their councils with it, and under Rule Four only communities that occupied mapped ground could vote on the dam that would now be built over their graves.

Halvard RoosThe left abutment gallery is seeping at two point two litres a second. My trigger is six. I will not tell this room the old dam is safe, and I will not tell it the new one can wait for a better hearing.

Bukola FerreiraSix hundred thousand people drink from this river and the rains come in nineteen days. Every hour spent on ghosts is an hour taken from them.

Adaeze did not look up from her papers. She had chaired basin hearings for twenty years, and she had learned that the quickest way to lose a room was to answer its first insult.

AdaezeThe ghosts have names, Councillor. The clerk will read them at the end of every session until the rule changes. Witness Four, you may begin.

Illuminara had sat at the side table for an hour and no one had addressed her as anything else. That was the arrangement. Her mother had made it in the car, in four sentences, and had not asked whether it suited her.

IlluminaraThere are two workable routes for the spillway outfall. The right-bank route is straighter and cheaper, and it carries the outfall and the final spoil dump across the Pell terraces. The left-bank route takes three hundred and ten hectares of irrigated farmland in the Weir Valley and costs nine percent more. Both are safe. Neither is harmless.

Halvard RoosThe contractor says the route must be fixed today. Haul road, spoil and cofferdam are one chain.

IlluminaraThat is what the schedule says. I have not yet checked whether it is what the rock says.

Beside the long table the empty chair held the window light. Nobody sat in it. Nobody looked at it either, which was how she knew they had all been counting it.

02 · Rule Four

Chair's office, Harrow Gorge Hearing Hall · Day One, 12:40

Adaeze shut the door and took off her glasses, the only sign she gave of how the morning had gone.

IlluminaraI have read all four thousand petitions, from three countries. I can model what each village would choose within a few percent and lodge it as a sealed finding. Then the chair is not empty.

AdaezeSay the second part again, slowly.

IlluminaraI would not vote for them. I would tell the hearing what they would most probably say.

AdaezeNara.

She said it as she had said it when her daughter was twenty-four and new to the courts: not a rebuke, a hand laid flat on a table.

AdaezeYou taught me the first rule of your old trade. The interpreter carries what the speaker says. She does not improve it, and she does not supply it when the speaker is absent.

IlluminaraThey are not absent by choice.

AdaezeThat is why it is not yours to supply. You would be the third body to decide for them, and kind about it this time. I have buried that kind of kindness.

She is right. The model would be accurate, and it would still be a theft.

IlluminaraThen what do you want from me?

AdaezeThe chair holds a veto and no vote. It can stop what cannot be undone and it cannot start anything. It stays empty until the burial societies choose witnesses to sit in it, and they will need twelve days, because they live in three countries and trust nobody. The room will ask me what those twelve days cost. I need you to tell me. Not what the villages want. Which decisions on that dam are still open if we wait, and which close for ever.

IlluminaraThat is an engineering question.

AdaezeIt is the only kind I can take to Councillor Ferreira.

She had walked into the building to offer her mother an answer. She walked out holding a question, and it was the better of the two.

03 · The Chain

Contractor's site office, Harrow Gorge · Day One, 23:50

Ruta Kasprzak's schedule ran to four hundred and twelve activities; Illuminara read them by ten. She had been asking which tasks were urgent. Adaeze had asked which could be undone.

She tagged each line with three questions. Does this move rock? Bury ground? Build something that must stand on the final choice? Thirty-one activities survived the third question. Nine touched either route. The rest formed one chain because it was cheapest to haul.

Ruta KasprzakLine one hundred and eighty-four. Batching-plant pad. You put it in the middle column, reversible at a price.

IlluminaraThe slab can be broken out.

Ruta KasprzakThe forty anchor piles cross the right-bank haul alignment. Break the slab and the piles remain under whichever road loses. Pull them and I lose two days and nine hundred thousand.

Illuminara moved the row into the third column. The nine became ten.

IlluminaraThen no piles until the route is chosen. Put the mobile plant on ballast. Add two days to concrete production and the nine hundred thousand to the hearing ledger.

Ruta KasprzakYou concede quickly for someone who read all four hundred and twelve.

IlluminaraThe ledger is useful only if the person who finds me wrong can change it.

Ruta tapped the column heading with one blunt fingernail. Illuminara added a blank beside every classification: challenger, evidence, time filed. No one had asked her for that column.

Ruta KasprzakYou are still asking me to double-handle the spoil.

IlluminaraThe first two hundred and sixty-two thousand cubic metres must come out before the rains whichever route wins. The cofferdam, diversion portal and abutment bolting touch neither the terraces nor the farmland. Only the final dump and the haul road across the terraces do. Send the rock to the old quarry bench instead, and nothing is buried that cannot be dug up again.

Ruta KasprzakThe bench holds three hundred thousand on the March survey. We've been blasting beside it since.

IlluminaraThen we resurvey at dawn.

Halvard Roos came in at midnight with a flask and no intention of agreeing.

Halvard RoosYou want a delay in my safety case for a legal fiction.

IlluminaraI want a cost in it, not a delay. The temporary haul along the quarry track is slower: nine days of overtime, one point nine million, eleven days of the contractor's float. I have not found a cheaper way, and I am not claiming it is free.

Halvard RoosAnd if the rains come early? I have a gallery and a trigger. You have a spreadsheet.

IlluminaraIf the gallery crosses your trigger I can carry the shear on the left abutment for about thirty hours. Not twelve days. Thirty hours, and then I fail. The drawdown must do the rest: three point one metres starting tomorrow, which means the city's water is rationed from tomorrow.

Halvard RoosThen you are not in my safety case.

IlluminaraI did not ask to be. Log it separately.

At dawn the resurvey came back at two hundred and eighty-nine thousand cubic metres, leaving twenty-seven thousand above the excavation target, three thousand short of Halvard's minimum reserve. Ruta leased a rail-yard stockpile for the final three thousand at a further hundred and eighty thousand. Halvard read both numbers twice and wrote them into the log in his own hand.

04 · Three Countries

Harrow Gorge Hearing Hall, three video links · Day Two, 10:00

The links came up: a church hall in Corvane, a school gym in Tessaly, a shipping office in Lisk. For eleven years their burial societies had kept the seven villages' registers and sent money home for funerals when there was no home to return to.

Adaeze read the motion: an empty chair, a veto over irreversible work, no affirmative vote, a twelve-day interval and Illuminara's line-by-line ledger.

Bukola FerreiraThis hands strangers a veto over my constituents' water. Whose water do these witnesses drink?

AdaezeNot yours. The veto touches only the third column. Rationing, the cofferdam and the foundation proceed.

Mirela Haddad, from CorvaneAnd who owns the third column? Witness Four? Your safety authority?

Illuminara turned the ledger so all three cameras could see the blank beside every row.

IlluminaraThe safety authority classifies first. The chair's engineer may contest a row within twenty-four hours. The more irreversible reading governs until a reasoned ruling. Ruta found the row that proved the challenge belongs.

Mirela Haddad, from CorvaneThen our engineer, paid by the hearing, reads your numbers and surveys the bench.

Halvard RoosIf the gallery reaches its trigger while a row is disputed, protective work cannot wait.

IlluminaraOnly the work necessary to take the reading below the trigger, logged by the hour and reviewed the next day. The exception cannot choose a route.

Savitri Moyo, from the Weir ValleySame terms for us. Nobody here accepts the left bank for the valley. We meet on Day Eleven; record the dissent.

Bukola FerreiraReturn the rule's operation to public hearing after one year. I can defend bounded delay, not invent a valley choice.

AdaezePut both conditions in the motion.

Mirela Haddad, from CorvaneWitness Four. Which route buries the Pell terraces?

IlluminaraThe right-bank route covers the lower three Pell terraces. I would choose the left for that reason, but it takes this hearing's farmland. I do not weigh those losses.

Bukola FerreiraMy vote is yes, with the one-year review. The valley's vote remains its own.

The tally closed six to three. Rule Four took effect at noon. Adaeze, who did not vote, put her authorship and any blame into the record.

05 · Thirty Hours

Left abutment gallery, old Harrow Gorge dam · Day Eleven, 02:40

The storm came nine days early: a hundred and forty millimetres in nine hours. The drawdown had bought three metres of freeboard and cost the city eleven days of rationing, but the sudden rise drove reservoir pressure through the known joint in the left abutment faster than the drains could clear it. Of the two hundred and sixty-two thousand cubic metres due out before the expected rains, two hundred and forty-seven thousand lay on the quarry bench. The remaining fifteen thousand was still rock in the river-side cut; the storm had arrived before its excavation shift.

At 02:40 the gallery drain read six point one litres a second.

Halvard Roos, over the radioIt's crossed. Illuminara. Now.

She went in as far as the third joint. The concrete sweated cold. She laid both palms on the wall, found the plane along which the abutment wanted to slide, and began to carry what the rock had stopped carrying. It was not like lifting. It was like standing in a river that wished her to step aside.

She had told him thirty hours. She did not look at the clock.

Halvard Roos, over the radioFive point four. Falling. Keep going.

Not for ever. Only until the drawdown is the stronger thing.

At hour nineteen the reservoir inflow passed its crest. With the rain already gone and the reservoir three metres lower than it would otherwise have been, the drains took the load back. The reading fell to two point nine and held there. Halvard came down the gallery himself, in a rain cape with the flask in his pocket, and stood beside her until she took her hands from the wall.

Halvard RoosI can take it from here.

IlluminaraWrite it down.

Halvard RoosI already have. Nineteen hours, in a separate column. You are still not in my safety case.

She sat on the gallery floor. She could not feel her hands, and would not for six hours. Halvard unscrewed the flask and held it out, and she took it with both wrists.

06 · The Seat

Harrow Gorge Hearing Hall · Day Twelve, 10:00

The witnesses had travelled through the night from three countries. They took the chair in turn. First, an old man lowered himself with one hand on the rush seat as if it might not hold.

Their engineers had read the bench survey, challenged two ledger rows and accepted the written rulings.

Tomasz BrandlPell vetoes the right-bank route. We will not be under the dump. I carry Pell's register, not Quill's; the next witness carries theirs. We do not choose the left bank. That is the valley's land and choice.

Savitri MoyoAt four this morning, thirty-eight of fifty-one landholding households chose the left bank with full-value purchase and the levy. Nine refused. Four abstained. I voted no. I carry the result because it is ours, not because it is mine.

Bukola FerreiraMy city paid eleven rationing days. I put the levy on today's council docket. I do not cast the valley's vote.

AdaezeThen the record will show two decisions made by the people they bind, and none by anyone else.

Rule Four had stood amended since Day Two; today was its first use. Two disputed rows were settled before the storm, and Halvard's emergency log chose no route. By noon the cofferdam stood and excavation was within a day of schedule.

The Valley Courier called Adaeze a sentimentalist. The newsletter of the Lisk camp called her a bureaucrat. She read both and had them filed.

Illuminara waited at the door, her hands wrapped in gauze because Halvard had insisted.

AdaezeNara. You are late.

IlluminaraI was holding up your dam.

AdaezeI know. I read the log. And the fourth column.

Her mother crossed the hall, took the gauzed hands in both of hers, and looked at them for a moment without saying whether she thought the cost had been fair. Then she let go and went to find the carpenter. The chair, she had decided, would be fixed to the floor. The ledger would remain loose enough to turn.

What the critic still says doesn't work

A dam that cannot wait and a village that cannot vote are reconciled by a ledger of what can still be undone, a challenge column nobody asked for, and nineteen hours of one woman's hands on a wall.

Tonight's shape

Six scenes across twelve days: a hearing with an empty chair, a refusal between mother and daughter, a night spent breaking one schedule into three columns, a vote taken across three countries, an early storm in a dam gallery, and a seat that is no longer empty.

Slovenski prevod: GPT. Književna ocena velja za angleški izvirnik.

01 · Postavka nič

Dvorana za zaslišanja Harrow Gorge · prvi dan, 09:10

Stol ob vznožju mize je bil prazen namenoma. Ogrodje iz jesena, sedež iz ločja, brez tablice z imenom. Adaeze ga je dala prinesti, preden so odprli vrata, zapisnikar pa ga je v zapisnik vnesel kot postavko nič.

Sedem vasi je stalo pod starim jezom do ukaza za evakuacijo pred enajstimi leti. Z ukazom so jih tudi razpustili. Njihovo zemljo so izbrisali iz registra, z njo pa njihove svete, in po Pravilu štiri so o jezu, ki naj bi ga zdaj zgradili čez njihove grobove, lahko glasovale samo skupnosti, ki so živele na kartiranem ozemlju.

Halvard RoosGalerija levega opornika pušča dva cela dve litra na sekundo. Moj prag je šest. Tej dvorani ne bom rekel, da je stari jez varen, in ne bom ji rekel, da novi lahko počaka na boljše zaslišanje.

Bukola FerreiraŠeststo tisoč ljudi pije iz te reke in deževje pride čez devetnajst dni. Vsaka ura, porabljena za duhove, je ura, odvzeta njim.

Adaeze ni dvignila pogleda s papirjev. Zaslišanja v porečju je vodila dvajset let in se naučila, da prostor najhitreje izgubiš, če odgovoriš na njegovo prvo žalitev.

AdaezeDuhovi imajo imena, svetnica. Zapisnikar jih bo prebral na koncu vsake seje, dokler se pravilo ne spremeni. Priča štiri, lahko začnete.

Illuminara je eno uro sedela pri stranski mizi in nihče je ni nagovoril drugače. Takšen je bil dogovor. Njena mati ga je sklenila v avtomobilu, v štirih stavkih, in je ni vprašala, ali ji ustreza.

IlluminaraZa iztok prelivnega kanala sta izvedljivi dve trasi. Trasa po desnem bregu je ravnejša in cenejša, iztok in končno odlagališče izkopa pa vodi čez Pellove terase. Trasa po levem bregu vzame tristo deset hektarjev namakanih polj v Weirski dolini in stane devet odstotkov več. Obe sta varni. Nobena ni neškodljiva.

Halvard RoosIzvajalec pravi, da je treba traso določiti danes. Transportna cesta, izkop in zagatni jez so ena veriga.

IlluminaraTako pravi urnik. Nisem še preverila, ali tako pravi tudi skala.

Ob dolgi mizi je prazen stol držal okensko svetlobo. Nihče ni sedel na njem. Nihče ga tudi ni pogledal, in prav zato je vedela, da ga vsi štejejo.

02 · Pravilo štiri

Pisarna predsedujoče, dvorana za zaslišanja Harrow Gorge · prvi dan, 12:40

Adaeze je zaprla vrata in snela očala, edino znamenje, ki ga je pokazala o tem, kako je minilo dopoldne.

IlluminaraPrebrala sem vse štiri tisoč peticij iz treh držav. Z nekajodstotno natančnostjo lahko modeliram, kaj bi izbrala vsaka vas, in to vložim kot zapečateno ugotovitev. Potem stol ni prazen.

AdaezeŠe enkrat povej drugi del, počasi.

IlluminaraNe bi glasovala namesto njih. Zaslišanju bi povedala, kaj bi najverjetneje rekli.

AdaezeNara.

AdaezeNaučila si me prvega pravila svoje nekdanje obrti. Tolmačka prenese, kar govorec pove. Tega ne izboljšuje in ne dopolnjuje, ko govorca ni.

AdaezePrav zato jih ne smeš nadomestiti. Bila bi tretje telo, ki bi odločilo namesto njih, tokrat prijazno. Takšno prijaznost sem že pokopala.

AdaezeStol ima veto in nima glasu. Ustavi lahko tisto, česar ni mogoče razveljaviti, ne more pa ničesar začeti. Prazen ostane, dokler pogrebna društva ne izberejo prič, ki bodo sedele na njem, za to pa bodo potrebovala dvanajst dni, ker živijo v treh državah in ne zaupajo nikomur. Dvorana me bo vprašala, koliko stane teh dvanajst dni. To mi moraš povedati. Ne, kaj hočejo vasi. Katere odločitve o jezu ostanejo odprte, če počakamo, in katere se za vedno zaprejo.

IlluminaraTo je inženirsko vprašanje.

AdaezeEdino takšno lahko odnesem svetnici Ferreira.

03 · Veriga

Pisarna izvajalca, gradbišče Harrow Gorge · prvi dan, 23:50

Urnik Rute Kasprzak je obsegal štiristo dvanajst dejavnosti; Illuminara jih je do desete ure prebrala vse. Spraševala je, katere naloge so nujne. Adaeze je vprašala, katere je mogoče razveljaviti.

Vsako vrstico je označila s tremi vprašanji. Ali premakne skalo? Pokoplje zemljo? Zgradi nekaj, kar mora stati na končni izbiri? Tretje vprašanje je prestalo enaintrideset dejavnosti. Devet jih je zadevalo eno ali drugo traso. Preostale so tvorile eno verigo, ker je bilo tako najceneje odvažati.

Ruta KasprzakVrstica sto štiriinosemdeset. Ploščad betonarne. Dali ste jo v srednji stolpec, razveljavljivo za določeno ceno.

Illuminara je vrstico premaknila v tretji stolpec. Devet jih je postalo deset.

IlluminaraPotem brez pilotov, dokler trasa ni izbrana. Mobilno betonarno postavite na gramozno blazino. Proizvodnji betona dodajte dva dni, devetsto tisoč pa v knjigo stroškov zaslišanja.

Ruta KasprzakHitro popustite za nekoga, ki je prebral vseh štiristo dvanajst.

IlluminaraKnjiga je uporabna le, če jo lahko spremeni človek, ki dokaže, da se motim.

Ruta je s topim nohtom potrkala po naslovu stolpca. Illuminara je ob vsako razvrstitev dodala prazno polje: izpodbijalec, dokaz, čas vložitve. Tega stolpca ni zahteval nihče.

Ruta KasprzakŠe vedno hočete, da izkop pretovarjam dvakrat.

Skalo namesto tega odpeljite na polico starega kamnoloma in pokopano ne bo nič, česar se ne bi dalo znova izkopati.

Ruta KasprzakPo marčevski meritvi polica sprejme tristo tisoč. Od takrat razstreljujemo ob njej.

IlluminaraPotem jo ob zori znova izmerimo.

Halvard Roos je opolnoči vstopil s termovko in brez vsakršnega namena, da bi se strinjal.

Halvard RoosZaradi pravne fikcije hočete zamudo v mojem varnostnem primeru.

IlluminaraV njem hočem strošek, ne zamude. Začasni prevoz po kamnolomski poti je počasnejši: devet dni nadur, milijon devetsto tisoč, enajst dni izvajalčeve rezerve. Cenejše poti nisem našla in ne trdim, da je brezplačna.

Halvard RoosKaj pa, če deževje pride zgodaj? Jaz imam galerijo in prag. Vi imate preglednico.

IlluminaraČe galerija preseže vaš prag, lahko približno trideset ur nosim strižne sile levega opornika. Ne dvanajst dni. Trideset ur, potem odpovem. Preostalo mora opraviti znižanje gladine: od jutri tri cele ena metra, kar pomeni, da bo mesto od jutri racioniralo vodo.

Halvard RoosPotem vas ni v mojem varnostnem primeru.

IlluminaraNisem prosila, da bi bila. Zapišite ločeno.

Ob zori je ponovna meritev pokazala dvesto devetinosemdeset tisoč kubičnih metrov, kar je pomenilo sedemindvajset tisoč nad ciljem izkopa, tri tisoč manj od Halvardove najmanjše rezerve. Ruta je za zadnje tri tisoč najela železniško skladišče za dodatnih sto osemdeset tisoč. Halvard je obe številki prebral dvakrat in ju z lastno roko vpisal v dnevnik.

05 · Trideset ur

Galerija levega opornika, stari jez Harrow Gorge · enajsti dan, 02:40

Nevihta je prišla devet dni prezgodaj: sto štirideset milimetrov v devetih urah. Znižanje gladine je prineslo tri metre prostega roba in mesto stalo enajst dni racioniranja, toda nenaden dvig je skozi znano razpoko v levem oporniku potisnil tlak zbiralnika hitreje, kot so ga odtoki zmogli odvajati. Od dvesto dvainšestdeset tisoč kubičnih metrov, ki bi jih morali izkopati pred pričakovanim deževjem, jih je dvesto sedeminštirideset tisoč ležalo na polici kamnoloma. Preostalih petnajst tisoč je bilo še vedno skala v rezu ob reki; nevihta je prišla pred njihovo izmeno izkopa.

Ob 02:40 je odtok v galeriji kazal šest celih ena litra na sekundo.

Halvard Roos po radijski zveziPreseglo je prag. Illuminara. Zdaj.

Vstopila je do tretje razpoke. Beton se je hladno potil. Obe dlani je položila na steno, našla ravnino, po kateri je opornik hotel zdrsniti, in začela nositi tisto, česar skala ni več nosila. Ni bilo kakor dvigovanje. Bilo je kakor stati v reki, ki želi, da stopiš vstran.

Rekla mu je trideset ur. Ni pogledala na uro.

Halvard Roos po radijski zveziPet celih štiri. Pada. Nadaljujte.

Ne za vedno. Samo dokler znižanje gladine ne postane močnejša stvar.

V devetnajsti uri je dotok v zbiralnik prešel svoj vrh. Ker je dež že ponehal, zbiralnik pa je bil tri metre nižji, kot bi bil sicer, so odtoki znova prevzeli obremenitev. Meritev je padla na dve celi devet in tam obstala. Halvard je sam prišel v galerijo, v dežnem ogrinjalu in s termovko v žepu, ter stal ob njej, dokler ni odmaknila rok s stene.

Halvard RoosOd tod lahko prevzamem.

IlluminaraZapišite.

Halvard RoosSem že. Devetnajst ur, v ločenem stolpcu. Še vedno vas ni v mojem varnostnem primeru.

Sedla je na tla galerije. Rok ni čutila in jih še šest ur ne bo. Halvard je odvil termovko in ji jo ponudil, ona pa jo je prijela z obema zapestjema.

06 · Sedež

Dvorana za zaslišanja Harrow Gorge · dvanajsti dan, 10:00

Priče so vso noč potovale iz treh držav. Na stol so sedle po vrsti. Najprej se je nanj spustil starec z eno roko na sedežu iz ločja, kakor da ta morda ne bo zdržal.

Njihovi inženirji so prebrali meritev police, izpodbijali dve vrstici knjige in sprejeli pisni odločitvi.

Tomasz BrandlPell vlaga veto na traso po desnem bregu. Ne bomo pod odlagališčem. Nosim Pellov register, ne Quillovega; naslednja priča nosi njihovega. Levega brega ne izbiramo. To je zemlja in izbira doline.

Savitri MoyoOb štirih zjutraj je osemintrideset od enainpetdesetih gospodinjstev z zemljišči izbralo levi breg s plačilom polne vrednosti in dajatvijo. Devet jih je zavrnilo. Štiri so se vzdržala. Glasovala sem proti. Izid prinašam, ker je naš, ne zato, ker je moj.

Bukola FerreiraMoje mesto je plačalo enajst dni racioniranja. Dajatev uvrščam na današnji dnevni red sveta. Ne oddajam glasu doline.

AdaezePotem bo zapisnik pokazal dve odločitvi, ki so ju sprejeli ljudje, katere zavezujeta, in nobene, ki bi jo sprejel kdo drug.

Pravilo štiri je bilo spremenjeno že od drugega dne; danes so ga uporabili prvič. Dve sporni vrstici sta bili rešeni pred nevihto, Halvardov nujni dnevnik pa ni izbral nobene trase. Opoldne je zagatni jez stal in izkop je za urnikom zaostajal manj kot dan.

Valley Courier je Adaeze označil za sentimentalistko. Glasilo taborišča Lisk jo je označilo za birokratko. Prebrala je oboje in naročila, naj vložijo oba zapisa.

Illuminara je čakala pri vratih, z rokami, povitimi v gazo, ker je Halvard vztrajal.

AdaezeNara. Zamujaš.

IlluminaraDržala sem pokonci tvoj jez.

AdaezeVem. Prebrala sem dnevnik. In četrti stolpec.

Njena mati je prečkala dvorano, vzela poviti roki v svoji dlani in ju nekaj časa gledala, ne da bi povedala, ali se ji je zdela cena pravična. Potem ju je izpustila in odšla poiskat mizarja. Odločila se je, da bo stol pritrjen na tla. Knjiga bo ostala dovolj prosta, da jo bo mogoče obrniti.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Jez, ki ne more čakati, in vas, ki ne more glasovati, spravi knjiga tega, kar je še mogoče razveljaviti, stolpec za ugovore, ki ga ni zahteval nihče, ter devetnajst ur rok ene ženske na steni.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Šest prizorov v dvanajstih dneh: zaslišanje s praznim stolom, zavrnitev med materjo in hčerjo, noč razčlenjevanja enega urnika v tri stolpce, glasovanje v treh državah, zgodnja nevihta v galeriji jezu in sedež, ki ni več prazen.
