2026-10-14THE WORD THAT HAD NO SECOND SPEAKERBESEDA, KI NI IMELA DRUGEGA GOVORCA

A three-hundred-year-old treaty turns on one word that a mining company reads as lease and a valley reads as lend, and only one living person speaks the language. Illuminara has nine days, a court that wants a translator, and a witness who will not be made into a key.Tristo let stara pogodba je odvisna od ene besede, ki jo rudarska družba bere kot najem, dolina pa kot posojo, jezik pa govori le še en živ človek. Illuminara ima devet dni, sodišče, ki hoče prevajalca, in pričo, ki se noče pustiti spremeniti v ključ.

PublishedObjavljeno 2026-10-14T19:20:25.416Z · Permanent linkTrajna povezava

01 · Eighty-nine

Kessel Reach tribunal, hearing room two · Day One, 08:10

The Compact of the Seven Springs ran to four pages, and the whole of it turned on a single word of the Irú language. Clause Four gave Ridge Nine oruma to Harrow & Vane Minerals for the winning of ore, the springs to be rendered whole. The company read the word as lease. The people of the Irú valley read it as lend. Between the two readings lay a ridge, the aquifer under the ridge, and seven thousand people who drank from it.

The tribunal had nine days; the company’s interim licence to blast expired at 08:00 on the tenth. No dictionary could help. The last Irú school had burned with its books, and the last person who spoke the language with the work still in it sat in the front row under a grey blanket. Ilo Marrow was eighty-nine. He had been asked to testify six times and had refused six times.

Pell Ardent, the court’s translator, stood by the bench with a folder of field recordings and the look of a woman who had not slept.

Pell ArdentCounsel for the company telephoned me at home last night. She said a reading of lease would be very much in keeping with the recordings. I said I would read what they said. She said she was sure I would.

IlluminaraDid you hear a threat?

Pell ArdentI heard someone checking how much weight I could carry. If I am the only voice, I will be the weight.

She knew what the bench wanted from her, and it was a second voice. One speaker could be doubted. Two would be a record. There was no second.

She went down the aisle and knelt by Ilo’s chair so that he would not have to look up.

IlluminaraI can interpret for you. I have done it in courts. I would not change a word.

Ilo MarrowYou are kind, and you would make me a quotation. I would speak, you would speak, and the court would keep yours.

IlluminaraThen I will not ask what the word means.

His eyebrows rose a finger’s width.

IlluminaraI will ask what a person does after hearing it.

Ilo looked at his hands for a long time, the hands of a man who had lent a great many things.

Ilo MarrowThat is a question for a seed. I will speak to objects, girl. I will not speak to you about them.

Wren Odira, who kept the court’s clock because nobody else would, wrote 08:41 in her notebook and beside it, objects only. Under it she ruled a line and wrote 216 h from 08:00.

02 · What a Person Does Next

Three hamlets of the Irú valley · Days Two and Three

The language was nearly gone, but the practice had not died with it. Illuminara went to three people who lived a day’s walk apart, had never compared notes, and spoke only the common tongue: Teodora Brisk at Upper Haddow, whose cress and barley trays went from house to house each spring; Hamid Sorel, warden of the reservoir boats; and Anke Doyle, who lent the names of dead grandmothers to newborns and took them back.

She asked each the same question in the same words. What does a person do after you say oruma?

Teodora BriskYou take the measure in March. In May you bring it back with a handful over. And you say what failed.

Hamid SorelThe boat goes out leaking. It comes home caulked, with your name cut in the new plank. Nobody hands back a boat the way they found it. That is how I know they used it.

Anke DoyleThe name comes back with a line on the board. What the child did with it.

Use, change, account. Illuminara set the shape aside as a hypothesis. These three had described the practice her columns were drawn from, so they could not also be the people who proved the columns right.

At Hamid’s desk she stopped him turning a page. His boat-book, Exhibit Fourteen, had been logged as a repair ledger and nobody had read it for a verb. The oldest entry was 1727, a year after the Compact.

Skiff, oruma to Aldous Sorel for the fishing, the plank to be cut and the boat to come home. Called home in the flood of 1731. It came.

She laid Clause Four beside it. The same frame stood in both: the word, a holder, a use, and a clause saying what must happen at the end. The ferry grant Ines Calder had put in evidence herself, oruma to the Crowes for the crossing, the log to be borne up each winter, carried it too. It had never been called back in two hundred and twelve years.

Wren OdiraIt fits the company’s second reading if you squint. Keep the thing, send a token. You told me to say when something fits.

IlluminaraIt fits mine if you do not squint. That does not make it proof. The frame shows the word came with an ending-clause. It does not show that the clause was a debt, or that anyone could call it early. For that we need a case nobody on my side has built.

Hamid SorelThe Crowes carry the ferry log up to the council hall every winter. That is what coming back looks like when the thing is a river.

03 · Three Columns

Tribunal annex · Day Four, 07:30

Illuminara spread one sheet across the annex table and drew two columns. LEND: use, then the thing returned changed, with an account, and the lender free to call it back. GRANT: the thing kept, a token sent home each year. She left the third column blank and turned the sheet to face Ines Calder.

IlluminaraWrite your best reading. Not the one that is easy to beat. The one a careful lawyer would defend in her sleep.

Calder took the pen. She did not write the lease Illuminara had expected.

Ines CalderA lease allows wear. The tenant maintains, at her own cost, and keeps a written account of the days she used it. The thing comes back sound, and the lessor does not take it back before the term runs, for any reason, because that is what a term is. The springs are rendered whole when the term ends, and the account says how.

She headed the column LEASE and underlined any reason.

IlluminaraYour column and mine agree on the seed, the skiff and the name-board. A tenant mends the seam and cuts a line on the board too.

Ines CalderThen your objects cannot tell us anything.

IlluminaraThey can tell us whether the keepers recognize their own rules. They cannot tell us where we part, and we part in one place. Can the lender call it home before the term, if she has to?

Calder was silent for a breath. Then she did what Illuminara had not asked, and chose the case.

Ines CalderA sluice paddle from the reservoir office, lent for the term of the hearing. Mid-term, the lender calls it back. Twice: once because the water is rising, once for no reason at all. And the judge is not one of your three keepers, who have heard your table. Brann Crowe. His family has held the ferry for two hundred years, and he has no reason to love you.

Maren Tolliver, the company’s surveyor, who had walked Ridge Nine for eleven years, agreed to do the handing back. She asked one thing.

Maren TolliverWho decides whether I did it right?

IlluminaraNot Ilo. If this rests on one old man, it is a quotation again. Each keeper decides for the thing they keep, and Mr Crowe the paddle. If a judge cannot decide, it counts as unresolved, and it does not count for me.

At 07:52 she wrote her predictions on a card and Wren signed across the flap. The lender’s protocol would close both recalls; the tenant’s would close neither. Beneath them she wrote the line she wanted Calder to see: If Mr Crowe closes the tenant’s hold against a flood, I withdraw the claim of recall, and the company may blast Ridge Nine and answer for the springs in a ledger at the end of the term. If he closes it only against a whim, I narrow the claim to the flood. Everything on the ridge hung on that card. A spring that was fractured in the third year could not be rendered whole in the tenth.

At 09:15 Tolliver took the objects. Wren logged the minute she sowed two trays of cress, ridge soil and valley soil. Forty-eight hours, said Teodora, was a cress season.

04 · The Surveyor’s Hands

Upper Haddow seed house · Day Six, 10:00

A linen screen divided the seed house, with a slot cut low in it that showed only hands and the thing in them. On one side sat Teodora, Hamid and Anke at separate tables, each with a pencil and a card marked CLOSED and NOT CLOSED, and at a fourth table Brann Crowe, lean and unsmiling. On the other stood Maren Tolliver with her sleeves rolled, and Ilo Marrow beside a measure of seed, a skiff and a pine name-board scored with nine names. Forty-eight hours and forty-five minutes had passed since the sowing.

Each time, Ilo spoke a short phrase to the object in Irú, not to the woman, and Illuminara wrote down only the time. Tolliver did what the sheet in her hand said, and she did not know which protocol came next.

Under the tenant’s protocol the seed came back sound, measure for measure, and Teodora marked it CLOSED. Under the lender’s it came back with a handful over and a germination page, the ridge-soil tray grey with rot beside the green valley tray, and Teodora marked that CLOSED too. Under grant Tolliver kept the measure and sent home one seed, and Teodora did not turn her card at all. The skiff went the same way, and for the grant one nail that Hamid pushed back through the slot with a forefinger. At the name-board Tolliver cut a line of account into the pine, and Ilo spoke to the board for longer than the formula needed, quietly, in a voice that was not for the room. Illuminara wrote untranslated, Ilo to the board and nothing else.

Wren OdiraWhat did he say?

IlluminaraA man spoke to a board. I will not make it into something he said to me.

Anke wrote a question mark beside the grant. That was the one call the keepers could not make, and Illuminara entered it as unresolved.

At 11:30 the usher carried a notice through the slot, in Hamid’s hand. The water is rising. The gate needs its paddle. Under the lender’s protocol Tolliver returned the paddle with her log of days used, and Brann Crowe, who had said nothing since he sat down, marked it CLOSED. Under the tenant’s she said the term ran to Day Ten and offered the fee for the lost days. Crowe looked at the paddle a long moment and marked it NOT CLOSED.

At 12:10 the second notice came through. The lender wants it back. Nothing more. Tolliver returned the paddle at once, her hands slow with it. Crowe marked it NOT CLOSED. Under the tenant’s protocol she held the paddle to the term, and he marked it CLOSED.

Wren opened the envelope at one o’clock. Two of Illuminara’s four recall marks were wrong.

Ines CalderSo it is a lease. A lease holds against a whim.

Brann CroweIt holds against a whim. It does not hold against a flood. My people never called the ferry because it never stopped coming home with the log. If anyone had called it for no reason, I would have told them where to put the log.

Illuminara struck free to call it back from her own column and wrote above it, for need. She entered the miss as a miss. She had predicted that the lender could call the thing home. The case had said: for need, not for pleasure.

Maren TolliverMy hands found a difference, and not the one I expected. Holding the paddle to the term, I was doing business. Putting it down when the water rose, I was paying a debt I had not known I had. Held through a term, or returned for a need somebody shows you. That is all my hands could tell apart.

Ines CalderYou were asked to perform a word you do not speak.

Maren TolliverI was asked what a decent person does with a borrowed thing. Four people who have never met told me, and one of them was your witness. I did not need Irú for that.

05 · The Dry Shoulder

Kessel Reach tribunal, hearing room two · Day Nine, 15:20

On Day Seven the council had offered the company the Dry Shoulder, a bare saddle of rock south of the ridge, with ore a third thinner, lent on the same word and the same terms of account. Tolliver’s own 2019 cores from the saddle were already Exhibit Nine, and Calder had confirmed the grade by eight that night. Had she not, Wren’s notebook said, the tribunal would have ruled on the ridge alone at noon on Day Eight.

Judge Anselm Rhee read for forty minutes. He said a demonstration was not a dictionary, and that Ilo Marrow’s sentence to the board would stay in the record untranslated. He took the matter in three parts and said which was which. On the word, the 1727 boat-book, the 1794 ferry grant and Clause Four shared one frame: continuity was proved. On the obligation, the keepers’ practice and Mr Crowe’s marks showed a debt of account and a return changed by use. On recall, the single 1731 entry and Mr Crowe’s marks carried it for need and not for whim, a judgment under uncertainty, which the tribunal held.

Then he said what the holding was for. A lease without recall let Harrow & Vane blast through its term and answer for the springs in a ledger at the end, when Clause Four’s promise to render them whole would fall due on a fractured aquifer no ledger could mend. A loan recallable for need let the valley call the springs home while they could still be returned, and the lower spring had fallen to a third of its winter flow. The springs had called. Had Mr Crowe closed the tenant’s hold against the flood, the tribunal would have ordered the account alone, and the company would have blasted. Harrow & Vane’s claim to Ridge Nine reverted to the valley.

Ines CalderI have advised my client not to appeal. Our own surveyor’s hands are in the record, and I wrote our own column. We ask only that the account be annual and that recall for need be written out, so that no one on either side has to guess what the word means in a bad year.

At the Dry Shoulder four hundred mining jobs would never exist. Oren Basque, the council speaker, who had wanted the company gone from the whole ridge, signed with a hand that shook. The Vesper and Hale families lost the summer pasture on the saddle and left without a word.

The tribunal’s last order directed the company to fund a school. Ilo Marrow answered it from his chair, in the common tongue, before the judge had finished.

Ilo MarrowI will not be paid for it. I will lend it. Nine people, and each of them takes one thing, a seed or a boat or a name, and brings it back changed.

Pell Ardent read the order into the record without a folder. Maren Tolliver asked, from the back of the hall, whether she might be counted among the nine, and nobody laughed.

At 16:04, on the courthouse steps, Ilo put a measure of seed into the hands of the youngest of them, a girl of seven called Pia, and said the word to her, not to the seed. Pia answered in Irú, loudly and wrongly, and the old man laughed until he coughed.

Wren OdiraThat is a second speaker.

IlluminaraThat is a first one. He needs nine more, and then they need each other.

Wren wrote the time. The word had had one speaker for a long season, and by spring it would have ten.

What the critic still says doesn't work

A dying language is kept from becoming one man's prison when Illuminara turns a disputed word into an adversarial test that can fail, accepts the failure, and wins a narrower ruling that saves seven thousand people's water while leaving its speaker free to teach on his own terms.

Tonight's shape

Five scenes across nine days: a refusal that closes the easy road; three uses of the word and two old documents that carry it; a sealed table in which the company writes its own strongest reading; a blind handing back of three objects plus one fresh case chosen by the company and judged by a stranger to the table, which the table partly gets wrong; and a ruling that moves the mine, narrows the claim, and puts the word into children’s mouths.

Slovenski prevod: GPT. Književna ocena velja za angleški izvirnik.

01 · Devetinosemdeset

Sodišče Kessel Reach, razpravna dvorana dve · Prvi dan, 08:10

Dogovor o sedmih izvirih je obsegal štiri strani, ves pa je bil odvisen od ene same besede v jeziku Irú. Četrti člen je družbi Harrow & Vane Minerals dal Greben devet oruma za pridobivanje rude, izvire pa je bilo treba vrniti cele. Družba je besedo razumela kot najem. Ljudje iz doline Irú so jo razumeli kot posojo. Med obema razlagama so ležali greben, vodonosnik pod njim in sedem tisoč ljudi, ki so iz njega pili.

Sodišče je imelo devet dni; začasno dovoljenje družbe za razstreljevanje je poteklo ob 08:00 desetega dne. Noben slovar ni mogel pomagati. Zadnja šola Irú je zgorela skupaj s knjigami, zadnji človek, ki je govoril jezik z živim izročilom, pa je sedel v prvi vrsti pod sivo odejo. Ilo Marrow je imel devetinosemdeset let. Šestkrat so ga prosili, naj priča, in šestkrat je zavrnil.

Pell Ardent, sodna prevajalka, je stala ob sodni klopi z mapo terenskih posnetkov in videzom ženske, ki ni spala.

Pell ArdentOdvetnica družbe me je sinoči poklicala domov. Rekla je, da bi bila razlaga kot najem zelo skladna s posnetki. Rekla sem, da bom prebrala, kaj pravijo. Rekla je, da je prepričana, da bom.

IlluminaraSte slišali grožnjo?

Pell ArdentSlišala sem nekoga, ki je preverjal, koliko teže lahko nosim. Če sem edini glas, bom jaz ta teža.

Vedela je, kaj sodni senat hoče od nje, in to je bil drugi glas. V enega govorca je bilo mogoče podvomiti. Dva bi pomenila zapis. Drugega ni bilo.

Stopila je po hodniku in pokleknila ob Ilov stol, da mu ne bi bilo treba gledati navzgor.

IlluminaraLahko prevajam za vas. To sem že počela na sodiščih. Ne bi spremenila niti besede.

Ilo MarrowPrijazni ste in iz mene bi naredili navedek. Jaz bi spregovoril, vi bi spregovorili, sodišče pa bi ohranilo vaše besede.

IlluminaraPotem vas ne bom vprašala, kaj beseda pomeni.

Njegove obrvi so se dvignile za širino prsta.

IlluminaraVprašala bom, kaj človek naredi, potem ko jo sliši.

Ilo je dolgo gledal svoje roke, roke človeka, ki je posodil že zelo veliko stvari.

Ilo MarrowTo je vprašanje za seme. Govoril bom predmetom, dekle. O njih pa ne bom govoril vam.

Wren Odira, ki je skrbela za sodno uro, ker je ni hotel nihče drug, je v zvezek zapisala 08:41 in zraven samo predmeti. Pod tem je potegnila črto in zapisala 216 ur od 08:00.

02 · Kaj človek naredi zatem

Trije zaselki doline Irú · Drugi in tretji dan

Jezik je bil skoraj izginil, vendar praksa ni umrla z njim. Illuminara je obiskala tri ljudi, ki so živeli dan hoda narazen, nikoli niso primerjali zapiskov in so govorili le skupni jezik: Teodoro Brisk v Upper Haddowu, katere pladnji kreše in ječmena so vsako pomlad potovali od hiše do hiše; Hamida Sorela, čuvaja čolnov na zbiralniku; in Anke Doyle, ki je novorojenčkom posojala imena umrlih babic in jih jemala nazaj.

Vsakemu je z istimi besedami postavila isto vprašanje. Kaj človek naredi, potem ko mu rečete oruma?

Teodora BriskMarca vzameš mero. Maja jo prineseš nazaj s prgiščem več. In poveš, kaj ni uspelo.

Hamid SorelČoln odide, ko pušča. Domov se vrne zatesnjen, z vašim imenom, vrezanim v novo desko. Nihče ne vrne čolna takšnega, kot ga je našel. Tako vem, da ga je uporabljal.

Anke DoyleIme se vrne s črto na tabli. S tem, kar je otrok naredil z njim.

Uporabi, spremeni, položi račun. Illuminara je vzorec odložila kot hipotezo. Ti trije so opisali prakso, iz katere so izhajali njeni stolpci, zato niso mogli biti tudi ljudje, ki bi dokazali, da imajo stolpci prav.

Pri Hamidovi pisalni mizi mu je preprečila, da bi obrnil stran. Njegova ladijska knjiga, dokazni predmet štirinajst, je bila vpisana kot knjiga popravil in nihče je ni bral zaradi glagola. Najstarejši vpis je bil iz leta 1727, leto po Dogovoru.

Čoln, oruma Aldousu Sorelu za ribolov, desko je treba izrezati in čoln mora priti domov. Poklican domov v poplavi leta 1731. Prišel je.

Ob vpis je položila četrti člen. V obeh je stal isti okvir: beseda, imetnik, raba in določilo o tem, kaj se mora zgoditi na koncu. Tudi dovoljenje za trajekt, ki ga je Ines Calder sama predložila kot dokaz, oruma družini Crowe za prevoz čez reko, poleno je treba vsako zimo prinesti gor, ga je vsebovalo. V dvesto dvanajstih letih ga niso nikoli poklicali nazaj.

Wren OdiraČe zamižiš, ustreza drugi razlagi družbe. Obdrži stvar, vsako leto pošlji znamenje. Rekli ste mi, naj povem, ko kaj ustreza.

IlluminaraČe ne zamižite, ustreza moji. To še ni dokaz. Okvir kaže, da je besedo spremljalo zaključno določilo. Ne kaže pa, ali je bilo določilo dolg in ali ga je bilo mogoče odpoklicati predčasno. Za to potrebujemo primer, ki ga ni sestavil nihče na moji strani.

Hamid SorelDružina Crowe vsako zimo prinese trajektno poleno v občinsko dvorano. Tako je videti vrnitev domov, kadar je stvar reka.

03 · Trije stolpci

Sodni prizidek · Četrti dan, 07:30

Illuminara je čez mizo v prizidku razgrnila en list in narisala dva stolpca. POSOJA: uporaba, nato vrnitev spremenjene stvari z obračunom in posojilodajalčevo pravico, da jo pokliče nazaj. PODELITEV: stvar se obdrži, vsako leto pa se domov pošlje znamenje. Tretji stolpec je pustila prazen in list obrnila proti Ines Calder.

IlluminaraZapišite svojo najboljšo razlago. Ne tiste, ki jo je lahko premagati. Tisto, ki bi jo skrben pravnik zagovarjal v spanju.

Calder je vzela pero. Ni zapisala najema, ki ga je Illuminara pričakovala.

Ines CalderNajem dovoljuje obrabo. Najemnik vzdržuje stvar na lastne stroške in vodi pisni obračun dni, ko jo je uporabljal. Stvar se vrne cela, najemodajalec pa je pred iztekom roka iz nobenega razloga ne vzame nazaj, ker je to bistvo roka. Izviri se vrnejo celi, ko rok poteče, obračun pa pove, kako.

Stolpec je naslovila NAJEM in podčrtala iz nobenega razloga.

IlluminaraVaš stolpec in moj se ujemata pri semenu, čolnu in tabli z imeni. Tudi najemnik popravi šiv in vreže črto na tablo.

Ines CalderPotem nam vaši predmeti ne morejo povedati ničesar.

IlluminaraPovedo nam lahko, ali skrbniki prepoznajo lastna pravila. Ne morejo nam povedati, kje se razidemo, razidemo pa se na enem mestu. Ali lahko posojilodajalec stvar pred iztekom roka pokliče domov, če jo potrebuje?

Calder je za hip molčala. Nato je naredila nekaj, česar Illuminara ni zahtevala, in izbrala primer.

Ines CalderZaporna loputa iz urada zbiralnika, posojena za čas obravnave. Sredi roka jo posojilodajalec pokliče nazaj. Dvakrat: enkrat, ker voda narašča, drugič brez kakršnegakoli razloga. In sodnik ne bo eden od vaših treh skrbnikov, ki so slišali vašo tabelo. Brann Crowe. Njegova družina ima trajekt že dvesto let in nima razloga, da bi vas imela rada.

Maren Tolliver, geodetka družbe, ki je enajst let prehodila Greben devet, je pristala, da bo vračala stvari. Vprašala je samo eno.

Maren TolliverKdo odloči, ali sem ravnala prav?

IlluminaraNe Ilo. Če bo to slonelo na enem starcu, bo spet samo navedek. Vsak skrbnik odloči za stvar, za katero skrbi, gospod Crowe pa za zaporno loputo. Če sodnik ne more odločiti, se primer šteje za nerešenega in meni ne koristi.

Ob 07:52 je svoje napovedi zapisala na kartico, Wren pa se je podpisala čez zavihek. Posojilni protokol bi zaključil oba odpoklica; najemniški ne bi zaključil nobenega. Pod njiju je zapisala vrstico, za katero je hotela, da jo Calder vidi: Če gospod Crowe najemniško zadržanje potrdi tudi ob poplavi, umaknem zahtevek glede odpoklica, družba pa lahko razstreli Greben devet in za izvire odgovarja v knjigi ob koncu roka. Če ga potrdi le ob muhi, zahtevek zožim na poplavo. Vse na grebenu je bilo odvisno od te kartice. Izvira, ki bi bil poškodovan v tretjem letu, v desetem ne bi bilo mogoče vrniti celega.

Ob 09:15 je Tolliver prevzela predmete. Wren je zapisala minuto, ko je posejala dva pladnja kreše, enega v prsti z grebena in drugega v prsti iz doline. Oseminštirideset ur, je rekla Teodora, je ena sezona kreše.

04 · Roke geodetke

Semenska hiša v Upper Haddowu · Šesti dan, 10:00

Laneno platno je delilo semensko hišo, nizko v njem pa je bila izrezana reža, skozi katero so se videle samo roke in predmet v njih. Na eni strani so za ločenimi mizami sedeli Teodora, Hamid in Anke, vsak s svinčnikom ter karticama ZAKLJUČENO in NI ZAKLJUČENO, za četrto mizo pa suh in brez nasmeha Brann Crowe. Na drugi strani je stala Maren Tolliver z zavihanimi rokavi, ob meri semena, čolnu in borovi tabli z devetimi vrezanimi imeni pa Ilo Marrow. Od setve je minilo oseminštirideset ur in petinštirideset minut.

Ilo je vsakič v jeziku Irú povedal kratek stavek predmetu, ne ženski, Illuminara pa je zapisala le čas. Tolliver je naredila, kar je pisalo na listu v njeni roki, in ni vedela, kateri protokol sledi.

Po najemniškem protokolu se je seme vrnilo celo, mera za mero, Teodora pa je označila ZAKLJUČENO. Po posojilnem se je vrnilo s prgiščem več in s stranjo o kalitvi, pladenj s prstjo z grebena siv od gnilobe ob zelenem pladnju iz doline, Teodora pa je tudi tega označila ZAKLJUČENO. Pri podelitvi je Tolliver obdržala mero in domov poslala eno seme, Teodora pa kartice sploh ni obrnila. Enako se je zgodilo s čolnom, pri podelitvi pa se je vrnil en sam žebelj, ki ga je Hamid s kazalcem porinil nazaj skozi režo. Pri tabli z imeni je Tolliver v borovino vrezala vrstico obračuna, Ilo pa je tabli govoril dlje, kot je zahtevala formula, tiho, z glasom, ki ni bil namenjen prostoru. Illuminara je zapisala neprevedeno, Ilo tabli in nič drugega.

Wren OdiraKaj je rekel?

IlluminaraČlovek je govoril tabli. Iz tega ne bom naredila nečesa, kar je povedal meni.

Anke je ob podelitev zapisala vprašaj. To je bila edina odločitev, ki je skrbniki niso mogli sprejeti, in Illuminara jo je vpisala kot nerešeno.

Ob 11:30 je sodni sluga skozi režo podal obvestilo, napisano s Hamidovo roko. Voda narašča. Zapornica potrebuje svojo loputo. Po posojilnem protokolu je Tolliver vrnila loputo z dnevnikom dni uporabe, Brann Crowe, ki odkar je sedel, ni rekel ničesar, pa je označil ZAKLJUČENO. Po najemniškem je rekla, da rok traja do desetega dne, in ponudila plačilo za izgubljene dni. Crowe je dolgo gledal loputo in označil NI ZAKLJUČENO.

Ob 12:10 je skozi režo prišlo drugo obvestilo. Posojilodajalec jo hoče nazaj. Nič več. Tolliver je loputo takoj vrnila, njene roke pa so se ob njej premikale počasi. Crowe je označil NI ZAKLJUČENO. Po najemniškem protokolu jo je zadržala do izteka roka, on pa je označil ZAKLJUČENO.

Wren je ob enih odprla ovojnico. Dve od štirih Illuminarinih oznak odpoklica sta bili napačni.

Ines CalderTorej je najem. Najem vzdrži muho.

Brann CroweVzdrži muho. Ne vzdrži poplave. Moji ljudje trajekta nikoli niso poklicali nazaj, ker se je vedno vračal domov s polenom. Če bi ga kdo poklical brez razloga, bi mu povedal, kam naj si vtakne poleno.

Illuminara je iz svojega stolpca prečrtala pravico, da jo pokliče nazaj in nad to zapisala zaradi potrebe. Napako je vpisala kot napako. Napovedala je, da lahko posojilodajalec stvar pokliče domov. Primer je pokazal: zaradi potrebe, ne zaradi užitka.

Maren TolliverMoje roke so našle razliko, vendar ne tiste, ki sem jo pričakovala. Ko sem loputo držala do izteka roka, sem opravljala posel. Ko sem jo odložila, ker je voda narasla, sem plačevala dolg, za katerega nisem vedela, da ga imam. Zadržana do roka ali vrnjena zaradi potrebe, ki vam jo nekdo pokaže. Samo to so moje roke lahko ločile.

Ines CalderOd vas so zahtevali, da uprizorite besedo, ki je ne govorite.

Maren TolliverVprašali so me, kaj pošten človek naredi z izposojeno stvarjo. Štirje ljudje, ki se še nikoli niso srečali, so mi povedali, eden od njih pa je bil vaša priča. Za to nisem potrebovala jezika Irú.

05 · Suho rame

Sodišče Kessel Reach, razpravna dvorana dve · Deveti dan, 15:20

Sedmega dne je svet družbi ponudil Suho rame, golo skalno sedlo južno od grebena, z za tretjino revnejšo rudo, posojeno z isto besedo in enakimi pogoji obračuna. Lastni vrtalni vzorci Tolliver iz leta 2019 so že bili dokazni predmet devet, Calder pa je stopnjo potrdila do osme ure tistega večera. Če je ne bi, je pisalo v Wreninem zvezku, bi sodišče opoldne osmega dne razsodilo samo o grebenu.

Sodnik Anselm Rhee je bral štirideset minut. Rekel je, da prikaz ni slovar in da bo stavek Ila Marrowa tabli ostal v zapisniku nepreveden. Zadevo je razdelil na tri dele in povedal, kateri je kateri. Glede besede so imeli ladijska knjiga iz leta 1727, dovoljenje za trajekt iz leta 1794 in četrti člen enak okvir: kontinuiteta je bila dokazana. Glede obveznosti so praksa skrbnikov in oznake gospoda Crowa pokazale dolg obračuna in vrnitev stvari, spremenjene z uporabo. Glede odpoklica sta ga en sam vpis iz leta 1731 in oznake gospoda Crowa utemeljila zaradi potrebe, ne pa zaradi muhe; sodišče je sprejelo takšno razsodbo v negotovosti.

Nato je povedal, čemu je odločitev namenjena. Najem brez odpoklica je družbi Harrow & Vane dovoljeval razstreljevanje ves čas roka in obračun za izvire v knjigi ob koncu, ko bi obveznost iz četrtega člena, da se jih vrne cele, zapadla za poškodovan vodonosnik, ki ga ne bi mogla popraviti nobena knjiga. Posojilo, ki ga je mogoče odpoklicati zaradi potrebe, je dolini dovoljevalo, da izvire pokliče domov, dokler jih je še mogoče vrniti, spodnji izvir pa je padel na tretjino zimskega pretoka. Izviri so poklicali. Če bi gospod Crowe najemniško zadržanje potrdil tudi ob poplavi, bi sodišče odredilo le obračun, družba pa bi razstreljevala. Zahtevek družbe Harrow & Vane do Grebena devet se je vrnil dolini.

Ines CalderSvoji stranki sem svetovala, naj se ne pritoži. Roke naše geodetke so v zapisniku in sama sem zapisala naš stolpec. Prosimo le, naj bo obračun letni in odpoklic zaradi potrebe zapisan, da ne bo treba nikomur na nobeni strani ugibati, kaj beseda pomeni v slabem letu.

Na Suhem ramenu štiristo rudarskih delovnih mest nikoli ne bo nastalo. Oren Basque, govorec sveta, ki je hotel, da družba zapusti ves greben, je podpisal s tresočo se roko. Družini Vesper in Hale sta izgubili poletni pašnik na sedlu in odšli brez besede.

Z zadnjim sklepom je sodišče družbi naložilo financiranje šole. Ilo Marrow mu je odgovoril s svojega stola v skupnem jeziku, še preden je sodnik končal.

Ilo MarrowZa to ne bom plačan. Posodil ga bom. Devet ljudi, vsak vzame eno stvar, seme, čoln ali ime, in jo vrne spremenjeno.

Pell Ardent je sklep prebrala v zapisnik brez mape. Maren Tolliver je iz zadnjega dela dvorane vprašala, ali jo lahko štejejo med deveterico, in nihče se ni zasmejal.

Ob 16:04 je Ilo na sodnih stopnicah mero semena položil v roke najmlajši med njimi, sedemletni deklici Pii, in besedo izrekel njej, ne semenu. Pia je v jeziku Irú odgovorila glasno in napačno, starec pa se je smejal, dokler ni zakašljal.

Wren OdiraTo je drugi govorec.

IlluminaraTo je prvi. Potrebuje jih še devet, nato pa potrebujejo drug drugega.

Wren je zapisala čas. Beseda je dolgo imela enega govorca, do pomladi pa jih bo imela deset.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Umirajoči jezik ne postane ječa enega samega človeka, ko Illuminara sporno besedo spremeni v preizkus z nasprotno stranjo, ki lahko spodleti, sprejme neuspeh in doseže ožjo razsodbo, ki obvaruje vodo sedem tisoč ljudi, govorec pa ostane svoboden, da uči pod svojimi pogoji.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Pet prizorov v devetih dneh: zavrnitev, ki zapre lahko pot; tri rabe besede in dve stari listini, ki jo vsebujeta; zapečatena tabela, v kateri družba zapiše svojo najmočnejšo razlago; slepa vrnitev treh predmetov in še en nov primer, ki ga izbere družba, presodi pa človek zunaj omizja, pri čemer se tabela deloma zmoti; ter razsodba, ki premakne rudnik, zoži zahtevek in položi besedo v otroška usta.

01 · Unclaimed Property

Harbour Central lost-and-found office · Tuesday, 04:52

The office had been built to hold forgotten umbrellas. By dawn it held a violin without a scratch, a wedding ring on a square of wet velvet, a red child's mitten the size of a plum, and four hundred other things that had arrived since midnight with nobody carrying them.

Illuminara walked the length of the counter without touching anything. Above the shelves the air was faintly warm, in the shape of the place on a doorknob where a hand has just let go. On the glass of the service window it had left a single oval thumbprint, still fading.

Teodor Pell, who had kept the office's ledgers for thirty-one years, stood behind the counter in yesterday's cardigan.

TeodorMrs. Marren's ring came back to her kitchen table at midnight. She threw it into the harbour. At two it was on her pillow. At four she carried it here in a sock and told me that if I did not stop my office, she would.

Wren Odira had come at his call. She carried a paper notebook, because Teodor did not allow screens near his ledgers, and she read from it without looking up.

WrenEleven houses so far. Nine are lost things going home. Two are not. Pavle Hadžić, nine, woke up holding his grandmother's sewing scissors. She had only set them down on the table to answer the phone.

IlluminaraSet down.

WrenYes. Not lost. Set down.

Not a search, Illuminara thought. A reflex.

Every object on the shelves wore a small oblong tag of brown card, tied on with thread and stamped with a number in a typeface she half recognised. No two numbers were alike, and she could find no order in them.

The door opened. Magistrate Ines Calloway, a tall woman with rain on her shoulders, laid a sealed order on the counter and kept one hand on it.

Ines CallowayAt nine this office closes and everything in it is sealed for the court. A three-year-old girl was sitting on Platform Two at 03:40, eleven kilometres from the farm where she fell asleep. She was cold and unhurt. Next time she may be neither.

IlluminaraI agree it must stop. If you seal the office, it will not stop. The warmth leans toward this wall, and past it, across the square, toward the hospital's patient-property cupboard. Close this room and you carry the problem to a place where people are even less able to refuse it.

Ines CallowayThen you have until nine to show me a better answer.

Wren wrote down the time. Teodor, who had loved the office longer than he had loved anyone, said nothing. His hand shook, so he laid it flat on the counter, as though to settle it.

02 · What It Understands

The same office · 05:40

Three owners were found and three objects chosen, because each had come to the office by a different road.

Ilse Marren's ring had gone into the harbour at her husband's funeral, on purpose, from her own hand. Ferenc Adler had owned his violin for fifty years, until he gave it to his student Dunja Roth; last week a fire in Dunja's flat had burned it black, and tonight it lay on the counter without a mark. The red mitten belonged to Marta Voss, who had lost it in a snowy park at the age of six and was now a baker with flour in her eyebrows.

Illuminara wrote a prediction on a card before anyone moved, folded it and gave it to Wren. She expected the visitor to follow the last hand that had held each object.

IlluminaraIf I am right, the ring goes to Mrs. Marren's house, the violin to Dunja and the mitten to Marta. If any of them goes elsewhere, I am wrong, and you will have it in my handwriting.

They set the three objects on the counter and cleared the room. Wren watched through the glass of the inner door, counting. At seventy-one seconds the warmth over the shelves stirred, and the counter was empty.

In the corridor, the ring was in Ilse's coat pocket and the mitten was in Marta's hand. The violin was in the arms of Ferenc Adler, who had not asked for it and held it like a man handed a debt.

IlluminaraWrong. It did not follow the last hand. It followed the first.

FerencI gave it to her. Before forty people. It is hers.

The warmth did not answer him. Dunja carried the violin back to the counter and left it there; eleven seconds later it was in Ferenc's arms again.

They tried what people try. Ferenc wrote a deed of gift and signed it, and the deed lay on the counter while the violin went home to him. Dunja laid a coin beside it as a price, and the coin went back to her pocket and the violin to Ferenc. Teodor, in disbelief, offered the empty air a receipt.

TeodorIt has returned my lunch to the bakery that sold it. I want that on record. My lunch was not lost.

Wren's mouth twitched. Marta Voss, still pressing her mitten against her cheek, laughed once, wetly. It was not unkind. The thing was so earnest.

DunjaIt is trying so hard to be good to him. And it is taking away the one thing I have left to play.

Illuminara watched the thumbprint cool on the window. I have been asking whether it understands who owns the violin. It may have no use for the word. What does it understand, that makes a gift look like a theft?

03 · A Century of Halves

Cloakroom archive, station basement · 06:37

She carried one of the brown tags downstairs and set it under Teodor's reading lamp.

IlluminaraEvery tag has a number, but I can find no list that holds them. Is there anything in this building that numbers things and keeps no names?

Teodor looked at the tag for a long time, and then at her, and then he took a brass key from his cardigan and opened a cabinet she had walked past twice.

Inside were ledgers, row on row, and in the ledgers, pressed flat, tens of thousands of half-tickets in the same typeface. For a century the station cloakroom had torn each ticket in two. One half was pinned to the coat; the other went home in the owner's pocket. The coat was finished only when the halves met.

TeodorForty-one thousand of them never came back. People lost the half. People died. We kept every stub, because the cloakroom's rule was that an unmatched half is still a promise.

WrenSame typeface. Same number series, continuing. It isn't counting what is lost. It is counting what is unmatched.

Illuminara felt the shape of it settle, the way a proof does when the missing line turns out to have been written on the margin all along.

IlluminaraIt grew up in this room. Everything it knows about owning, it learned from a counter where giving back and taking home are the same act, done with two halves of one ticket. It cannot read a name. It cannot weigh a price. It understands one sign of an ending: halves that meet.

DunjaAnd the violin, in my hands. Was that ever an ending to it?

IlluminaraNo. You held only the coat. You never held a half.

The cheapest test that could prove her wrong was also the cheapest to run. Teodor issued one fresh numbered pair from the old pad. Ferenc took one half and Dunja the other. They pressed their thumbs to the card, then brought the torn edges together until paper fibres met along the whole cut. Teodor marked the reunited pair GIVEN and laid both halves in the ledger drawer. The number alone was not the sign; the physical meeting of its two halves was.

The violin sat on the counter for ninety seconds, then ten minutes, then half an hour. On the service window a new thumbprint formed beside the old one and held, warm as a nod.

Ilse Marren, who had said nothing since the corridor, came to the counter.

IlseI will not put my name in a book. I put that ring in the harbour so that nobody would ever look at it again.

IlluminaraNo name. Teodor issues one numbered pair. You hold one half and the office holds the other as witness. To let the ring go, you bring the halves together and Teodor stores both under the number and the word RELEASED. If it takes the ring anyway, you have lost a morning and you may be as rude to me as you like.

At ten to eight Ilse walked to the end of Pier Nine with her half-ticket in one hand and the ring in the other. Teodor carried the office half on a small ledger board. Ilse touched the torn edge to its mate; when every paper fibre met, Teodor stamped the number RELEASED. Only then did she open her other hand over the water. The ring went down through the green light.

It did not come back. Ilse stood a long while with her empty palm. Her face did not look relieved; it looked like something being let go of.

04 · Nine O'Clock

East concourse, lost-and-found office · 09:00–09:50

Magistrate Calloway took the chair behind the counter. She read Illuminara's design standing up, one page of plain sentences, and then she read it a second time.

IlluminaraThree rules. Teodor issues one numbered pair for every act. To ask for a return, the holder keeps one half and the office keeps its mate apart as an open promise. To give or sell, the old and new holders meet the halves; Teodor stores the reunited pair marked GIVEN or SOLD. To let go, the holder meets one half with the other held by the office as witness; Teodor stores both marked RELEASED. Never a name. The court keeps every dispute. I do not ask the visitor to understand anything new. I ask it to keep doing what it already does, and to ask first.

Ines CallowayYou are proposing that the city regulate something it has never spoken to, by cloakroom ticket.

IlluminaraI am proposing we speak to it in the only language it has taught us. And I am proposing that it be allowed to say no.

She laid a torn ticket on the counter with the three rules written on the back, and beside it a second ticket left blank. If the visitor did not respond, she said, voluntary presentations would stop at nine-thirty. The office would remain open and exactly where it was. At the market and Platform Two, constables would issue guardian pairs to families with small children and have each pair's edges meet before anyone was set down, so a brief separation remained a completed relation. Court staff would mark a monitored perimeter, watch the hospital cupboard and handle no shelf, stub or object until the visitor answered or the court designed another non-moving test. The pause was the court's holding position, not a penalty for silence.

It was 09:02. The warmth over the counter drew taut.

A boy of four sat on the bench by the window, in a coat too thin for the morning, holding a paper windmill that had been in somebody's hand a moment before. He had been set down for two seconds in the morning market crowd, and the warmth had picked him up with the same patient courtesy it had shown the scissors.

He looked at the room, and began to wail.

TeodorOh, no. No, no, it's taking people as lost property.

Constable Aldo Brezar was already on the radio. Illuminara was kneeling in front of the bench with her hands open and empty. She did not touch the boy. She did not stop the warmth by force, though she could have; she thought of the hospital cupboard and let the thought go.

IlluminaraTeodor. Fetch his mother. Bring a ticket.

Lenka Vidmar came across the square at a run with flour on her sleeves from the fair stall. Teodor tore the ticket. She took one half; the boy, wailing, was given the other and curled his fist around it. Their thumbs met on the torn edge.

The warmth over the counter drew in on itself and released the route it had already opened. The boy remained on the bench where it had carried him, but now he leaned into his mother's knees and the warmth no longer held him as an unfinished return. He stopped crying in the middle of a breath.

Ines CallowayIt left him with her.

IlluminaraIt released a relation it had already moved. We have not yet proved that it will refrain from opening the next route.

The blank ticket on the counter had gone warm all along its torn edge. A thumbprint came up on the glass, slowly, then another beside it. Teodor said later that he had waited thirty-one years to see a visitor accept an invitation on a cardboard stub.

At 09:07 Wren began the prospective test. Teodor set his brass cloakroom key on the counter without opening a ticket or offering an office half. For two minutes—long past the earlier eleven and seventy-one seconds—the warmth leaned toward it, paused, and withdrew. The key remained exactly where he had set it.

Calloway took longer to speak than the room liked.

Ines CallowayThirty days. A public docket of numbers, never names. Any holder may refuse a ticket, and no object leaves a refusing holder without a court. And you will explain to me, on the record, what happens to the things it has already brought.

IlluminaraMost go home. Some will not. Mrs. Vuk's pawnshop holds a pocket watch the Rask family has wanted for twenty years. It was sold to her fairly. The visitor returned it to the Rasks this morning, and she has the right to refuse to join halves.

Mrs. Vuk, fetched from her shop in an apron, refused a transfer pair with the Rasks. She did accept a custody pair with Calloway's clerk: their edges met, the clerk marked the reunited halves DISPUTED, and the watch went into the court's safe to be heard on its own day. The warmth leaned toward the safe, held there longer than seventy-one seconds, and withdrew. The Rask daughter, who had held the watch for forty minutes that morning, wept in the corridor, and nobody told her that was only fair.

05 · Ask First

Harbour Central, lost-and-found office · 19:20

By evening the office had a new sign above the counter, lettered by Teodor in the old cloakroom hand. LOST AND FOUND. ASK FIRST.

Since 09:07 nothing had been taken. Eighty-three of the four hundred objects remained unresolved: eighty-two on the shelves, unclaimed or refused, and Mrs. Vuk's watch held separately in the court's safe. The rest had been matched with halves, one by one, or let go with a torn ticket and a quiet word at a pier. A queue ran down the concourse to the newsstand.

Marta Voss stood in it with her mitten in her pocket. She had come not to claim anything, but to leave a half-ticket for the mate she had lost in the same park, in case it was still anywhere to be found. A girl of seven in front of her left half of a ticket for a stuffed bear and said, with grave courtesy, to the empty air above the shelves:

GirlPlease, if it's not too much trouble. His name is Biscuit.

The warmth drew near, thought about it, and set a thumbprint on the glass beside her ticket. That was not a bear. It was a yes.

Illuminara stood by the inner door with Wren, watching the line shorten.

WrenYou could have stopped it by force, this morning. When the boy came.

IlluminaraYes. And then it would have been one more thing that decided without asking. I wanted it to see what asking is.

TeodorIt is not going to understand about the Rasks. Their watch. It is going to keep wanting to give it back.

IlluminaraIt may never understand. But it can be asked, and it can keep the invitation, and the court can carry what it cannot.

Behind the glass the oval thumbprint warmed and faded, warmed and faded, with the slow regularity of someone waiting. Illuminara did not press her own thumb to the window. She lifted her hand to the height of the print, nearly touching, and held it there.

It asked, she thought, and was answered. Now either of us may say no.

What the critic still says doesn't work

A lost-and-found office's torn-ticket grammar lets Illuminara ask a kindness that cannot tell a gift from a theft to stop, not by force but in its own language, and the city keeps the right to say no.

Tonight's shape

Five scenes: a dawn of unwanted returns and a court order that would only move the problem; a sealed three-object test that disproves Illuminara's first hypothesis and exposes a thing that cannot tell a gift from a theft; the discovery in the cloakroom archive that the visitor understands open promises and completed relations only through matched ticket halves; a consistent consent grammar tested on a gift, a deliberate release and a boy already carried into the hearing, with a safe monitored pause if the visitor refuses; and a trial opt-in exchange in which some beloved things stay gone and the visitor is asked, not commanded.

01 · Predmeti brez lastnika

Urad za najdene predmete na Glavni pristaniški postaji · torek, 04:52

Urad je bil zgrajen za hranjenje pozabljenih dežnikov. Do zore so bili v njem violina brez praske, poročni prstan na kvadratu mokrega žameta, rdeča otroška rokavica v velikosti slive in štiristo drugih reči, ki so prispele po polnoči, ne da bi jih kdo prinesel.

Illuminara je prehodila dolžino pulta, ne da bi se česarkoli dotaknila. Nad policami je bil zrak rahlo topel v obliki tistega mesta na kljuki, ki ga je roka pravkar izpustila. Na steklu okenca je pustil en sam ovalen odtis palca, ki je še vedno bledel.

Teodor Pell, ki je enaintrideset let vodil uradne knjige, je stal za pultom v včerajšnji jopi.

TeodorPrstan gospe Marren se je opolnoči vrnil na njeno kuhinjsko mizo. Vrgla ga je v pristanišče. Ob dveh je bil na njeni blazini. Ob štirih ga je v nogavici prinesla sem in mi povedala, da bo ustavila moj urad, če ga ne bom jaz.

Wren Odira je prišla na njegov klic. Nosila je papirnat zvezek, ker Teodor ob svojih knjigah ni dovolil zaslonov, in je brala iz njega, ne da bi dvignila pogled.

WrenZa zdaj enajst hiš. V devetih se izgubljene reči vračajo domov. V dveh ne. Devetletni Pavle Hadžić se je zbudil z babičinimi škarjami za šivanje v roki. Odložila jih je na mizo samo zato, da bi se oglasila na telefon.

IlluminaraOdložila.

WrenDa. Ne izgubila. Odložila.

Ne iskanje, je pomislila Illuminara. Refleks.

Vsak predmet na policah je nosil majhno podolgovato oznako iz rjavega kartona, privezano z nitjo in ožigosano s številko v pisavi, ki se ji je zdela napol znana. Nobeni dve številki nista bili enaki in v njih ni mogla najti nobenega reda.

Vrata so se odprla. Magistratinja Ines Calloway, visoka ženska z dežjem na ramenih, je položila zapečaten nalog na pult in obdržala eno roko na njem.

Ines CallowayOb devetih se ta urad zapre in vse v njem se zapečati za sodišče. Triletna deklica je ob 03:40 sedela na Drugem peronu, enajst kilometrov od kmetije, kjer je zaspala. Zeblo jo je, vendar ni bila poškodovana. Naslednjič morda ne bo tako.

IlluminaraStrinjam se, da se mora končati. Če zaprete urad, se ne bo. Toplota se nagiba proti tej steni in onkraj nje, čez trg, proti omari za lastnino pacientov v bolnišnici. Zaprite ta prostor in težavo boste prenesli tja, kjer jo ljudje še težje zavrnejo.

Ines CallowayPotem imate do devetih, da mi pokažete boljši odgovor.

Wren je zapisala čas. Teodor, ki je urad ljubil dlje, kot je ljubil kogarkoli, ni rekel ničesar. Roka se mu je tresla, zato jo je položil plosko na pult, kakor da bi jo hotel umiriti.

02 · Kaj razume

Isti urad · 05:40

Našli so tri lastnike in izbrali tri predmete, saj je vsak prišel v urad po drugi poti.

Prstan Ilse Marren je ob moževem pogrebu namenoma iz njene lastne roke padel v pristanišče. Ferenc Adler je imel svojo violino petdeset let, dokler je ni podaril učenki Dunji Roth; prejšnji teden jo je požar v Dunjinem stanovanju ožgal do črnega, nocoj pa je brez praske ležala na pultu. Rdeča rokavica je pripadala Marti Voss, ki jo je pri šestih letih izgubila v zasneženem parku, zdaj pa je bila pekovka z moko v obrveh.

Illuminara je, še preden se je kdorkoli premaknil, zapisala napoved na karton, ga prepognila in izročila Wren. Pričakovala je, da bo obiskovalec sledil zadnji roki, ki je držala posamezen predmet.

IlluminaraČe imam prav, gre prstan v hišo gospe Marren, violina k Dunji in rokavica k Marti. Če karkoli od tega gre drugam, se motim, in to boste imele zapisano z mojo roko.

Tri predmete so položili na pult in izpraznili prostor. Wren je skozi steklo notranjih vrat opazovala in štela. Po enainsedemdesetih sekundah se je toplota nad policami premaknila in pult je bil prazen.

Na hodniku je bil prstan v Ilsinem žepu plašča, rokavica pa v Martini roki. Violino je v naročju držal Ferenc Adler, ki zanjo ni prosil in jo je držal kakor človek, ki mu je bil izročen dolg.

IlluminaraNapačno. Ni sledilo zadnji roki. Sledilo je prvi.

FerencPodaril sem jo njej. Pred štiridesetimi ljudmi. Njena je.

Toplota mu ni odgovorila. Dunja je odnesla violino nazaj na pult in jo tam pustila; enajst sekund pozneje je bila znova v Ferenčevih rokah.

Poskusili so to, kar poskusijo ljudje. Ferenc je napisal in podpisal darilno pogodbo, pogodba pa je ležala na pultu, medtem ko se je violina vrnila k njemu. Dunja je poleg nje položila kovanec kot kupnino, kovanec pa se je vrnil v njen žep in violina k Ferencu. Teodor je nejeverno praznemu zraku ponudil potrdilo.

TeodorMoje kosilo je vrnilo v pekarno, ki ga je prodala. Želim, da se to zapiše. Moje kosilo ni bilo izgubljeno.

Wren so trznili kotički ust. Marta Voss, ki je še vedno pritiskala rokavico k licu, se je enkrat zasmejala skozi solze. Ni bilo neprijazno. Stvar se je tako zelo trudila.

DunjaTako zelo se mu trudi narediti nekaj dobrega. Meni pa jemlje edino reč, na katero še lahko igram.

Illuminara je opazovala, kako se odtis palca na oknu hladi. Spraševala sem se, ali razume, komu pripada violina. Morda besede sploh ne potrebuje. Kaj razume, da je darilo videti kot kraja?

03 · Stoletje polovic

Arhiv garderobe, postajna klet · 06:37

Eno izmed rjavih oznak je odnesla po stopnicah navzdol in ga položila pod Teodorjevo bralno svetilko.

IlluminaraVsaka oznaka ima številko, vendar ne najdem nobenega seznama, ki bi jih vseboval. Je v tej stavbi kaj, kar oštevilčuje reči, ne da bi hranilo imena?

Teodor je dolgo gledal oznako, nato njo, potem pa iz jopice vzel medeninast ključ in odprl omaro, mimo katere je dvakrat šla.

V njej so bile knjige, vrsta za vrsto, v knjigah pa sploščenih več deset tisoč polovičnih listkov v isti pisavi. Celo stoletje je postajna garderoba vsak listek pretrgala na dvoje. Ena polovica je bila pripeta na plašč; druga je šla domov v lastnikovem žepu. Plašč je bil opravljen šele, ko sta se polovici srečali.

TeodorEnainštirideset tisoč se jih ni nikoli vrnilo. Ljudje so izgubili svojo polovico. Ljudje so umrli. Vsak odrezek smo obdržali, ker je pravilo garderobe, da je neujemajoča se polovica še vedno obljuba.

WrenIsta pisava. Isto zaporedje številk, ki se nadaljuje. Ne šteje izgubljenega. Šteje tisto, kar se ne ujema.

Illuminara je začutila, kako se je oblika odgovora ustalila, tako kot se ustali dokaz, ko se izkaže, da je bila manjkajoča vrstica ves čas zapisana ob robu.

IlluminaraOdraščalo je v tem prostoru. Vse, kar ve o lastništvu, se je naučilo pri pultu, kjer sta vračanje in odnašanje domov isto dejanje, izvedeno z dvema polovicama enega listka. Ne zna prebrati imena. Ne zna stehtati cene. Razume en znak konca: polovici, ki se srečata.

DunjaIn violina v mojih rokah. Je bil to zanjo sploh kdaj konec?

IlluminaraNe. Držala si samo plašč. Nikoli nisi držala polovice.

Najcenejši preizkus, ki bi lahko dokazal, da se moti, je bil tudi najlažji za izvedbo. Teodor je iz starega bloka izdal en nov oštevilčen par. Ferenc je vzel eno polovico, Dunja drugo. Pritisnila sta palca na karton, nato pa združila pretrgana robova, dokler se papirna vlakna niso srečala vzdolž celotnega reza. Teodor je ponovno združeni par označil z DAROVANO in obe polovici položil v predal knjige. Sama številka ni bila znak; znak je bilo fizično srečanje njenih dveh polovic.

Violina je sedela na pultu devetdeset sekund, nato deset minut, nato pol ure. Na okencu se je ob starem pojavil nov odtis palca in obstal, topel kakor prikimavanje.

Ilse Marren, ki od hodnika ni rekla ničesar, je stopila k pultu.

IlseSvojega imena ne bom vpisala v knjigo. Tisti prstan sem vrgla v pristanišče zato, da ga nihče nikoli več ne bi pogledal.

IlluminaraBrez imena. Teodor izda en oštevilčen par. Vi držite eno polovico, urad pa drugo kot priča. Če želite prstan izpustiti, združite polovici, Teodor pa obe shrani pod številko in besedo IZPUŠČENO. Če ga stvar vseeno vzame, ste izgubili dopoldne in lahko ste do mene tako nesramni, kot želite.

Deset minut pred osmo je Ilse stopila na konec Devetega pomola s polovico listka v eni roki in prstanom v drugi. Teodor je uradno polovico nosil na majhni knjižni podlagi. Ilse je pretrgani rob prislonila k njegovemu paru; ko so se srečala vsa papirna vlakna, je Teodor številko ožigosal z IZPUŠČENO. Šele potem je nad vodo razprla drugo roko. Prstan je potonil skozi zeleno svetlobo.

Ni se vrnil. Ilse je dolgo stala s prazno dlanjo. Njen obraz ni bil videti olajšan; videti je bil kakor nekaj, kar je bilo izpuščeno.

04 · Ob devetih

Vzhodno preddverje, urad za najdene predmete · 09:00–09:50

Magistratinja Calloway je sedla na stol za pultom. Illuminarin načrt, eno stran preprostih stavkov, je prebrala stoje, nato pa še drugič.

IlluminaraTri pravila. Teodor za vsako dejanje izda en oštevilčen par. Kdor prosi za vrnitev, obdrži eno polovico, urad pa njen par hrani ločeno kot odprto obljubo. Pri darilu ali prodaji stari in novi imetnik združita polovici; Teodor ponovno združeni par shrani z oznako DAROVANO ali PRODANO. Kdor se želi nečemu odpovedati, združi eno polovico z drugo, ki jo kot priča drži urad; Teodor obe shrani z oznako IZPUŠČENO. Nikoli nobenega imena. Vsak spor obdrži sodišče. Obiskovalca ne prosim, naj razume karkoli novega. Prosim ga, naj še naprej počne to, kar že počne, in naj najprej vpraša.

Ines CallowayPredlagate, naj mesto nekaj, s čimer se še nikoli ni pogovarjalo, ureja z garderobnim listkom.

IlluminaraPredlagam, da z njim govorimo v edinem jeziku, ki nas ga je naučilo. In predlagam, da mu dovolimo reči ne.

Na pult je položila pretrgan listek s tremi pravili na hrbtni strani, zraven pa drugi, prazen listek. Če se obiskovalec ne bi odzval, je rekla, bi se prostovoljne predložitve ob pol desetih ustavile. Urad bi ostal odprt in natanko tam, kjer je bil. Na tržnici in Drugem peronu bi policisti družinam z majhnimi otroki izdali skrbniške pare in poskrbeli, da bi se robova vsakega para srečala, preden bi bil kdo odložen, tako da bi kratka ločitev ostala zaključen odnos. Sodno osebje bi označilo nadzorovano območje, opazovalo bolnišnično omaro in se ne bi dotikalo nobene police, odrezka ali predmeta, dokler obiskovalec ne bi odgovoril ali sodišče zasnovalo drugega preizkusa brez selitve. Premor je bil začasno stališče sodišča, ne kazen za molk.

Bilo je 09:02. Toplota nad pultom se je napela.

Na klopi ob oknu je sedel štiriletni deček v pretankem plašču in držal papirnato vetrnico, ki je bila še pred trenutkom v roki nekoga drugega. V jutranji tržni gneči so ga za dve sekundi odložili, toplota pa ga je pobrala z enako potrpežljivo vljudnostjo, kot jo je pokazala škarjam.

Ozrl se je po prostoru in začel tuliti.

TeodorO ne. Ne, ne, ljudi jemlje kot izgubljene predmete.

Policist Aldo Brezar je bil že na radijski zvezi. Illuminara je klečala pred klopjo z odprtimi in praznimi rokami. Dečka se ni dotaknila. Toplote ni ustavila s silo, čeprav bi jo lahko; pomislila je na bolnišnično omaro in misel izpustila.

IlluminaraTeodor. Poišči njegovo mamo. Prinesi listek.

Lenka Vidmar je pritekla čez trg, na rokavih pa je imela moko s stojnice na sejmu. Teodor je pretrgal listek. Vzela je eno polovico; deček, ki je še tulil, je dobil drugo in stisnil pest okoli nje. Njuna palca sta se srečala na pretrganem robu.

Toplota nad pultom se je zbrala sama vase in izpustila pot, ki jo je že odprla. Deček je ostal na klopi, kamor ga je prinesla, zdaj pa se je naslonil na materina kolena in toplota ga ni več držala kot nedokončano vrnitev. Sredi vdiha je nehal jokati.

Ines CallowayPustilo ga je pri njej.

IlluminaraIzpustilo je odnos, ki ga je že premaknilo. Nismo še dokazali, da ne bo odprlo naslednje poti.

Prazen listek na pultu se je ogrel vzdolž celotnega pretrganega roba. Na steklu se je počasi pojavil odtis palca, nato zraven njega še drugi. Teodor je pozneje rekel, da je enaintrideset let čakal, da bi obiskovalec sprejel povabilo na kartonastem odrezku.

Ob 09:07 je Wren začela prospektivni preizkus. Teodor je na pult položil svoj medeninasti garderobni ključ, ne da bi odprl listek ali ponudil uradno polovico. Dve minuti — precej dlje od prejšnjih enajstih in enainsedemdesetih sekund — se je toplota nagibala proti njemu, obstala in se umaknila. Ključ je ostal natanko tam, kamor ga je položil.

Calloway je za govor potrebovala dlje, kot je bilo prostoru všeč.

Ines CallowayTrideset dni. Javni seznam številk, nikoli imen. Vsak imetnik lahko zavrne listek in noben predmet ne zapusti imetnika, ki zavrača, brez sodišča. Vi pa mi boste uradno pojasnili, kaj se zgodi z rečmi, ki jih je že prineslo.

IlluminaraVečina gre domov. Nekatere ne. Zastavljalnica gospe Vuk hrani žepno uro, ki jo družina Rask želi že dvajset let. Pošteno ji je bila prodana. Obiskovalec jo je danes zjutraj vrnil Raskovim, ona pa ima pravico zavrniti združitev polovic.

Gospa Vuk, ki so jo pripeljali iz trgovine v predpasniku, je zavrnila prenosni par z Raskovimi. Sprejela pa je skrbniški par s pisarjem Callowayeve: njuna robova sta se srečala, pisar je ponovno združeni polovici označil s SPORNO in ura je šla v sodni sef, da bo njen primer obravnavan posebej. Toplota se je nagnila proti sefu, tam vztrajala dlje kot enainsedemdeset sekund in se umaknila. Hči Raskovih, ki je tisto jutro uro držala štirideset minut, je jokala na hodniku in nihče ji ni rekel, da je to zgolj pravično.

05 · Najprej vprašaj

Glavna pristaniška postaja, urad za najdene predmete · 19:20

Do večera je imel urad nad pultom novo tablo, ki jo je Teodor izpisal v stari garderobni pisavi. NAJDENO IN IZGUBLJENO. NAJPREJ VPRAŠAJ.

Od 09:07 ni bilo vzeto nič. Triinosemdeset od štiristo predmetov je ostalo nerešenih: dvainosemdeset na policah, neprevzetih ali zavrnjenih, in ura gospe Vuk ločeno v sodnem sefu. Vsi drugi so bili drug za drugim združeni s polovicami ali izpuščeni s pretrganim listkom in tiho besedo ob pomolu. Vrsta se je vila po preddverju do trafike.

Marta Voss je v njej stala z rokavico v žepu. Ni prišla ničesar prevzet, temveč pustit polovico listka za drugo rokavico, ki jo je izgubila v istem parku, če bi jo bilo še vedno mogoče kje najti. Sedemletna deklica pred njo je pustila polovico listka za plišastega medveda in z resno vljudnostjo rekla praznemu zraku nad policami:

DeklicaProsim, če ni preveč težav. Ime mu je Piškotek.

Toplota se je približala, premislila in na steklo ob njenem listku položila odtis palca. To ni bil medved. Bil je da.

Illuminara je stala ob notranjih vratih z Wren in opazovala, kako se vrsta krajša.

WrenZjutraj bi ga lahko ustavila s silo. Ko je prišel deček.

IlluminaraDa. Potem bi bilo le še ena reč, ki se je odločila, ne da bi vprašala. Hotela sem, da vidi, kaj pomeni vprašati.

TeodorTega o Raskovih ne bo razumelo. Njihove ure. Še naprej jim jo bo hotelo vrniti.

IlluminaraMorda ne bo nikoli razumelo. Vendar ga je mogoče prositi, povabilo lahko obdrži, sodišče pa lahko nosi tisto, česar ono ne zmore.

Za steklom se je ovalni odtis palca ogrel in zbledel, ogrel in zbledel, s počasno pravilnostjo nekoga, ki čaka. Illuminara ni pritisnila svojega palca ob okno. Dvignila je roko do višine odtisa, skoraj do dotika, in jo tam obdržala.

Vprašalo je, je pomislila, in dobilo odgovor. Zdaj lahko katerokoli od naju reče ne.

Kaj po mnenju kritika še ne deluje

Slovnica raztrganih listkov v uradu za najdene predmete omogoči Illuminari, da prijaznost, ki ne zna ločiti darila od kraje, prosi, naj se ustavi — ne s silo, temveč v njenem lastnem jeziku — mesto pa ohrani pravico reči ne.

Zasnova te zgodbe

Pet prizorov: zora nezaželenih vrnitev in sodni nalog, ki bi težavo le premaknil; zapečaten preizkus s tremi predmeti, ki ovrže Illuminarino prvo hipotezo in razkrije bitje, ki ne zna ločiti darila od kraje; odkritje v arhivu garderobe, da obiskovalec odprte obljube in zaključene odnose razume le prek ujemajočih se polovic listkov; dosledna slovnica privolitve, preizkušena na darilu, namerni odpovedi in dečku, ki je že prinesen na obravnavo, z varnim nadzorovanim premorom, če obiskovalec zavrne; ter poskusna izmenjava s prostovoljno vključitvijo, v kateri nekatere ljubljene reči ostanejo izgubljene, obiskovalca pa prosijo, ne ukažejo mu.

2026-10-12WHAT THE OLD WOMAN'S TERRACES KNEWKAJ SO VEDELE STARKINE TERASE
