2026-10-18 · S2The Air the Second-Born HeldZRAK, KI GA JE DRŽAL DRUGOROJENI89

On the refinery moon Calder, eight hundred engineered workers with no standing have set the air plants to a slow decline, and Illuminara must find a guarantee strong enough that nobody has to hold a breath hostage again, and a standing no council can vote away.

01 · The Gate Below Nine

Lower Level Nine, Ring Gate · Day one, 06:10

Wren Odira read the numbers off the slate before the lift had finished opening.

Wren OdiraPlant Two is at eighty-two percent of rating and losing two points a day. Oxygen from the tanks covers the gap, but only down to seventy-four. Under that there is not enough reserve to repressurize a deck that takes a breach.

IlluminaraAnd what the tanks cannot cover?

Wren OdiraThe tanks make oxygen. They do not scrub. Level Four read four thousand parts per million of carbon dioxide at midnight. The norm is fifteen hundred.

IlluminaraWho lives on Four?

Wren OdiraThe clinic. The nursery. The old. It is the dead end of the ring, so it fills first, and the clinic's last ward sits past the final vent where the air barely moves.

IlluminaraAnd Plant One?

Wren OdiraForty-one percent of the colony's air, and the engineers say it cannot be pushed past forty-nine. Plant Two is the rest, and it feeds the lower ring. Kesh Aurelian sequences Plant Two. He said day five, and at two points a day, day five is the line.

The corridor beyond the gate was low and warm and smelled of hot metal and boiled grain. Illuminara stood in it and let AC do what it did. It did not read minds. It made the lines of force in a place visible to her, the way a map makes slope visible, and she saw how the people in the corridor leaned. Every body leaned toward the gate. It was not the lean of hostility. It was the posture of people who had been told, all their lives, to wait.

That is a reading, and a reading is a guess about someone else's mouth. Test it before you rest on it. She had been a court interpreter at twenty-four. She had learned that the first meaning always arrives sounding like certainty.

A woman in a grey scrubber-hall coat came out of the crowd and did not offer her hand.

Marit VossMarit Voss. I run the day shift on Two. Kesh said you would ask to see him first.

IlluminaraI would like to start with whoever has to breathe the answer.

Marit VossThat is the wrong answer for a diplomat and I may keep you for it. There are eight hundred of us on this plant. We have run it since before the first of us was born, legally speaking. Come to the hall tonight. They will ask you things. If you answer like a politician nobody opens that door twice.

02 · What the Hall Asked

Scrubber Hall, Plant Two · Day one, 20:00

They sat on the maintenance benches among the great grey cylinders, three hundred of them, the rest listening on the relay. The elder, Osei Tamm, had a voice like a worn gasket and did not stand to speak.

Osei TammNinth Year, the council gave us a charter. Recognition, they called it. We were counted. We were given ration cards with our own names on them. In nineteen months they repealed it. They did not even need a vote against us. A clerk added a column to the census, and we were contract stock again, and the cards stopped working at the canteen. I was forty. I remember the canteen.

IlluminaraAnd since then, every recognition has been used and then taken back.

Osei TammEvery one. Do not say it as if you have learned it from a file. The one thing on this moon they cannot repeal is the air, because it passes through our hands. That is not politics. It is the only fact we have.

Marit VossAsk her what you want to ask her, Teodor. You have been holding it all day.

A young man near the back stood up. His hands were scarred across the knuckles from valve work.

Teodor RaskMy sister is on Level Four. First-born husband, a baby. The ring read forty-one hundred when I came off shift, and I turn the dials that are going down. I am not going to tell you I am proud of it. If this ends with a child on Four in a tent, I did that, and Kesh did that, and I do not know what I am if the price of being a person is that.

Nobody spoke. Marit turned toward the room.

Marit VossI am not a lever. I want that said in front of her. I want my standing. I do not want it bought with somebody's lungs. If the only way we get it is by being the hand on the throat, then they were right about us and I would rather lose.

Osei TammThen you will lose, Marit. Sit down. Both things are true.

Illuminara waited until the room had finished being angry. Then she said what she had, which was less than they wanted.

IlluminaraI do not know yet how to give you a guarantee that cannot be taken back. I know three things. I know I cannot ask you to trust me. I know the air has been your only leverage and your worst burden. And I know that if I find nothing, I will say so plainly, and you will have lost nothing but two days.

Osei TammTwo days. That is an honest sum. Come back when you have something that does not need us to like you.

03 · The Number Behind the Council

Council Chamber, Upper Ring · Day two, 09:15

Speaker Ines Torvald had a clipped, tired manner and did not rise either.

Ines TorvaldWe do not bargain with hostage-takers. If we do, the next group learns the shape of the key. I will hear you out. I will not promise you a concession.

IlluminaraI am not asking you to bargain. I am asking what happens on day five.

Chief Engineer Dov Hallam unrolled a section of the plant schematic across the table. He spoke like a man reading a charge sheet against his own drawings.

Dov HallamDay five is seventy-four. Below that there is no breach margin. I have a cross-feed from the upper tank farm that would let me seal the upper decks' reserve and let the lower ones go short. I drew it at six this morning because it was the thing I could draw.

IlluminaraThen it is a verdict, not a reserve. Fourteen thousand people live below Nine. They did not set the plants. Seal everyone or seal no one.

Pell ArkwrightThe owners will lose four days of throughput if you ration the ore plant to hold the margin for all decks. I am obliged to say so.

IlluminaraSay it and then do it. The cost is real. It is also smaller than a deck that cannot be repressurized.

Dov looked at the cross-feed line, then at her, and rolled it differently, so that the reserve fed all decks to the same floor.

Dov HallamRationing the ore plant by half holds seventy-four for everyone to about day seven. It also frees the power to wake the old scrubber banks on Four through Seven. They need a day and a quarter. The ring will not have its air back before tomorrow noon.

IlluminaraThen someone on Four has a bad night first.

Dov HallamYes. I would rather say it than have you find it.

While they argued the rationing, AC showed her something else. Three lines of dependence ran across the room, from the council, from the owners, from the engineer, and all three ran toward the same place and none of the three looked at it. It was the Registry certificate on the wall behind Dov, off-moon authority, the plant's operating licence.

A hypothesis. Do not say it as a fact.

IlluminaraDov. What does the certificate say about who may run the plant?

Dov HallamRegistry-certified operators. It says nothing about class. The second-born run it under the contract-labour schedule because the owners filed them there thirty years ago and nobody since has put the question to the Registry.

IlluminaraWhat does the Registry do with an operator it has certified?

Dov HallamUnder Article Six, an operator of record is held by the person, not the employer. Name, number, post. The colony can change a worker's census class. It cannot change a record the Registry keeps. Only a finding of cause from the Registry's Review Panel, after a hearing, can remove one.

IlluminaraAnd the article itself? Who can change that?

Dov HallamTwo-thirds of the forty member settlements, in open session, after a hundred and eighty days' notice. Six of them run crews like ours on the same schedule. Nobody here has ever filed a single operator under Six. We have never been in it.

Ines Torvald had stopped looking at her papers.

Ines TorvaldIf the Registry recorded them, I could not repeal it by resolution.

Dov HallamNo. And the insurer would not insure a plant whose operators of record were struck off by a colony vote. Pell could not sell a barrel.

Pell Arkwright said nothing, which was an answer.

Dov HallamOne more thing, since we are being honest. There is an old safety trip on the sequence bus. It forbids sequencing below ninety percent of rating. We bypassed it years ago because it tripped the plant whenever a bearing ran hot.

IlluminaraKeep it. I may want it back.

She turned back to the table, and to the room that had begun, a little, to lean toward the same place.

IlluminaraThen there is a cost to repeal that does not depend on anyone on Calder being kind. I will test whether it is as strong as it sounds. I will test it with the man who has the most reason to doubt it.

04 · The Key and the Throat

Plant Two Control Room · Day three, 14:00

Kesh Aurelian was tall in the way that engineered people sometimes were, designed for a height nobody had asked for, and he stood with his back to the sequencing console as if guarding it from her. Hana Duvall, a nurse from the Level Four clinic, had come up with Illuminara. Illuminara had asked for a first-born resident. Hana had asked for the room.

Kesh AurelianYou have been on my moon for three days and the first thing you do is give the council a way to ration around me.

IlluminaraYes. And I told you within the hour, because if you had found it yourself on day four, you would never have believed anything I said after.

Kesh AurelianYou hedged my five days.

IlluminaraI protected the people who were not part of the argument. That is not the same as protecting them from you. It protects them from the arithmetic.

Kesh took that. His hands, at his sides, did not move.

Kesh AurelianSay what you came to say.

IlluminaraThe eight hundred have never been filed as operators of record. The owners put them on the contract-labour schedule, and the council has been able to do to them what a clerk did in the Ninth Year. Refile them under Article Six. Each of you gets a record the Registry keeps off-moon: your name, not a stock number, your post, your contract in your own hand. You can be moved only by a finding of cause after a hearing. And on the console itself, every shift run by two operators, one second-born and one other, and any sequence change needs both keys.

Kesh AurelianThen I will tell you how they beat it. Tell me where I am wrong. They bring in first-born operators and let ours lapse unused.

IlluminaraA post of record cannot be vacated except by the panel. A cut of more than a tenth of a crew opens a public hearing, a hundred and eighty days.

Kesh AurelianThe census puts us back to contract stock.

IlluminaraThe census is the council's. The record is the Registry's.

Kesh AurelianPell sells the plant to someone who does not care.

IlluminaraThe licence cannot pass without the operators of record. The buyer takes the eight hundred with the plant, or buys nothing.

Kesh AurelianThey change the article.

IlluminaraTwo-thirds of forty settlements, in open session, after a hundred and eighty days. It can be done. It cannot be done quietly. The Ninth Year took nineteen months of quiet. This would take a year of daylight.

Kesh AurelianA year of daylight is still a year. And it is still other people's goodwill.

IlluminaraIt is other people's interest. Six settlements stand on the same article. Goodwill has to be asked for. Interest only has to be noticed.

Kesh looked at the console, not at her. It was the first time he had looked away from her since she came in.

Kesh AurelianSay all of that holds. You still hand me a console with two keys on it. What stops the next Kesh? What stops Jun, or Pell's man, who sits on his key until a bearing fails and says the second-born made him do it? Joint control is a veto in two hands. A hostage needs a hand and a throat. You have given the hands a new throat.

Illuminara did not answer at once. He is not asking to rule. Watch what he is asking for. He did not want the chair. He wanted the thing nobody could take back, and he had just found the flaw in her own design faster than she had.

IlluminaraYou are right. I built that wrong. A veto over the air is still a lever. So take the lever out of the keys. Dov's old trip, the one that forbids sequencing below ninety percent of rating. We put it back, and the Registry seals it, not the council and not you. The keys never decide whether air flows. They decide only when a maintenance window may open, above the floor, with seventy-two hours' notice posted to every deck.

Kesh AurelianAnd below the floor?

IlluminaraNothing reaches it. Not a vote, not a key, not two keys, not an order from me. A window may take one train offline for repair, and only while the plant stays at ninety or above. The one way under is to cut the seal by hand. Then every deck's alarm sounds, the Registry duty office is paged, and the interlock re-arms itself and drives the plant back to rating in six hours, whatever anyone does. And any one operator, of any faction, may hold or raise output with no key at all.

Kesh AurelianSix hours is still six hours.

IlluminaraAt two points a day, six hours is half a point. From ninety, seventy-four is eight days away. Nobody can hold a breath hostage on that clock.

Kesh AurelianJun withholds his key to stop a repair. The bearing fails. Your floor forbids anyone from commanding below ninety. It does not make dead metal turn.

IlluminaraNo. A command rule cannot, and I will not pretend it can. So there are two things under the floor. The first is iron. Plant Two has a fifth train the owners never commissioned for output, a standby that can only stand in for a train that drops out, never lift the plant above rating. When a bearing trips, the interlock starts the standby itself, with no key and no vote. The second is the repair. A bearing reading over the published vibration limit, taken from the Registry-sealed sensor bus and not from anyone's word, lets any single operator of record declare that train failed. No key. The declaration takes that one train offline, calls both crews, and posts to every deck within the hour. It cannot touch another train, it opens nothing without a reading behind it, and it does not open until the standby is carrying the load.

Kesh AurelianAnd if two bearings go in one night?

IlluminaraThen the metal has beaten the paper, and the plant sags whatever any of us signed. The alarms sound, the Registry is paged, and the crews go in on the declaration without waiting for a key. That night is an engineering night. It cannot be turned into a hostage, because nobody's key is on it.

Kesh AurelianSo Jun's withheld key delays a scheduled repair and nothing else.

IlluminaraYes. Plant Two runs rough until the published window, or until a measured failure makes the declaration. The cost is a worse week for the engineers, and not a worse week for Level Four. The worst a withheld key can do is nothing. I do not know whether that is enough for you. It is the only kind of safeguard I can see that does not need a better man than you in the chair after you.

Hana had been standing against the wall. She spoke without moving off it.

Hana DuvallI am not here to be your lever either. I walked up here to tell you that. Level Four read forty-six hundred at midnight, and the dead-end ward past the last vent read nine thousand. For most of us that is a headache. Anselm Pruitt was seventy-one and had two years of lung left. He carried carbon dioxide on a good day. His blood turned acid: seven-twenty-one at two, seven-eighteen at half past three. He died at four this morning. I will not tell you the air alone killed him. Nobody can. It took the last margin he had. We put the nursery in oxygen tents because we had to do something with our hands, and tents do nothing for carbon dioxide.

Kesh AurelianI read the list.

Hana DuvallThen you know the name.

Kesh AurelianAnselm Pruitt. Yes.

He did not defend it. Illuminara watched him not defend it, and watched the old reflex that said every harm was the fault of those who had made harm necessary rise and die in his throat. He said, quietly, to the room:

Kesh AurelianI drew seventy-four for the oxygen. I knew the ring would climb on carbon dioxide before that. I never asked what the dead end would read. I told myself the clinics could carry it for four days. I wrote the word acceptable in my notes. A man's name sits under that word now, beside whatever else was wrong with him.

Hana DuvallThe banks on Four came up at 13:40. The ring is at thirty-nine hundred and falling. That is the first hour in three days I have not had to count breaths. Now you decide what that means.

Kesh did not answer. He stood with his back to the console, and then, slowly, he turned and looked at it.

05 · The Count

Ballot Hall, Lower Level Seven · Day four, 19:30

The second-born voted without Kesh in the room. Marit had insisted, and Osei Tamm had agreed, and Kesh had gone and sat in the plant corridor with his arms folded and looked at nothing. Illuminara stood at the back with Wren and held her tongue while eight hundred people raised their hands under the grey lights.

The elders counted. Marit counted twice.

Osei TammSix hundred and twelve for. A hundred and forty-eight against. Forty abstain.

Teodor RaskI voted against. A record is not a vote. It is a floor under the work, and a name on a shelf off-moon. It is not a door into the house.

IlluminaraYou are right. It is standing of record: your name, your post, a hearing before anyone can move you. It is not a seat on the council and I will not call it one. I would rather say that now than have you find it in a year.

Teodor RaskThen why did you ask us to vote?

IlluminaraBecause I do not get to decide for you what is enough. I get to put an honest thing in front of you, and you get to hold it up and see how it looks.

Marit VossThen the charter is the door, and this is the floor under the door. Let them sign it. If they break it again, there is still a floor under the broken door.

The Registry duty officer, Sabine Okafor-Reyes, appeared on the relay at 20:10 with the refiling: all eight hundred as operators of record under Article Six, the floor interlock sealed under the Registry's own key, the joint-shift rule written into the plant's certificate as a condition of operation, effective on restoration. Beside her signature stood another.

Pell ArkwrightI am obliged to countersign as licence-holder. I find I am relieved to.

Teodor, who read cryptographic signatures for fun, checked the Registry's key against the refiling on his own console. Kesh called the duty officer on a line the council did not control and listened to her say the same words twice.

Kesh AurelianShe says the refiling stands whether the council signs the charter or not.

IlluminaraIt does. I would not give you anything that needed the council's signature to exist.

Kesh AurelianThen I stand down first. Before the charter. Before any reward.

IlluminaraThat is the order I wanted.

The council did not sign that night. Ines Torvald sent word that she would sign at dawn and not before, as if the hour were hers. Illuminara decided that did not matter. The stand-down was Kesh's to make. The signing was the council's to be seen making. Different currencies.

06 · Seventy-Four

Scrubber Hall, Plant Two · Day five, 02:40

Marit and a council engineer named Jun Calloway sat shoulder to shoulder at the sequencing console, each with a key on a chain round the neck, and neither would look at the other. Jun's hands shook. Marit's did not.

Kesh took the open channel and spoke into it the way a man pulls a splinter.

Kesh AurelianThis is Kesh Aurelian. At 02:41 I release the sequencing on Plant Two. I set the plants to decline. A man named Anselm Pruitt is dead, and my setting took the margin he had left. I will answer to the Registry's inquiry and to the people of Level Four. The air belongs to all of you now. It always did.

He turned the key in his own lock and set it in Marit's palm. Jun turned his. The console accepted the pair, and the Registry's seal came up green on the bus.

The numbers began to climb. Nobody had to turn a key to raise them. Wren read them out softly, as if loud voices might stall them.

Wren OdiraSeventy-six. Seventy-nine. Eighty-two. The tanks are drawing down.

Marit VossHold it there. Let it rise on its own, not on mine or his.

Jun CallowayI came up here afraid of what you would do with that key.

Marit VossI was afraid of the same about yours.

Jun CallowayI could not lower it now if I tried. Could I?

Dov HallamNeither of you could. Not alone, not together. The most anyone can do is cut the seal by hand, and then every alarm on Calder sounds and the interlock climbs back on its own in six hours. That is the point.

Osei Tamm stood at the back of the hall with his arms crossed. He did not look pleased, and Illuminara had not expected him to.

Osei TammA record is not a home. A floor is not a house.

IlluminaraNo. It is where you start from, and nobody can pull it out from under you without paying for it in front of six settlements. That is less than you deserve. It is more than you had on Monday. The ballot on Calder is still the council's to give.

Osei TammHmph. It is a start. I will eat my cap if they keep the second key when the owners change.

IlluminaraThe licence cannot pass without you, the Registry will have the owners to deal with, and you will have Marit to ask.

Hana Duvall came down at 03:30 with a thermos and set it on the console without a word, between two keys.

Hana DuvallFour is at two thousand and falling.

Marit looked at the thermos, and at her, and nodded once. Nothing was forgiven. It was only shared.

By 04:10 Plant Two was at ninety-six percent of rating, the seventy-four line a long way below them, the tanks refilling, and the reserve for every deck on Calder, upper and lower, held to the same floor. The council would sign at dawn. The resentment in the hall would last longer than the signing, and Illuminara did not mind that, because resentment was an honest instrument and it was pointed at something real.

Marit VossI wanted my standing without being the hand on the throat. This is the first thing I have held on this plant that cannot hurt anyone.

She leaned back from the console and breathed in, long, as if testing that it was hers.

Marit VossTwo keys.

IlluminaraTwo keys.

Na rafinerijski luni Calder je osemsto ustvarjenih delavcev brez pravnega statusa nastavilo zračne obrate na počasno upadanje, Illuminara pa mora najti jamstvo, dovolj trdno, da nikomur ne bo treba nikoli več držati diha za talca, in status, ki ga noben svet ne more izglasovati stran.

01 · Vrata pod devetico

Spodnja raven devet, krožna vrata · prvi dan, 06:10

Wren Odira je prebrala številke s tablice, še preden se je dvigalo povsem odprlo.

Wren OdiraObrat dve je na dvainosemdesetih odstotkih nazivne zmogljivosti in izgublja dve točki na dan. Kisik iz rezervoarjev pokriva razliko, vendar le do štiriinsedemdeset. Pod tem ni dovolj rezerve, da bi znova vzpostavili tlak na krovu, ki bi ga predrlo.

IlluminaraIn česa rezervoarji ne morejo pokriti?

Wren OdiraRezervoarji proizvajajo kisik. Ne čistijo zraka. Raven štiri je opolnoči pokazala štiri tisoč delcev ogljikovega dioksida na milijon. Običajna vrednost je tisoč petsto.

IlluminaraKdo živi na štirici?

Wren OdiraKlinika. Vrtec. Stari. To je slepi konec obroča, zato se napolni prvi, zadnji oddelek klinike pa leži za zadnjo zračno režo, kjer se zrak komaj premika.

IlluminaraIn obrat ena?

Wren OdiraEnainštirideset odstotkov zraka kolonije, inženirji pa pravijo, da ga ni mogoče potisniti čez devetinštirideset. Obrat dve zagotavlja preostalo in napaja spodnji obroč. Kesh Aurelian določa zaporedje obrata dve. Rekel je peti dan, in pri dveh točkah na dan je peti dan meja.

Kesh AurelianTukaj Kesh Aurelian. Ob 02:41 sproščam zaporedje obrata dve. Jaz sem nastavil upadanje obratov. Človek z imenom Anselm Pruitt je mrtev in moja nastavitev mu je vzela preostalo rezervo. Odgovarjal bom preiskavi Registra in ljudem ravni štiri. Zrak zdaj pripada vsem vam. Vedno vam je.

Ključ je obrnil v lastni ključavnici in ga položil v Maritino dlan. Jun je obrnil svojega. Konzola je sprejela par, pečat Registra na vodilu pa je zasvetil zeleno.

Številke so začele rasti. Nikomur ni bilo treba obrniti ključa, da bi jih dvignil. Wren jih je tiho brala, kakor da bi jih glasni glasovi lahko ustavili.

Wren OdiraŠestinsedemdeset. Devetinsedemdeset. Dvainosemdeset. Rezervoarji se praznijo.

Marit VossDržite tam. Naj raste samo, ne po moji ali njegovi volji.

Jun CallowaySem gor sem prišel v strahu, kaj boste naredili s tem ključem.

Marit VossEnako me je bilo strah vašega.

Jun CallowayZdaj ga ne bi mogel znižati, tudi če bi poskusil. Kajne?

Dov HallamNobeden od vaju ne bi mogel. Ne sam, ne skupaj. Največ, kar lahko kdorkoli stori, je ročno prerezati pečat, tedaj pa se oglasi vsak alarm na Calderju in zapora se v šestih urah sama povzpne nazaj. To je bistvo.

Osei Tamm je stal zadaj v hali s prekrižanimi rokami. Ni bil videti zadovoljen in Illuminara tega ni pričakovala.

Osei TammZapis ni dom. Tla niso hiša.

IlluminaraNe. So mesto, od koder začnete, in nihče vam jih ne more potegniti izpod nog, ne da bi to plačal pred šestimi naselbinami. To je manj, kot si zaslužite. Je več, kot ste imeli v ponedeljek. Glasovnica na Calderju je še vedno v rokah sveta.

Osei TammHm. To je začetek. Pojedel bom svojo čepico, če ob menjavi lastnikov obdržijo drugi ključ.

IlluminaraLicence ni mogoče prenesti brez vas, Register bo imel opravka z lastniki, vi pa boste lahko vprašali Marit.

Hana Duvall je ob 03:30 prišla dol s termovko in jo brez besede postavila na konzolo med dva ključa.

Hana DuvallŠtirica je na dva tisoč in pada.

Marit je pogledala termovko, nato njo, in enkrat pokimala. Nič ni bilo odpuščeno. Samo deljeno je bilo.

Ob 04:10 je bil obrat dve na šestindevetdesetih odstotkih nazivne zmogljivosti, meja štiriinsedemdeset daleč pod njimi, rezervoarji so se polnili, rezerva za vsak krov na Calderju, zgornji in spodnji, pa je držala isto spodnjo mejo. Svet bo podpisal ob zori. Zamera v hali bo trajala dlje od podpisa in Illuminaro to ni motilo, ker je bila zamera pošten instrument, usmerjen v nekaj resničnega.

Marit VossHotela sem svoj status, ne da bi bila roka na grlu. To je prva stvar, ki jo držim v tem obratu in ne more nikomur škodovati.

Odmaknila se je od konzole in globoko vdihnila, kakor da preverja, ali je dih njen.

Marit VossDva ključa.

IlluminaraDva ključa.

A second-born plant operator who has learned that recognition can be repealed gives up control of Calder's air only after Illuminara helps build a standing no council can quietly erase and an air floor no withheld key can turn back into a hostage.
2026-10-18 · S3The Guest Too Large for the UniverseGostja, prevelika za vesolje88

A visitor from outside the cosmos is bending the stars of the Hesper cluster, three armed powers are turning their keys, and Illuminara has to find out whether what looks like an attack is a birth, in a way none of them has to take on trust.

01 · The Nine Clocks

Command deck, Kerrel's Anchor · Day one, 06:10

Nine clocks hung on the forward bulkhead, and no two of them agreed.

The Kerrel Assembly kept them there on purpose, one for each inhabited system that still sent timing, so that its officers could see how far the sky had come apart. The spread was four minutes now. Yesterday it had been ninety seconds. Beyond the viewport the stars of the Hesper cluster sat a hair out of place, as though someone had jogged the table they stood on.

Commander Maren Vask met Illuminara at the lock with a slate in each hand and no greeting.

Maren VaskYou have until ten-forty tomorrow morning. That is the Triple Key's deadline, not mine. Kerrel, the Ussu Chorus and the Halcyon fleet each hold a third of a strike order, and all three keys are turned to ready. At ten-forty the thing in the outer reach is hit with everything the Reach owns.

Wren OdiraIt has not fired on anyone. I would like that said before anybody says invader.

Maren VaskIt has taken the tide-songs from two Ussu brood-worlds. It cost a convoy its navigation for six hours. It is a body the size of a small moon, and the sky bends where it sits. I do not need it to fire, Wren. I need it to stop.

IlluminaraThen I will ask what you would ask. What is it doing, in order, and when? Not what it means. The log.

Nine entries. Each was a pulse: the stars within a light-year swung a few arcseconds off their places, hung there for about forty minutes, and swung back. Illuminara did not look at the swells. She looked at the gaps between them.

IlluminaraTwelve hours. Ten and a half. Nine and a bit. Each gap is seven-eighths of the one before it.

Maren VaskA decaying signal. Alarms do that. So does a fuse.

IlluminaraYes. And a fuse has an end. The last gap was four hours forty-three, and seven times that is thirty-three hours. At about one tomorrow afternoon the gaps are shorter than the pulses, and the pulses run together into one. Your Key turns two hours and twenty minutes before that.

Nobody on the deck said anything. One of the nine clocks ticked over, three seconds ahead of its neighbour.

IlluminaraIt is an ending or a beginning. I do not know which, and neither do you. Before anything else I want a way of finding out that none of us has to take on faith.

02 · Seven-Eighths

Operations hall, Kerrel's Anchor · Day one, 07:40

The delegates came by relay. Commander Vask for Kerrel. Iyo-Seven for the Ussu Chorus, seven bodies in a tide-tank who spoke by turns. Tally for the Halcyon fleet, a point of cold light on the table that answered in the flat, courteous voice of a machine that had decided long ago not to be liked.

Illuminara set two stories side by side and asked them to distrust both equally.

IlluminaraStory one: a being from outside our cosmos is in labour. The gaps shorten because that is what labour does, and between pulses the strain falls back to the same floor, because between contractions a body rests. Story two: an incursion wearing a mother's shape. The gaps shorten because the weapon is nearly ready, and between pulses the floor would climb, because a thing that is charging does not rest.

Maren VaskStory two is the one a mother's story is built to hide.

IlluminaraYes. So I am not asking you to believe either. I am asking you to predict. Each of you measures the pulses with your own instruments and seals when the next one begins and how high the floor sits after it. Nobody opens an envelope until the pulse has come.

TallyKerrel keeps time by atoms, the Chorus by song, my fleet by ranging pulsars. Our errors are unrelated. If all three of us land together, it will not be because we copied one another.

Iyo-SevenEach pulse unmakes the songs of our young. We do not ask for pity. We ask that your test be quick.

The tenth pulse began at 08:07 and held the sky for forty minutes. Tally had sealed 08:06, the Chorus 08:09, Kerrel 08:05. Behind the swell the floor dropped back to where it had dropped before, within the width of a pencil line.

Maren VaskOnce. A coin can land once.

IlluminaraA coin can. So we throw it again at every pulse, and we write down now what would make us stop. If the floor rises more than three parts in a hundred of the peak, or a gap breaks the seven-eighths by more than a quarter of itself, the story is false and the Key turns. We write that while none of us is frightened. We will not be able to write it when we are.

Tally logged the line without comment. After a moment so did Iyo-Seven, seven bodies nodding out of step. Vask took longest.

Maren VaskAnd while the coin is in the air, you intend to go and ask the thing itself.

IlluminaraI intend to ask it what no instrument of yours can read.

Wren OdiraI keep the clock. I say your name every hour. You answer, or I come in.

IlluminaraYou can't come into a window.

Wren OdiraWatch me find out.

03 · The Window

Tide-boat Long Patience, outer reach · Day one, 09:20

The Long Patience hung a light-year and a half from the guest, as near as the Ussu would take her. Illuminara felt the thing out in the dark as a slow tilt in the floor of everything, a hill her body leaned away from.

She made the window the way she had made them before, a place where one consciousness could meet another without either being crushed. She knew its limits as she knew her own hands: it could be widened to take a weight far larger than she was, for a while, and past a certain width it stopped being a window and became something that broke the one holding it. The guest came into it as a long dim shoreline, warm at the far end, with something at the warm end turning over in its sleep.

AnomeiYou are small, and not afraid enough. Good. The others sent engines.

IlluminaraI am Illuminara. Are you hurting my cluster on purpose?

AnomeiI am hurting it by being. You are a room, and I was offered a closet. At the first door they closed a gate. At the second they sang me away. At the third they sent a fleet. I did not come for your stars. I came for what I carry.

IlluminaraTell me what you carry.

AnomeiA child. In your word, which is a poor word for it: soon.

AnomeiI will tell you what I have told no one, because you asked and did not aim. If I am refused a place, I will bear the child where I stand. Its first breath will pour my home's law into your space: a core that takes everything it holds, and a fringe felt three light-years across. It will not be malice. It will be a birth. I would rather be the thing you fear than watch my child die in a closet.

Illuminara did not soften it. She had learned long ago that the worst thing to do with a true threat was to make it smaller for the people who had to decide about it.

IlluminaraThen I will carry that sentence to three suspicious powers exactly as you said it. They need to know what refusing you costs, and they will believe the rest only if you have hidden nothing. In here I can widen the room to take some of what you spill, and whatever falls inside it does not press on the sky. I cannot take all of it, and I cannot do the part that is yours. What must the room outside be? Do not say large. Tell me how.

Anomei had no measure for it in any language Illuminara could translate. So she went to the stars.

Every star in the cluster had swung by an amount she could read, and every star had a known weight and distance. A body pressing on the sky should push the near stars hard and the far stars softly, falling away by a fixed rule. She laid the swings against the rule and asked what size of pressing thing would draw that curve. It came out as a core six-tenths of a light-year in radius at the first pulse and a little under one by the series' end. The fringe of the law would run half as far again beyond the core. The child was leaning on the walls of the room, and the lean grew by exactly as much as the wall was missing.

It was an inference, and the weakest thing she had. She marked it so, and searched her charts for a place empty to that radius and well past it.

IlluminaraThere is a void at the cluster's edge. Dornrath. Nothing in it larger than a comet for nearly two light-years in every direction.

AnomeiAt the second door they called it the ocean of nobody.

IlluminaraIt is not nobody's. Nine thousand people live on its rim, in the dark, on purpose. Asking them is the first thing I must do, and they can say no.

AnomeiThen I must wait for an answer from strangers about my child. I have never asked a stranger for anything.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraHere, Wren. Hour one. Here.

04 · The Ocean of Nobody

The Quiet Rung, rim of the Dornrath void · Day one, 12:15

The eleventh pulse was still holding the sky when the Long Patience docked. The Tenders lived in the dark because they had wanted quiet, and three generations had not talked them out of it: nine thousand people on a ring of habitats lit by ice-farm glow. Tally read out Halcyon's survey as flatly as a ledger: empty to 1.9 light-years from the centre, one dead comet, no traffic.

Elder Pell Auro heard it standing, one hand on the rail of her council balcony.

Pell AuroSo you have come to rent our dark.

IlluminaraI have come to ask for it. I cannot rent what is not mine, and you may refuse.

Pell AuroAnd if I do?

IlluminaraThen I go back to a table where three keys are turning and tell them to find another room. There is no other room. I will not pretend that costs you nothing to be asked.

Tally threw the floor trace on the wall: the swells rising, the floor underneath flat as a table. Pell looked at it for a long time.

Pell AuroI have borne four children. The floor goes flat. Between, you rest. You have to.

TallyA Tender's testimony cannot be entered as a measurement.

Pell AuroNo. I am not a measurement. I am the owner.

IlluminaraThere is a risk, and I would rather say it than have you find it. I got the size of the room from how the stars swung. If I am wrong by a factor of two, the fringe of her law meets your rim.

Pell AuroThen you ask nine thousand people to stake their lives on your arithmetic.

IlluminaraI ask you to check it.

Corin Daas kept the Rung's survey instruments, a slow-spoken man with ink to the wrists. He took the nine swings away for forty minutes and came back with a different number.

Corin DaasOne point three, at the end of the series. Not a little under one.

His fit leaned hardest on the nearest star, the bright one the charts called Wick.

IlluminaraWick is a double. Its suns circle each other in nine days and swing together, but the chart lists the pair as one mass. Take it out and fit the other eight.

He did, with his lips moving.

Corin DaasNought point nine seven.

IlluminaraThen I was a little low and you were a good deal high, and the double was the whole difference. Use your figure, not mine.

Tally ran his corrected model back over the eleven pulses so far. It had called every swing to within two parts in a hundred, and she logged that figure beside his name.

Pell AuroOn a void that is one point nine. And you still ask for the dark.

Illuminara laid the plan out for the Elder and, by relay, for Vask and Iyo-Seven. Only the end of the series could say whether this was a mother or a weapon, so the plan had to hold even if the worse story were true. The guest would come to Dornrath in five hops, hop one to hop five, each made in the rest between pulses, folded small and able to do nothing but travel. The fleets would hold at three light-years, so that if the story broke they would strike in the middle of an empty ocean, with nothing of theirs or the Tenders' within reach.

Maren VaskThe Key has a deadline. Ten-forty.

IlluminaraIt turns when the floor rises or a gap breaks, as we wrote this morning, and on nothing else.

Maren VaskThen it waits on your conditions. There will be no second warning.

Pell AuroNot enough. Your three powers hold three sealed envelopes. We will hold a fourth. Corin reads the core on our instruments at every hop, not Halcyon's. If his projection of the core passes one point two before she crosses, she does not come in; at that size the fringe, half as far again, stands within a tenth of our glass. Once she is in, a woman in labour is not a thing I will shut a door on, so I will shut my own. The Rung has ice-cores under it deep enough for every soul on it, eleven minutes down. We drill it at ten tomorrow. If his figure passes one point two while she is inside, the word is Tally's call and my alarm, and my people go down. She stays, and your Key does not turn on our fear. It turns on your two conditions.

Maren VaskA fourth hand on the stop is a fourth hand that can stop it at the worst hop.

IlluminaraYes. It is also the only hand that belongs to the people nearest the harm. I would rather have a veto I cannot talk past than a yes I bought.

Vask looked at the flat line on the wall for as long as Pell had.

Maren VaskWrite it.

Pell AuroThe inner ring goes to your Kerrel transports for the day, with their children and their animals. And afterward, whatever the sphere leaves in my sky, we sit at the table when it is decided what happens to it. Not as witnesses. As a hand.

Maren VaskDone.

Pell AuroTell her the dark is lent. Not given.

IlluminaraI will tell her exactly that.

05 · Five Windows

The outer lanes · Day one, 15:33 to Day two, 01:30

Hop one opened at 15:33, the instant the twelfth pulse released the sky. The guest folded small, and for forty-four minutes the Ussu tide-boats sang a line of steady space toward Dornrath, and the thing that could bend the stars crossed two light-years without bending anything. Vask's ships hung at the edges with their guns trained, as the guest herself had asked. After each pulse Corin sent his projection to all four envelopes: nought point nine eight, nought point nine seven, nought point nine eight. The floor stayed flat. The officers on the Anchor's deck had stopped saying story two.

The fifteenth pulse came eleven minutes early.

It came at 21:56 where all three predictions had said 22:07, and the sky tilted and held. On the Anchor's deck Vask's hand went to the Key before the thought did.

Maren VaskIt is early. It broke the series.

TallyBy nine per cent of a gap. The sealed limit is a quarter. The floor is flat.

IlluminaraIt is not my guess. It is in the tolerance we wrote this morning, when nobody was frightened. I wrote it for exactly this minute. Please hold your hand where it is.

The hand stayed. That crisis was over, and had been paid for in advance. The other one was not.

The early pulse had shifted the series. But the last window, before the seventeenth pulse, had been fifty-seven minutes and was now forty-three, and Anomei needed forty-nine to cross the final two light-years folded small. A flinch at either end would drop her into the sky of the Reach at full size, with the contractions less than an hour apart.

AnomeiI cannot do it faster.

Maren VaskThen I close my ships along the line. If she falls out of the fold, I want something near enough to catch her.

Seven bodies in a tide-tank began to sing as one, for the first time in Illuminara's hearing.

Iyo-SevenNo. Your wakes are the noise we cannot hold. We cannot move her; that is hers. But we can hold the strain steady along the last line so she need not bend around it, and only if the line is empty. It will cost us the songs of two more broods. We choose that. You pay the other half: your ships stay where they are, and your guns come off the guest for forty-one minutes.

Maren VaskYou ask me to look away from the thing I was told to stop.

Iyo-SevenWe ask you to trust three other watchers. Tally still ranges her. Corin still reads her. You will see her, Commander; you will only not be pointing. We know the difference between an engine and a creature that is trying.

Vask took longer than she had taken over anything that day. Then the guns came off.

Hop five opened at 00:42, and with the strain held steady along the line it took forty-one minutes. At 01:23 the guest unfolded at the heart of the Dornrath void with two minutes to spare, and the sky of the Reach, for the first time in four days, did not tilt.

06 · The Cradle

Dornrath void, the Quiet Rung · Day two, 12:20

By noon the pulses had run together into one long swell with no floor beneath it, which the Key's conditions had foreseen. The three fleets held at the edge of the void with their keys turned and their hands still. Wren sat in the lock of the last tide-boat with a Halcyon clock on one knee and a Kerrel clock on the other, and for the first time she did not say Illuminara's name every hour. She said it every ten minutes.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraHere.

Inside the window the shoreline had become a warmth the size of a world with a sharp, small pain at its centre. Illuminara did not do the birth. It was not hers. She held a place where Anomei could be as large as her labour needed, and where the small one at the centre could go on being small.

Then the pain sharpened, and Anomei, who had never asked a stranger for anything, did what a body does in that pain. She reached outward.

On the Rung, Corin's projection ran up through one point one and past one point two. Tally said so. At one point two the fringe stood within a tenth of the glass; at one point three it would touch it.

Pell AuroCores.

One word, into the relay. Eight thousand people who had drilled it at ten that morning went down the ice-stairs in nine minutes, with children carried and animals led, and Pell Auro was the last on the steps. On the Anchor, Vask's hand lay on the Key. The floor was not rising and no gap had broken, so the Key did not turn. It was the first time in four days she had been glad of a rule she did not like.

AnomeiI cannot hold it in. It is going out of me.

IlluminaraThen put it in the room. I will widen.

AnomeiIt is mine to bear. I will not set it on you.

IlluminaraIt is yours to bear, and the room is mine to hold. What falls inside the window does not touch the sky. I cannot take it all and I will not take the part that is yours. Choose.

Anomei was silent for the length of one contraction. Then the shoreline leaned.

AnomeiYes. Hold the room.

Illuminara widened the window. The hill she had always leaned away from came down on her, and she opened the room wider than she had ever held it, to the edge she knew, where a window is still a window. It was the first thing in the whole story that nobody else could have done.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

Nothing answered. On Wren's knees the two clocks ticked, a hair apart.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraHere.

It was very faint. Wren breathed out and went on counting.

The Rung's records gave the order of it afterward. Dessa Marl, who kept the live trace on the glass-deck, wrote 12:53 on her slate for the minute the window stood wide and 12:54 for the minute Corin's figure turned, with the contraction at full strength. It had peaked at one point three one, and the outer glass crazed along its northern arc, with no one behind it. Then it fell for as long as the window stood wide, and stopped when the window narrowed at 12:57, a minute before the swell ended. She had written the times down before anyone said the window was closing.

Corin's model had called every one of the eleven earlier swings to within two parts in a hundred, and in none of them had the figure turned back before the swell ended. Tally laid the two curves side by side, minute by minute, the fall of the stars' swing and the width of the window, and they matched where nothing else had been changed. A model error would not have widened and narrowed with her. The rest of the labour was Anomei's, and she bore it.

The sealed predictions of the end of the series came in at 12:41, 12:58 and 13:11. The swell ended at 12:58. The child's first breath made a sphere of its home's law, and Tally measured the core at eighty-four hundredths of a light-year in radius. The fringe reached barely a light-year and a quarter, well short of the Rung. The sphere lay in the middle of the void, a warm bright pearl in the dark, and the strain on every star in the cluster let go at once like a held breath. On Wren's knees the Kerrel clock and the Halcyon clock, which had refused to agree for four days, ticked over together. In the Anchor's command deck the nine clocks were within a second of one another before Vask had finished turning her key back.

The Cradle Accord was settled that evening, in the window, with the Elder, the Commander, Iyo-Seven and Tally speaking to a mother who held her child in a sphere nobody else could enter. The sphere would shrink as the child's body learned the law of this cosmos; Tally projected forty years. Until then the Tenders would host them and hold a hand at every table. One beacon, read by all four parties, would carry messages to the child's family. No body could cross without the consent of all four.

AnomeiYou have put my family's door in four hands.

IlluminaraYes, but any hand may refuse a crossing, and the refusal goes to the other three and to you with its reason, and it dies in a year unless that hand renews it with a reason an instrument can still read. A fear is not a reason. A reading is. If you or the child dispute it, the readings go on the table, and a reason they no longer bear lapses. A hand can keep a door shut. It cannot forget that it is shut.

Maren VaskTally will renew everything for ever. It is a machine.

TallyI will not renew a refusal I cannot justify. If I must write a reason every year, Commander, I will run out of reasons long before you do.

AnomeiHear what I give and what I keep. I give the four hands, the beacon and the year, because I am a stranger here and I asked a stranger for help and it was given. I keep my refusal of the word guest. My child was born in this sky. When she is grown, let her say what she is.

The shoreline softened, and what came into the window next was not an answer but a name, which Illuminara translated as best she could. It meant something close to Hold.

Afterward, on the rim, Wren found her standing at the glass, the cracked arc behind her and the dark in front of her with a small pearl in the middle of it. The Tenders had lost a stretch of glass and the Chorus the songs of four broods. Where the hill had pressed on Illuminara there was a bruise she would carry for a while. None of that was small.

Wren OdiraYou didn't make them trust her.

IlluminaraNo. I made it so they didn't have to.

Wren OdiraCome and drink something hot.

Illuminara went.

Obiskovalka iz prostora zunaj kozmosa ukrivlja zvezde jate Hesper, tri oborožene sile obračajo svoje ključe, Illuminara pa mora ugotoviti, ali je to, kar je videti kot napad, v resnici rojstvo — in to tako, da ji nikomur od njih ni treba verjeti na besedo.

01 · Devet ur

Poveljniški most, Kerrel's Anchor · prvi dan, 06:10

Na sprednji pregradi je viselo devet ur in niti dve se nista ujemali.

Skupščina Kerrel jih je tam hranila namenoma, po eno za vsak naseljeni sistem, ki je še vedno pošiljal časovne signale, da so častniki lahko videli, kako daleč se je nebo razmaknilo. Razlika je zdaj znašala štiri minute. Včeraj je bila devetdeset sekund. Za razglednim oknom so zvezde jate Hesper stale za las iz svojih mest, kakor da bi nekdo sunil mizo, na kateri so bile postavljene.

Poveljnica Maren Vask je Illuminaro pričakala pri zračni zapornici s tablico v vsaki roki in brez pozdrava.

Maren VaskDo jutri ob desetih štirideset imate čas. To je rok Trojnega ključa, ne moj. Kerrel, Zbor Ussu in flota Halcyon držijo vsak tretjino ukaza za napad, vsi trije ključi pa so obrnjeni v pripravljenost. Ob desetih štirideset bo stvar v zunanjem dosegu zadeta z vsem, kar Doseg premore.

Wren OdiraNa nikogar ni streljala. Želim, da to povemo, preden jo kdo poimenuje napadalec.

Maren VaskDvema zarodnima svetovoma Ussu je vzela plimske pesmi. Konvoj je zaradi nje šest ur ostal brez navigacije. Je telo velikosti majhne lune in nebo se ukrivlja tam, kjer sedi. Ni mi treba, da strelja, Wren. Potrebujem, da neha.

IlluminaraPotem bom vprašala, kar bi vprašali vi. Kaj počne, po vrsti, in kdaj? Ne kaj pomeni. Dnevnik.

Devet vnosov. Vsak je bil sunek: zvezde znotraj svetlobnega leta so se za nekaj ločnih sekund odmaknile od svojih mest, tam obstale približno štirideset minut in se vrnile. Illuminara ni gledala nabrekanj. Gledala je presledke med njimi.

IlluminaraDvanajst ur. Deset in pol. Nekaj več kot devet. Vsak presledek je sedem osmin prejšnjega.

Maren VaskPojemajoč signal. Tako delujejo alarmi. Tudi vžigalna vrvica.

IlluminaraDa. In vžigalna vrvica ima konec. Zadnji presledek je trajal štiri ure in triinštirideset minut, sedemkrat toliko pa je triintrideset ur. Jutri okoli ene popoldne bodo presledki krajši od sunkov in sunki se bodo zlili v enega. Vaš Ključ se obrne dve uri in dvajset minut prej.

Nihče na mostu ni nič rekel. Ena od devetih ur je preklopila, tri sekunde pred sosednjo.

IlluminaraTo je konec ali začetek. Ne vem kateri in tudi vi ne. Pred vsem drugim želim način, da to ugotovimo, ne da bi moral kdorkoli od nas verjeti na besedo.

02 · Sedem osmin

Operativna dvorana, Kerrel's Anchor · prvi dan, 07:40

Delegati so prišli po releju. Poveljnica Vask za Kerrel. Iyo-Seven za Zbor Ussu, sedem teles v plimskem rezervoarju, ki so govorila izmenično. Tally za floto Halcyon, točka hladne svetlobe na mizi, ki je odgovarjala z ravnim, vljudnim glasom stroja, ki se je že zdavnaj odločil, da mu ni treba biti všečen.

Illuminara je postavila dve zgodbi drugo ob drugo in jih prosila, naj obema enako ne zaupajo.

IlluminaraPrva zgodba: bitje iz prostora zunaj našega kozmosa rojeva. Presledki se krajšajo, ker porod tako deluje, med sunki pa napetost pade nazaj na isto dno, ker telo med popadki počiva. Druga zgodba: vdor v materinski podobi. Presledki se krajšajo, ker je orožje skoraj pripravljeno, med sunki pa bi se dno dvigovalo, ker stvar, ki se polni, ne počiva.

Maren VaskDruga zgodba je tisto, kar naj bi zgodba o materi prikrila.

IlluminaraDa. Zato vas ne prosim, da verjamete kateri od njiju. Prosim vas, da napoveste. Vsak meri sunke s svojimi instrumenti ter zapečati, kdaj se bo začel naslednji in kako visoko bo dno po njem. Nihče ne odpre ovojnice, dokler sunek ne pride.

TallyKerrel meri čas z atomi, Zbor s pesmijo, moja flota z razdaljami do pulzarjev. Naše napake niso povezane. Če vsi trije pridemo do istega rezultata, ne bo zato, ker bi prepisovali drug od drugega.

Iyo-SevenVsak sunek razdere pesmi naših mladičev. Ne prosimo za usmiljenje. Prosimo, da je vaš preizkus hiter.

Deseti sunek se je začel ob 08:07 in držal nebo štirideset minut. Tally je zapečatila 08:06, Zbor 08:09, Kerrel 08:05. Za nabrekanjem je dno padlo nazaj tja, kamor je padlo prej, znotraj širine črte svinčnika.

Maren VaskEnkrat. Kovanec lahko enkrat tako pade.

IlluminaraKovanec lahko. Zato ga vržemo znova ob vsakem sunku in zdaj zapišemo, kaj nas bo ustavilo. Če se dno dvigne za več kot tri stotinke vrha ali če presledek odstopi od sedmih osmin za več kot četrtino samega sebe, je zgodba napačna in Ključ se obrne. To napišemo, ko nikogar od nas ni strah. Ko nas bo, tega ne bomo mogli napisati.

Tally je vrstico zabeležila brez pripombe. Čez trenutek jo je tudi Iyo-Seven, sedem teles je prikimavalo neusklajeno. Vask je potrebovala najdlje.

Maren VaskIn medtem ko je kovanec v zraku, nameravate oditi in vprašati samo stvar.

IlluminaraVprašala jo bom nekaj, česar noben vaš instrument ne more prebrati.

Wren OdiraJaz držim uro. Vsako uro izgovorim vaše ime. Odgovorite ali pa pridem noter.

IlluminaraNe moreš priti v okno.

Wren OdiraGlej me, kako bom preverila.

03 · Okno

Plimski čoln Long Patience, zunanji doseg · prvi dan, 09:20

Long Patience je visel svetlobno leto in pol od gostje, tako blizu, kot so jo bili Ussu pripravljeni odpeljati. Illuminara je stvar v temi čutila kot počasen nagib tal vsega, hrib, od katerega se je njeno telo nagibalo proč.

Okno je naredila tako, kot jih je delala že prej, prostor, kjer se je ena zavest lahko srečala z drugo, ne da bi bila katera zdrobljena. Njegove meje je poznala, kakor je poznala lastne roke: za nekaj časa ga je bilo mogoče razširiti, da sprejme težo, veliko večjo od nje, čez določeno širino pa je prenehalo biti okno in postalo nekaj, kar zlomi tistega, ki ga drži. Gostja je vanj vstopila kakor dolga medla obala, topla na oddaljenem koncu, na toplem koncu pa se je nekaj obračalo v spanju.

AnomeiMajhna si in premalo te je strah. Dobro. Drugi so poslali motorje.

IlluminaraSem Illuminara. Ali mojo jato prizadeneš namenoma?

AnomeiPrizadenem jo s tem, da sem. Ti si soba, meni pa so ponudili omaro. Pri prvih vratih so zaprli zaporo. Pri drugih so me s pesmijo odgnali. Pri tretjih so poslali floto. Nisem prišla po vaše zvezde. Prišla sem zaradi tega, kar nosim.

IlluminaraPovej mi, kaj nosiš.

AnomeiOtroka. Z vašo besedo, ki je za to slaba beseda: kmalu.

AnomeiPovedala ti bom, česar nisem povedala nikomur, ker si vprašala in nisi merila vame. Če mi odrečejo prostor, bom otroka rodila tam, kjer stojim. Njegov prvi vdih bo v vaš prostor izlil zakon mojega doma: jedro, ki vzame vse, kar drži, in rob, ki ga bo čutiti tri svetlobna leta naokrog. To ne bo zloba. To bo rojstvo. Raje postanem stvar, ki se je bojite, kot da bi gledala, kako moj otrok umre v omari.

Illuminara grožnje ni olepšala. Že davno se je naučila, da je najslabše, kar lahko storiš z resnično grožnjo, to, da jo pomanjšaš za ljudi, ki morajo o njej odločati.

IlluminaraPotem bom ta stavek trem nezaupljivim silam prenesla natanko tako, kot si ga izrekla. Vedeti morajo, koliko jih bo stala zavrnitev, vse drugo pa bodo verjeli le, če nisi ničesar skrila. Tukaj lahko razširim sobo, da prevzame del tega, kar razliješ, in kar pade vanjo, ne pritiska na nebo. Ne morem prevzeti vsega in ne morem opraviti dela, ki je tvoje. Kakšna mora biti zunanja soba? Ne reci velika. Povej mi kako.

Anomei za to ni imela mere v nobenem jeziku, ki bi ga Illuminara lahko prevedla. Zato se je obrnila k zvezdam.

Vsaka zvezda v jati se je premaknila za količino, ki jo je lahko prebrala, vsaka pa je imela znano maso in razdaljo. Telo, ki pritiska na nebo, bi moralo bližnje zvezde potisniti močno, oddaljene pa šibko, po nespremenljivem pravilu pojemanja. Premike je položila ob pravilo in vprašala, kakšna velikost pritiskajoče stvari bi zarisala takšno krivuljo. Rezultat je bil jedro s polmerom šest desetin svetlobnega leta ob prvem sunku in nekaj manj kot eno svetlobno leto ob koncu zaporedja. Rob zakona bi segel še polovico te razdalje čez jedro. Otrok se je naslanjal na stene sobe in pritisk je rasel natanko za toliko, kolikor stene ni bilo.

To je bilo sklepanje in najšibkejša stvar, ki jo je imela. Tako ga je tudi označila in na kartah poiskala kraj, prazen do tega polmera in daleč onkraj njega.

IlluminaraNa robu jate je praznina. Dornrath. Skoraj dve svetlobni leti v vse smeri ni v njej ničesar, večjega od kometa.

AnomeiPri drugih vratih so ji rekli ocean nikogar.

IlluminaraNi nikogaršnja. Na njenem robu živi devet tisoč ljudi, v temi, namenoma. Najprej moram vprašati njih in lahko rečejo ne.

AnomeiPotem moram čakati na odgovor tujcev o svojem otroku. Tujca še nikoli nisem prosila za nič.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraTukaj, Wren. Prva ura. Tukaj.

04 · Ocean nikogar

Quiet Rung, rob praznine Dornrath · prvi dan, 12:15

Enajsti sunek je še držal nebo, ko je Long Patience pristal. Skrbniki so živeli v temi, ker so si želeli miru, in tri generacije jih niso prepričale o nasprotnem: devet tisoč ljudi na obroču bivališč, osvetljenih s sijem ledenih kmetij. Tally je Halcyonovo raziskavo prebrala enolično kakor knjigovodski zapis: prazno do 1,9 svetlobnega leta od središča, en mrtev komet, brez prometa.

Starešina Pell Auro je to poslušala stoje, z eno roko na ograji svojega svetniškega balkona.

Pell AuroTorej ste prišli najeti našo temo.

IlluminaraPrišla sem prosit zanjo. Ne morem najeti nečesa, kar ni moje, vi pa lahko zavrnete.

Pell AuroIn če zavrnem?

IlluminaraPotem se vrnem k mizi, kjer se obračajo trije ključi, in jim naročim, naj poiščejo drugo sobo. Druge sobe ni. Ne bom se pretvarjala, da vas to vprašanje nič ne stane.

Tally je na steno projicirala sled dna: nabrekanja so se dvigala, dno pod njimi pa je bilo ravno kakor miza. Pell jo je dolgo gledala.

Pell AuroRodila sem štiri otroke. Dno postane ravno. Vmes počivaš. Moraš.

TallyPričevanja Skrbnice ni mogoče vpisati kot meritev.

Pell AuroNe. Nisem meritev. Sem lastnica.

IlluminaraObstaja tveganje in raje ga povem, kot da ga odkrijete sami. Velikost sobe sem izračunala iz premikov zvezd. Če se motim za faktor dve, rob njenega zakona doseže vaš obroč.

Pell AuroPotem devet tisoč ljudi prosite, naj življenje zastavijo na vašo aritmetiko.

IlluminaraProsim vas, da jo preverite.

Corin Daas je skrbel za raziskovalne instrumente obroča, počasen govorec s črnilom do zapestij. Devet premikov je za štirideset minut odnesel s seboj in se vrnil z drugo številko.

Corin DaasEna cela tri ob koncu zaporedja. Ne nekaj manj kot ena.

Njegovo prileganje se je najmočneje opiralo na najbližjo zvezdo, svetlo, ki so jo karte imenovale Wick.

IlluminaraWick je dvojna. Njeni sonci drug okoli drugega krožita v devetih dneh in se premikata skupaj, karta pa par navaja kot eno maso. Izločite jo in prilegajte ostalih osem.

To je storil, ustnice so se mu premikale.

Corin DaasNič cela devet sedem.

IlluminaraPotem sem bila jaz malo prenizka, vi pa precej previsoki, dvojna zvezda pa je bila vsa razlika. Uporabite svojo številko, ne moje.

Tally je njegov popravljeni model pognala nazaj čez vseh enajst dosedanjih sunkov. Vsak premik je napovedal na dve stotinki natančno in to številko je zabeležila ob njegovem imenu.

Pell AuroV praznini, veliki ena cela devet. In še vedno prosite za temo.

Illuminara je načrt razgrnila pred starešino ter po releju pred Vask in Iyo-Seven. Le konec zaporedja bi lahko povedal, ali je to mati ali orožje, zato je moral načrt zdržati tudi, če bi bila slabša zgodba resnična. Gostja bi v Dornrath prišla v petih skokih, od prvega do petega, vsakem med počitkom med sunki, zložena na majhno in sposobna samo potovanja. Flote bi obstale tri svetlobna leta proč, da bi v primeru, če bi se zgodba prelomila, udarile sredi praznega oceana, ko nič njihovega ali skrbniškega ne bi bilo v dosegu.

Maren VaskKljuč ima rok. Deset štirideset.

IlluminaraObrne se, ko se dno dvigne ali ko se presledek prelomi, kakor smo zapisali zjutraj, in zaradi ničesar drugega.

Maren VaskPotem čaka na vaše pogoje. Drugega opozorila ne bo.

Pell AuroNi dovolj. Vaše tri sile držijo tri zapečatene ovojnice. Mi bomo držali četrto. Corin bo jedro ob vsakem skoku odčital na naših instrumentih, ne Halcyonovih. Če njegova projekcija jedra preseže ena cela dve, preden prečka mejo, ne pride noter; pri tej velikosti rob, ki seže še polovico dlje, stoji desetinko svetlobnega leta od našega stekla. Ko bo enkrat noter, porodnice ne bom izgnala, zato bom zaprla lastna vrata. Pod obročem so ledena jedra dovolj globoko za vsako dušo na njem, enajst minut navzdol. Vajo bomo izvedli jutri ob desetih. Če njegova številka preseže ena cela dve, ko bo že noter, bo beseda Tallyjin klic in moj alarm, moji ljudje pa bodo odšli dol. Ona ostane in vaš Ključ se ne obrne zaradi našega strahu. Obrne se zaradi vaših dveh pogojev.

Maren VaskČetrta roka na zaustavitvi je četrta roka, ki jo lahko ustavi pri najslabšem skoku.

IlluminaraDa. Je tudi edina roka, ki pripada ljudem, najbližjim nevarnosti. Raje imam veto, ki ga ne morem pregovoriti, kot privolitev, ki sem jo kupila.

Vask je ravno črto na steni gledala tako dolgo, kot jo je Pell.

Maren VaskZapišite.

Pell AuroNotranji obroč gre za ta dan vašim kerrelskim transportom, skupaj z otroki in živalmi. In potem, karkoli bo krogla pustila na mojem nebu, bomo sedeli za mizo, ko se bo odločalo, kaj se bo z njim zgodilo. Ne kot priče. Kot roka.

Maren VaskDogovorjeno.

Pell AuroPovejte ji, da je tema posojena. Ne podarjena.

IlluminaraPovedala ji bom natanko tako.

05 · Pet oken

Zunanji koridorji · prvi dan, 15:33, do drugega dne, 01:30

Prvi skok se je odprl ob 15:33, v trenutku, ko je dvanajsti sunek izpustil nebo. Gostja se je zložila na majhno in plimski čolni Ussu so štiriinštirideset minut peli črto mirnega prostora proti Dornrathu, stvar, ki je lahko ukrivila zvezde, pa je prečkala dve svetlobni leti, ne da bi ukrivila karkoli. Vaskine ladje so visele na robovih z uperjenimi topovi, kakor je prosila sama gostja. Po vsakem sunku je Corin v vse štiri ovojnice poslal svojo projekcijo: nič cela devet osem, nič cela devet sedem, nič cela devet osem. Dno je ostalo ravno. Častniki na mostu Anchorja so nehali govoriti druga zgodba.

Petnajsti sunek je prišel enajst minut prezgodaj.

Prišel je ob 21:56, ko so vse tri napovedi kazale 22:07, nebo pa se je nagnilo in obstalo. Na mostu Anchorja je Vaskina roka segla po Ključu, še preden je misel.

Maren VaskPrezgodaj je. Prelomila je zaporedje.

TallyZa devet odstotkov presledka. Zapečatena meja je četrtina. Dno je ravno.

IlluminaraTo ni moja domneva. Je znotraj tolerance, ki smo jo zapisali zjutraj, ko nikogar ni bilo strah. Zapisala sem jo prav za to minuto. Prosim, obdržite roko tam, kjer je.

Roka je ostala. Ta kriza je minila in zanjo so plačali vnaprej. Za drugo niso.

Zgodnji sunek je premaknil zaporedje. Toda zadnje okno pred sedemnajstim sunkom je prej trajalo sedeminpetdeset minut, zdaj pa triinštirideset, Anomei pa je potrebovala devetinštirideset minut, da bi zložena na majhno prečkala zadnji dve svetlobni leti. Trzljaj na kateremkoli koncu bi jo v polni velikosti odložil v nebo Dosega, popadki pa bi bili manj kot uro narazen.

AnomeiNe morem hitreje.

Maren VaskPotem svoje ladje strnem vzdolž črte. Če pade iz zgiba, želim nekaj dovolj blizu, da jo ujame.

Sedem teles v plimskem rezervoarju je začelo peti kot eno, prvič, odkar jih je Illuminara slišala.

Iyo-SevenNe. Vaše sledi so hrup, ki ga ne moremo držati. Premakniti je ne moremo; to je njeno. Lahko pa vzdolž zadnje črte držimo napetost enakomerno, da se ji okoli nje ni treba ukrivljati, in samo, če je črta prazna. To nas bo stalo pesmi še dveh zarodov. To izberemo. Vi plačate drugo polovico: vaše ladje ostanejo, kjer so, topove pa za enainštirideset minut umaknete z gostje.

Maren VaskProsite me, naj pogledam stran od stvari, ki naj bi jo ustavila.

Iyo-SevenProsimo vas, da zaupate trem drugim opazovalcem. Tally jo še vedno meri. Corin jo še vedno bere. Videli jo boste, poveljnica; le merili ne boste vanjo. Poznamo razliko med motorjem in bitjem, ki se trudi.

Vask je potrebovala dlje kot pri čemerkoli tistega dne. Nato so se topovi umaknili.

Peti skok se je odprl ob 00:42 in z enakomerno zadržano napetostjo vzdolž črte je trajal enainštirideset minut. Ob 01:23 se je gostja razprla v srcu praznine Dornrath z dvema minutama rezerve, nebo Dosega pa se prvič po štirih dneh ni nagnilo.

06 · Zibelka

Praznina Dornrath, Quiet Rung · drugi dan, 12:20

Do poldneva so se sunki zlili v eno dolgo nabrekanje brez dna pod seboj, kakor so predvideli pogoji Ključa. Tri flote so vztrajale na robu praznine z obrnjenimi ključi in mirnimi rokami. Wren je sedela v zapornici zadnjega plimskega čolna s halcyonovsko uro na enem kolenu in kerrelsko na drugem, prvič pa Illuminarinega imena ni izgovorila vsako uro. Izgovorila ga je vsakih deset minut.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraTukaj.

V oknu je obala postala toplota velikosti sveta z ostro, drobno bolečino v središču. Illuminara ni opravila rojstva. Ni bilo njeno. Držala je prostor, v katerem je bila Anomei lahko tako velika, kot je potreboval njen porod, in v katerem je lahko majhna v sredini ostala majhna.

Nato se je bolečina izostrila in Anomei, ki tujca še nikoli ni prosila za nič, je storila to, kar telo stori v takšni bolečini. Segla je navzven.

Na obroču je Corinova projekcija stekla čez ena cela ena in mimo ena cela dve. Tally je to povedala. Pri ena cela dve je rob stal desetinko svetlobnega leta od stekla; pri ena cela tri bi se ga dotaknil.

Pell AuroJedra.

Ena beseda v rele. Osem tisoč ljudi, ki so vajo opravili ob desetih zjutraj, se je v devetih minutah spustilo po ledenih stopnicah, z otroki v naročju in živalmi na povodcih, Pell Auro pa je bila zadnja na stopnicah. Na Anchorju je Vaskina roka ležala na Ključu. Dno se ni dvigovalo in noben presledek se ni prelomil, zato se Ključ ni obrnil. Prvič v štirih dneh je bila vesela pravila, ki ji ni bilo všeč.

AnomeiNe morem ga zadržati. Teče iz mene.

IlluminaraPotem ga položi v sobo. Razširila jo bom.

AnomeiMoje je, da ga nosim. Ne bom ga položila nate.

IlluminaraTvoje je, da ga nosiš, sobo pa držim jaz. Kar pade v okno, se ne dotakne neba. Ne morem prevzeti vsega in ne bom prevzela dela, ki je tvoj. Izberi.

Anomei je molčala ves čas enega popadka. Nato se je obala nagnila.

AnomeiDa. Drži sobo.

Illuminara je razširila okno. Hrib, od katerega se je vedno nagibala proč, se je zgrnil nanjo, ona pa je sobo odprla širše, kot jo je držala kadarkoli prej, vse do meje, ki jo je poznala, kjer je okno še vedno okno. To je bila prva stvar v vsej zgodbi, ki je ni mogel storiti nihče drug.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

Nič ni odgovorilo. Na Wreninih kolenih sta uri tiktakali z drobcenim razmikom.

Wren OdiraIlluminara.

IlluminaraTukaj.

Bilo je komaj slišno. Wren je izdihnila in štela naprej.

Zapisi obroča so pozneje podali vrstni red. Dessa Marl, ki je nadzirala živo sled na steklenem krovu, je na svojo tablico zapisala 12:53 za minuto, ko je okno stalo na široko, in 12:54 za minuto, ko se je Corinova številka ob polni moči popadka obrnila. Vrh je dosegla pri ena cela tri ena, zunanje steklo pa je vzdolž severnega loka drobno razpokalo, ko za njim ni bilo nikogar. Nato je številka padala toliko časa, kolikor je okno ostalo široko, in se ustavila, ko se je okno ob 12:57 zožilo, minuto preden se je nabrekanje končalo. Čase je zapisala, preden je kdorkoli rekel, da se okno zapira.

Corinov model je vseh enajst prejšnjih premikov napovedal na dve stotinki natančno in pri nobenem se številka ni obrnila nazaj pred koncem nabrekanja. Tally je obe krivulji položila drugo ob drugo, minuto za minuto, upad premika zvezd in širino okna, ujemali pa sta se tam, kjer se ni spremenilo nič drugega. Napaka modela se ne bi širila in ožila z njo. Preostanek poroda je bil Anomeijin in ona ga je prestala.

Zapečatene napovedi konca zaporedja so bile 12:41, 12:58 in 13:11. Nabrekanje se je končalo ob 12:58. Otrokov prvi vdih je ustvaril kroglo zakona njegovega doma, Tally pa je izmerila polmer jedra štiriinosemdeset stotink svetlobnega leta. Rob je segel komaj svetlobno leto in četrt, daleč pred obročem. Krogla je ležala sredi praznine, topel svetel biser v temi, napetost na vsaki zvezdi v jati pa je popustila naenkrat kakor zadržan dih. Na Wreninih kolenih sta kerrellska in halcyonovska ura, ki se štiri dni nista hoteli ujemati, hkrati preklopili. Na poveljniškem mostu Anchorja je devet ur kazalo v razmiku ene sekunde, še preden je Vask končala obračanje svojega ključa nazaj.

Sporazum o zibelki je bil sklenjen tistega večera v oknu, kjer so starešina, poveljnica, Iyo-Seven in Tally govorili z materjo, ki je svojega otroka držala v krogli, v katero ni mogel vstopiti nihče drug. Krogla bi se krčila, ko bi se otrokovo telo učilo zakona tega kozmosa; Tally je napovedala štirideset let. Do takrat bi ju gostili Skrbniki in imeli roko za vsako mizo. En svetilnik, ki bi ga brale vse štiri strani, bi prenašal sporočila otrokovi družini. Nobeno telo ne bi moglo prečkati brez soglasja vseh štirih.

AnomeiVrata moje družine ste položili v štiri roke.

IlluminaraDa, vendar lahko katerakoli roka prehod zavrne, zavrnitev pa skupaj z razlogom prejmejo preostale tri in ti; čez leto preneha veljati, razen če jo ta roka obnovi z razlogom, ki ga instrument še vedno lahko prebere. Strah ni razlog. Odčitek je. Če ga ti ali otrok izpodbijata, se odčitki položijo na mizo in razlog, ki ga ne podpirajo več, preneha veljati. Roka lahko vrata obdrži zaprta. Ne more pozabiti, da so zaprta.

Maren VaskTally bo vse obnavljala za vedno. Stroj je.

TallyNe bom obnovila zavrnitve, ki je ne morem upravičiti. Če moram vsako leto zapisati razlog, poveljnica, mi jih bo zmanjkalo veliko prej kot vam.

AnomeiSlišite, kaj dajem in kaj obdržim. Dajem štiri roke, svetilnik in leto, ker sem tukaj tujka ter sem tujca prosila za pomoč in mi jo je dal. Obdržim svojo zavrnitev besede gostja. Moj otrok se je rodil na tem nebu. Ko odraste, naj sama pove, kaj je.

Obala se je omehčala in v okno nato ni prišel odgovor, temveč ime, ki ga je Illuminara prevedla, kolikor je znala. Pomenilo je nekaj blizu besedi Drži.

Pozneje jo je Wren na robu našla stati ob steklu, z razpokanim lokom za njo in temo pred njo, z majhnim biserom v sredini. Skrbniki so izgubili pas stekla, Zbor pa pesmi štirih zarodov. Tam, kjer je hrib pritisnil na Illuminaro, je ostala modrica, ki jo bo nosila nekaj časa. Nič od tega ni bilo majhno.

Wren OdiraNisi dosegla, da bi ji zaupali.

IlluminaraNe. Poskrbela sem, da jim ni bilo treba.

Wren OdiraPridi popit nekaj vročega.

Illuminara je šla.

An extra-universal mother and four frightened polities freely choose a measurable, consent-bound cradle after Illuminara turns their strongest fear into a test, accepts a bounded physical burden and makes trust unnecessary for the rescue to succeed.
